-- ==================================================
-- [dbo].[ApispInsertCompany]
-- ==================================================





/*RZ980419*/
/*RZ980516- Check CmpCode Is Duplicate*/
/*RZ990520- Add cmpKind*/
/*RZ14010321- Add cmpNameOfficial*/
/*RZ14010519- Add cmpCampaignIds*/
/*RZ14010913- Insert Campaign*/
/*RZ14010919- Add cmpNameOfficial In paremetre*/
/*RZ14011018- Convert Arabic characters to Persian*/
/*RZ14011211- Add CompanyChangeHistory*/
Create PROCEDURE [dbo].[ApispInsertCompany]
/*
	exec ApispInsertCompany 		
*/
(
/*Company*/
@CmpNumber INT = NULL,
@CmpName NVARCHAR(300),
@CmpNameEng NVARCHAR(300) = NULL,
@CmpTitleId INT = NULL,
@CmpFame NVARCHAR(200) = NULL,
@CmpActivityType NVARCHAR(100) = NULL,
@CmpGroupId INT = NULL,
@CmpSubGroupId INT = NULL,
@CmpGroupIdII INT = NULL,
@CmpGroupIdIII INT = NULL,
@CmpGroupIdIV INT = NULL,
@CmpTypeId INT = NULL,
@CmpPresentationId INT = NULL,
@CmpPreTel NVARCHAR(10) =  NULL,
@CmpTel NVARCHAR(15) = NULL,
@CmpTelUpTo NVARCHAR(10) = NULL,
@CmpTelDesc NVARCHAR(50) = NULL,
@CmpPreTelII NVARCHAR(10) = NULL,
@CmpTelII NVARCHAR(15) = NULL,
@CmpTelUpToII NVARCHAR(10) = NULL,
@CmpTelIIDesc NVARCHAR(50) = NULL,
@CmpMobile NVARCHAR(15) = NULL,
@CmpFax NVARCHAR(15) = NULL,
@CmpFaxDesc NVARCHAR(50) = NULL,
@CmpCountryId INT = NULL,
@CmpProvinceId INT = NULL,
@CmpCityId INT = NULL,
@CmpAddress NVARCHAR(250) = NULL,
@CmpAddressDesc NVARCHAR(100) = NULL,
@CmpZipCode NVARCHAR(50) = NULL,
@CmpPostBox NVARCHAR(50) = NULL,
@CmpWebSite NVARCHAR(50) = NULL,
@CmpEmail NVARCHAR(50) = NULL,
@CmpInteresting NVARCHAR(500) = NULL,
@CmpSpecialPoint NVARCHAR(500) = NULL,
@CmpDescription NVARCHAR(500) = NULL,
@CmpUserName NVARCHAR(50) = NULL,
@CmpPassword NVARCHAR(50) = NULL,	
@CmpRegistrationNumber NVARCHAR(50) = NULL,
@CmpEconomicCode NVARCHAR(50) = NULL,
@CmpNationalCode NVARCHAR(50) = NULL,
@CmpBuyLimit NVARCHAR(100) = NULL,
@CmpAccountNumber NVARCHAR(500) = NULL,
@CmpAccInquiry NVARCHAR(500) = NULL,
@CmpFreight NVARCHAR(200) = NULL,
@CmpInquiry NVARCHAR(500) = NULL,
@CmpReferUserId INT = NULL,
@CmpSpecialWordForCI NVARCHAR(300) = NULL,
@CmpCode NVARCHAR(500) = NULL,
@CmpEffectiveDatePersian NVARCHAR(8) = NULL,
@CmpEffectiveDateTime DATETIME = NULL,
@CmpCoordinateLatitude NVARCHAR(100) = NULL,
@CmpCoordinateLongitude NVARCHAR(100) = NULL,
@CmpCoordinateLastDate DATETIME = NULL,
@CmpCoordinateLastDatePersian NVARCHAR(8) = NULL,
@CmpCoordinateZoomLevel INT = NULL,
@CmpCoordinateLastUserId INT = NULL,
@CmpFOAccessLinkedCompany BIT = NULL,
/**/
@UserId INT,
@Month1 SMALLINT,
@RegisterDate DATETIME,
@RegisterDatePersian NVARCHAR(8),
@AppType VARCHAR(20), 
/**/
@InsertedCompanyId INT OUTPUT,
@ErrorMessage NVARCHAR(MAX) OUTPUT,
@PersonLabel NVARCHAR(50),
@CmpCodeNotDup BIT = NULL,/*RZ980516- Check CmpCode Is Duplicate*/
@CmpCampaignIds NVARCHAR(2000) = NULL,/*RZ14010519- Add cmpCampaignIds*/
@CmpCampaignName NVARCHAR(300) = NULL,/*RZ14010913- Insert Campaign*/
@cmpNameOfficial NVARCHAR(300) = NULL/*RZ14010919- Add cmpNameOfficial In paremetre*/
)
AS
BEGIN
/**/
/*-----------------------------------------------------------------------------*/
/*RZ14011018- Convert Arabic characters to Persian*/
SET @CmpName = (SELECT dbo.[fnConvertArabicCharacterToPersian](@CmpName))
SET @CmpAddress = (SELECT dbo.[fnConvertArabicCharacterToPersian](@CmpAddress))
/*RZ14011018- End*/
/*-----------------------------------------------------------------------------*/
/********************************************************************************/
/****************************** TRANSACTION *************************************/
/********************************************************************************/
BEGIN TRANSACTION Main
/**/
/********************************************************************************/
/**********************************Default LanguageID ***************************/
DECLARE @DefaultLanguageID INT 
SET @DefaultLanguageID = (SELECT TOP 1 [DefaultLanguageID] FROM [dbo].[CrmsysSetting])
/********************************************************************************/
/************************************ Year1 => FPId *****************************/
DECLARE @Year1 SMALLINT
SET @Year1 = (SELECT TOP 1 [ID] FROM [dbo].[WSFinancialPeriod] ORDER BY [ID] DESC)
/********************************************************************************/
/************************************ Error *************************************/
DECLARE @ErrorFrom VARCHAR(50), @ErrorNumber INT
/********************************************************************************/
/*********************** Check UserName for company *****************************/
/********************************************************************************/
DECLARE @CompanyIdForCheckUName INT 
SET @CompanyIdForCheckUName = (SELECT TOP 1 C.[CompanyID] FROM [dbo].[CrmCompany] C 
				WHERE C.[cmpUserName] = @CmpUserName)
IF(@CompanyIdForCheckUName IS NOT NULL)
	SET @ErrorMessage = @ErrorMessage + 'DupUName=1|'	

/*RZ980516- Check CmpCode Is Duplicate*/
IF(@CmpCode IS NOT NULL AND @CmpCode <> '' AND ISNULL(@CmpCodeNotDup,0) = 1 )
BEGIN
	DECLARE @CmpCodeFound INT
	SET @CmpCodeFound = (SELECT TOP 1 cmpNumber FROM dbo.CrmCompany C WHERE cmpCode = @cmpCode)
	IF(@CmpCodeFound IS NOT NULL)
		SET @ErrorMessage = @ErrorMessage + 'DupCmpCode=' + CAST(@CmpCodeFound AS NVARCHAR(200)) + '|'
END
/*RZ980516- END*/
/************************ Make A Table For check duplicate ***************************/
DECLARE @TmpTableForCheckDuplicate TABLE (
	[CompanyID] INT,	
	[cmpId] INT,	
	[cmpName] NVARCHAR(MAX),
	[Title] NVARCHAR(MAX),	
	[CompanyWithTitle] NVARCHAR(MAX),	
	[cmpTel] NVARCHAR(50),
	[cmpTelII] NVARCHAR(50),	
	[cmpFax] NVARCHAR(50),
	[Country] NVARCHAR(MAX),
	[Provice] NVARCHAR(MAX),
	[City] NVARCHAR(MAX),	
	[cmpAddress] NVARCHAR(MAX),
	[prsName] NVARCHAR(MAX),
	[prsTel] NVARCHAR(50),
	[prsTelII] NVARCHAR(50),
	[prsFax] NVARCHAR(50),
	[prsMobile] NVARCHAR(50),
	[prsEmail] NVARCHAR(300),
	[prsEmailII] NVARCHAR(300),
	[cmpNumber] INT,	
	[ReferUser] NVARCHAR(MAX)			
)
IF(@CmpName IS NOT NULL AND @CmpName <> '' AND @CmpName <> @PersonLabel AND @CmpName <> N'شخص')
BEGIN
	DECLARE @DupCompanyId INT , @DupCmpTitle NVARCHAR(MAX), @DupCmpName NVARCHAR(MAX), @DupCmpCity NVARCHAR(MAX), @DupCmpNumber INT
	, @TitleId INT , @CityId INT
	SELECT @TitleId = CASE WHEN @CmpTitleId IS NULL THEN 0 ELSE @CmpTitleId END
	SELECT @CityId = CASE WHEN @CmpCityId IS NULL THEN 0 ELSE @CmpCityId END
	/**/
	INSERT INTO @TmpTableForCheckDuplicate 
	EXEC [dbo].[ApispCheckDuplicateCompany]
			@Type  = 1, 
			@Id = 0, 
			@Val = @CmpName, 
			@TitleID = @TitleId, 
			@CityId = @CityId, 
			@UserID  = @UserId,
			@LackOfShowText = null,
			@LanguageID  = @DefaultLanguageID,
			@DefaultLanguageID = @DefaultLanguageID

	SELECT TOP 1
		@DupCompanyId = [CompanyID],
		@DupCmpTitle = [Title], 
		@DupCmpName = [cmpName], 
		@DupCmpCity = [City], 
		@DupCmpNumber = [cmpNumber]
	FROM @TmpTableForCheckDuplicate	 
		
	IF(@DupCompanyId IS NOT NULL)
	BEGIN
		SET @ErrorMessage = @ErrorMessage + 'DupCmp=' + CAST(@DupCmpNumber AS NVARCHAR(200))	+ '|'		
	END			
END
/********************************************************************************/
/********************************* Check fields *********************************/
/********************************************************************************/
/*CmpTitleId*/
IF(@CmpTitleId IS NOT NULL AND @CmpTitleId > 0)
BEGIN	
	IF((SELECT TOP 1 [CompanyTitleID] FROM [dbo].[CrmCompanyTitle] WHERE [CompanyTitleID] = @CmpTitleId) IS NULL)
		SET @ErrorMessage = @ErrorMessage + 'No CmpTitleId|'
END
/*CmpGroupId*/
IF(@CmpGroupId IS NOT NULL AND @CmpGroupId > 0)
BEGIN
	IF((SELECT TOP 1 [CompanyGroupID] FROM [dbo].[CrmCompanyGroups] WHERE [CompanyGroupID] = @CmpGroupId) IS NULL)
		SET @ErrorMessage = @ErrorMessage + 'No CmpGroupId|'
END
/*CmpSubGroupId*/
IF(@CmpSubGroupId IS NOT NULL AND @CmpSubGroupId > 0)
BEGIN	 
	IF((SELECT TOP 1 [CompanySubGroupID] FROM [dbo].[CrmCompanySubGroups] WHERE [CompanySubGroupID] = @CmpSubGroupId) IS NULL)
		SET @ErrorMessage = @ErrorMessage + 'No CmpSubGroupId|'
	ELSE IF (@CmpGroupId IS NOT NULL AND @CmpGroupId > 0)
	BEGIN
		IF((SELECT TOP 1 [CompanySubGroupID] FROM [dbo].[CrmCompanySubGroups] 
			WHERE [CompanySubGroupID] = @CmpSubGroupId AND [GroupId] = @CmpGroupId) IS NULL)
			SET @ErrorMessage = @ErrorMessage + 'No CmpSubGroupId|'
	END
END
/*CmpGroupIdII*/
IF(@CmpGroupIdII IS NOT NULL AND @CmpGroupIdII > 0)
BEGIN
	IF((SELECT TOP 1 [CompanyGroupIIID] FROM [dbo].[CrmCompanyGroupsII] WHERE [CompanyGroupIIID] = @CmpGroupIdII) IS NULL)
		SET @ErrorMessage = @ErrorMessage + 'No CmpGroupIdII|'	
END
/*CmpGroupIdIII*/
IF(@CmpGroupIdIII IS NOT NULL AND @CmpGroupIdIII > 0)
BEGIN
	IF((SELECT TOP 1 [CompanyGroupIIIID] FROM [dbo].[CrmCompanyGroupsIII] WHERE [CompanyGroupIIIID] = @CmpGroupIdIII) IS NULL)
		SET @ErrorMessage = @ErrorMessage + 'No CmpGroupIdIII|'	
END
/*CmpGroupIdIV*/
IF(@CmpGroupIdIV IS NOT NULL AND @CmpGroupIdIV > 0)
BEGIN
	IF((SELECT TOP 1 [CompanyGroupIVID] FROM [dbo].[CrmCompanyGroupsIV] WHERE [CompanyGroupIVID] = @CmpGroupIdIV) IS NULL)
		SET @ErrorMessage = @ErrorMessage + 'No CmpGroupIdIV|'	
END
/*CmpTypeId*/
IF(@CmpTypeId IS NOT NULL AND @CmpTypeId > 0)
BEGIN
	IF((SELECT TOP 1 [CompanyTypeID] FROM [dbo].[CrmCompanyType] WHERE [CompanyTypeID] = @CmpTypeId) IS NULL)
		SET @ErrorMessage = @ErrorMessage + 'No CmpTypeId|'	
END
/*CmpPresentationId*/
IF(@CmpPresentationId IS NOT NULL AND @CmpPresentationId > 0)
BEGIN
	IF((SELECT TOP 1 [CompanyPresentationID] FROM [dbo].[CrmCompanyPresentation] 
		WHERE [CompanyPresentationID] = @CmpPresentationId) IS NULL)
		SET @ErrorMessage = @ErrorMessage + 'No CmpPresentationId|'	
END
/*CmpCountryId*/
IF(@CmpCountryId IS NOT NULL AND @CmpCountryId > 0)
BEGIN
	IF((SELECT TOP 1 [CountryId] FROM [dbo].[Country] WHERE [CountryId] = @CmpCountryId) IS NULL)
		SET @ErrorMessage = @ErrorMessage + 'No CmpCountryId|'	
END
/*CmpProvinceId*/
IF(@CmpProvinceId IS NOT NULL AND @CmpProvinceId > 0)
BEGIN
	IF((SELECT TOP 1 [ProvinceID] FROM [dbo].[Province] WHERE [ProvinceID] = @CmpProvinceId) IS NULL)
		SET @ErrorMessage = @ErrorMessage + 'No CmpProvinceId|'	
	ELSE IF(@CmpCountryId IS NOT NULL AND @CmpCountryId > 0)
	BEGIN
		IF((SELECT TOP 1 [ProvinceID] FROM [dbo].[Province] 
			WHERE [ProvinceID] = @CmpProvinceId AND [CountryId]= @CmpCountryId) IS NULL)
		SET @ErrorMessage = @ErrorMessage + 'No CmpProvinceId|'	
	END
END
/*CmpCityId*/
IF(@CmpCityId IS NOT NULL AND @CmpCityId > 0)
BEGIN
	IF((SELECT TOP 1 [CityID] FROM [dbo].[City] WHERE [CityID] = @CmpCityId) IS NULL)
		SET @ErrorMessage = @ErrorMessage + 'No CmpCityId|'	
	ELSE IF(@CmpProvinceId IS NOT NULL AND @CmpProvinceId > 0)
	BEGIN
		IF((SELECT TOP 1 [CityID] FROM [dbo].[City] 
			WHERE [CityID] = @CmpCityId AND [ProvinceId]= @CmpProvinceId) IS NULL)
		SET @ErrorMessage = @ErrorMessage + 'No CmpCityId|'	
	END
END
/*CmpCoordinateLastUserId*/
IF(@CmpCoordinateLastUserId IS NOT NULL AND @CmpCoordinateLastUserId > 0)
BEGIN
	IF((SELECT TOP 1 [UserId] FROM [dbo].[Users] WHERE [UserId] = @CmpCoordinateLastUserId) IS NULL)
		SET @ErrorMessage = @ErrorMessage + 'No CmpCoordinateLastUserId|'
END
/*CmpReferUserId*/
IF(@CmpReferUserId IS NOT NULL AND @CmpReferUserId > 0)
BEGIN
	IF((SELECT TOP 1 [UserId] FROM [dbo].[Users] WHERE [UserId] = @CmpReferUserId) IS NULL)
		SET @ErrorMessage = @ErrorMessage + 'No CmpReferUserId|'
END
ELSE
	SET @CmpReferUserId = @UserId

/********************************************************************************/
/********************************************************************************/
/********************************* Campaign *************************************/
/*RZ14010913- Insert Campaign*/
DECLARE @CompanyCampaignId INT = 0,@CampaignOrderView INT,@CampaignCount INT
IF(ISNULL(@CmpCampaignName,'') <> '')
BEGIN
/**/
	SET @CompanyCampaignId = (SELECT TOP (1) [CompanyCampaignId]    
							  FROM [dbo].[CrmCompanyCampaign]
							  WHERE (REPLACE(REPLACE(REPLACE([CampaignName] ,NCHAR(1740), NCHAR(1610)),NCHAR(1603),NCHAR(1705)),NCHAR(1609),NCHAR(1740)))
									= 
									(REPLACE(REPLACE(REPLACE(@CmpCampaignName ,NCHAR(1740), NCHAR(1610)),NCHAR(1603),NCHAR(1705)),NCHAR(1609),NCHAR(1740)))
							  )
	IF(ISNULL(@CompanyCampaignId,0) = 0)
	BEGIN
	/**/
		SET @CampaignCount = (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM [dbo].[CrmCompanyCampaign])
		SET @CampaignOrderView = (@CampaignCount + 1) + (@CampaignCount * 9 ) - 1		

		INSERT INTO [dbo].[CrmCompanyCampaign]
			([ParentId]
			,[ParentPath]
			,[CampaignName]
			,[CampaignDepth]
			,[CampaignIsLeaf]
			,[OrderView]
			,[FirstRegisterUserId]
			,[FirstRegisterDate]
			,[FirstRegisterDatePersian]
			,[UserId]
			,[Year1]
			,[Month1]
			,[RegisterDate]
			,[RegisterDatePersian]			
			,[AppType]
			,[LanguageID])
		VALUES
			(0
			,'0'
			,@CmpCampaignName
			,0
			,1
			,@CampaignOrderView
			,@UserId
			,@RegisterDate
			,@RegisterDatePersian
			,@UserId
			,@Year1
			,@Month1
			,@RegisterDate
			,@RegisterDatePersian			
			,@AppType
			,@DefaultLanguageID)

		SET @CompanyCampaignId = SCOPE_IDENTITY()
		/*********** Insert to lang table ***********/
		IF(ISNULL(@CompanyCampaignId,0) <> 0)
		BEGIN
		/**/
			INSERT INTO [dbo].[CrmCompanyCampaign_Lang]
				([CompanyCampaignId]
				,[LanguageID]
				,[CampaignName])
			SELECT
				 @CompanyCampaignId
				 ,LN.LanguageID
				 ,@CmpCampaignName
			FROM dbo.Languages LN 
			WHERE LN.LanguageID <> @DefaultLanguageID	
			AND ISNULL(LN.IsActive,0) = 1
			/*===================== NeedUpdateUsersMobBaseInfo ===================*/
			EXEC [dbo].[ApispNeedUpdateUsersMobBaseInfo]
			@UpdateDetail = N'CrmCompanyCampaign',
			@UserId = NULL,
			@RollId = NULL
			/*-----------------------------------------------*/
		/**/
		END
	/**/
	END	
	/*----------------------*/
	IF(ISNULL(@CompanyCampaignId,0) <> 0)
	BEGIN
	/**/
		IF(ISNULL(@CmpCampaignIds,'') <> '')
				SET @CmpCampaignIds = ISNULL(@CmpCampaignIds,'')  + ','
		SET @CmpCampaignIds = ISNULL(@CmpCampaignIds,'')  + CAST(@CompanyCampaignId AS NVARCHAR(50))
	/**/
	END	
/**/
END
/*RZ14010913- End*/
/********************************************************************************/
/*===============================================================================*/
IF(@ErrorMessage IS NULL OR @ErrorMessage = '')
BEGIN
	/********************************************************************************/
	/****************************** Insert Company **********************************/
	/********************************************************************************/
	BEGIN TRAN
	/**/
	/** Get company number and use in cmpNumber And cmpUsername**/	
	SET @cmpNumber = (SELECT [dbo].[ApifnReturnCompanyNumber]())
	/********************************************/
	/*==================================*/
	/*RZ990520- Add cmpKind*/
	DECLARE @cmpKind INT = 0
	IF((REPLACE(REPLACE(REPLACE(CAST(@cmpName AS NVARCHAR(2000)) , NCHAR(1740), NCHAR(1610)),NCHAR(1603),NCHAR(1705)),NCHAR(1609),NCHAR(1740)) 
	 = REPLACE(REPLACE(REPLACE(CAST(N'شخص' AS NVARCHAR(2000)), NCHAR(1740), NCHAR(1610)),NCHAR(1603),NCHAR(1705)),NCHAR(1609),NCHAR(1740)) ))
	 BEGIN
		SET @cmpKind = 1
	 END
	/*RZ990520- End*/
	/*RZ14010321- Add cmpNameOfficial*/
	DECLARE /*@cmpNameOfficial NVARCHAR(300) = NULL,*/@cmpIsDraft BIT = NULL
	/*SET @cmpNameOfficial = @CmpName*/
	/*RZ14010919- Add cmpNameOfficial In paremetre*/
	IF(ISNULL(@cmpNameOfficial,'') <> '')
	BEGIN
	/**/
		SET @cmpNameOfficial = @CmpName
	/**/
	END
	/**/
	/*RZ14010321- End*/
	/*==================================*/
	INSERT INTO [dbo].[CrmCompany]
           ([ImportDate]
           ,[ImportDateTime]
           ,[cmpNumber]
           ,[cmpName]
           ,[cmpNameEng]
           ,[cmpTitleId]
           ,[cmpFame]
           ,[cmpActivityType]
           ,[cmpGroupId]
           ,[cmpSubGroupId]
           ,[cmpGroupIdII]
           ,[cmpGroupIdIII]
           ,[cmpGroupIdIV]
           ,[cmpTypeId]
           ,[cmpPresentationId]
           ,[cmpPreTel]
           ,[cmpTel]
           ,[cmpTelUpTo]
           ,[cmpTelDesc]
           ,[cmpPreTelII]
           ,[cmpTelII]
           ,[cmpTelUpToII]
           ,[cmpTelIIDesc]
           ,[cmpMobile]
           ,[cmpFax]
           ,[cmpFaxDesc]
           ,[cmpCountryId]
           ,[cmpProvinceId]
           ,[cmpCityId]
           ,[cmpAddress]
           ,[cmpAddressDesc]
           ,[cmpZipCode]
           ,[cmpPostBox]
           ,[cmpWebSite]
           ,[cmpEmail]
           ,[cmpInteresting]
           ,[cmpSpecialPoint]
           ,[cmpDescription]
           ,[cmpUserName]
           ,[cmpPassword]
           ,[cmpFirstRegisterUserId]
           ,[UserId]
           ,[Year1]
           ,[Month1]
           ,[RegisterDate]
           ,[RegisterDatePersian]
           ,[cmpRegistrationNumber]
           ,[cmpEconomicCode]
           ,[cmpNationalCode]
           ,[cmpBuyLimit]
           ,[cmpAccountNumber]
           ,[cmpAccInquiry]
           ,[cmpFreight]
           ,[cmpInquiry]
           ,[cmpReferUserId]
           ,[AppType]
           ,[cmpSpecialWordForCI]
           ,[cmpCode]
           ,[cmpEffectiveDatePersian]
           ,[cmpEffectiveDateTime]
           ,[LanguageID]
           ,[FirstRegisterUserId]
           ,[FirstRegisterDatePersian]
           ,[FirstRegisterDate]
           ,[cmpCoordinateLatitude]
           ,[cmpCoordinateLongitude]
           ,[cmpCoordinateLastDate]
           ,[cmpCoordinateLastDatePersian]
           ,[cmpCoordinateZoomLevel]
           ,[cmpCoordinateLastUserId]
		   ,[cmpFOAccessLinkedCompany]
		   ,[cmpKind]/*RZ990520- Add cmpKind*/
		   /*RZ14010321- Add cmpNameOfficial*/
		   ,[cmpIsDraft]
		   ,[cmpNameOfficial]
		   /*RZ14010321- End*/
		   ,[cmpCampaignIds]/*RZ14010519- Add cmpCampaignIds*/)
     VALUES(                          
            @RegisterDatePersian
           ,@RegisterDate
           ,@CmpNumber
           ,@CmpName
           ,@CmpNameEng
           ,@CmpTitleId
           ,@CmpFame
           ,@CmpActivityType
           ,@CmpGroupId
           ,@CmpSubGroupId
           ,@CmpGroupIdII
           ,@CmpGroupIdIII
           ,@CmpGroupIdIV
           ,@CmpTypeId
           ,@CmpPresentationId
           ,@CmpPreTel
           ,@CmpTel
           ,@CmpTelUpTo
           ,@CmpTelDesc
           ,@CmpPreTelII
           ,@CmpTelII
           ,@CmpTelUpToII
           ,@CmpTelIIDesc
           ,@CmpMobile
           ,@CmpFax
           ,@CmpFaxDesc
           ,@CmpCountryId
           ,@CmpProvinceId
           ,@CmpCityId
           ,@CmpAddress
           ,@CmpAddressDesc
           ,@CmpZipCode
           ,@CmpPostBox
           ,@CmpWebSite
           ,@CmpEmail
           ,@CmpInteresting
           ,@CmpSpecialPoint
           ,@CmpDescription
           ,@CmpUserName
           ,@CmpPassword
           ,@UserId
           ,@UserId
           ,@Year1
           ,@Month1
           ,@RegisterDate
           ,@RegisterDatePersian
           ,@CmpRegistrationNumber
           ,@CmpEconomicCode
           ,@CmpNationalCode
           ,@CmpBuyLimit
           ,@CmpAccountNumber
           ,@CmpAccInquiry
           ,@CmpFreight
           ,@CmpInquiry
           ,@CmpReferUserId
           ,@AppType
           ,@CmpSpecialWordForCI
           ,@CmpCode
           ,@CmpEffectiveDatePersian
           ,@CmpEffectiveDateTime
           ,@DefaultLanguageID
           ,@UserId
           ,@RegisterDatePersian
           ,@RegisterDate
           ,@CmpCoordinateLatitude
           ,@CmpCoordinateLongitude
           ,@CmpCoordinateLastDate
           ,@CmpCoordinateLastDatePersian
           ,@CmpCoordinateZoomLevel
           ,@CmpCoordinateLastUserId 
		   ,@CmpFOAccessLinkedCompany
		   ,@cmpKind/*RZ990520- Add cmpKind*/
		   /*RZ14010321- Add cmpNameOfficial*/
		   ,@cmpIsDraft
		   ,@cmpNameOfficial
		   /*RZ14010321- End*/
		   ,@CmpCampaignIds/*RZ14010519- Add cmpCampaignIds*/
		   )             
    SET @InsertedCompanyId = SCOPE_IDENTITY()
	/*********** Insert to lang table ***********/
	IF(ISNULL(@InsertedCompanyId,0) > 0)
	BEGIN
	/**/
		INSERT INTO [dbo].[CrmCompany_Lang]
			   ([CompanyID]
			   ,[LanguageID]
			   ,[cmpName]
			   ,[cmpFame]
			   ,[cmpActivityType]
			   ,[cmpTelDesc]
			   ,[cmpTelIIDesc]
			   ,[cmpFaxDesc]
			   ,[cmpAddress]
			   ,[cmpAddressDesc]
			   ,[cmpInteresting]
			   ,[cmpSpecialPoint]
			   ,[cmpDescription]
			   ,[cmpSpecialWordForCI]
			   ,[cmpInquiry]
			   /*RZ14010321- Add cmpNameOfficial*/
			   ,[cmpNameOfficial]
			   /*RZ14010321- End*/)
		 SELECT
				@InsertedCompanyId
			   ,LN.LanguageID
			   ,@CmpName
			   ,@CmpFame
			   ,@CmpActivityType
			   ,@CmpTelDesc
			   ,@CmpTelIIDesc
			   ,@CmpFaxDesc
			   ,@CmpAddress
			   ,@CmpAddressDesc
			   ,@CmpInteresting
			   ,@CmpSpecialPoint
			   ,@CmpDescription
			   ,@CmpSpecialWordForCI
			   ,@CmpInquiry
			   /*RZ14010321- Add cmpNameOfficial*/
			   ,@cmpNameOfficial
			   /*RZ14010321- End*/
		 FROM dbo.Languages LN 
		 WHERE LN.LanguageID <> @DefaultLanguageID	
		 AND ISNULL(LN.IsActive,0) = 1	
		/*----------------------------------------------*/
		/*RZ14011211- Add CompanyChangeHistory*/
		DECLARE @NewCmpGroupId INT = NULL,@NewCmpSubGroupId INT = NULL,
		@NewCmpGroupIdII INT = NULL,@NewCmpGroupIdIII INT = NULL,@NewCmpGroupIdIV INT = NULL,
		@NewCmpTypeId INT = NULL,@NewCmpPresentationId INT = NULL,@NewCmpReferUserId INT = NULL,
		@CmpChangeHistoryInsertedId INT = 0,
		@CmpChangeHistoryDesc NVARCHAR(MAX) = NULL,@CmpChangeHistory_Lang NVARCHAR(MAX) = NULL,
		@OldValue NVARCHAR(MAX) = NULL,@NewValue NVARCHAR(MAX) = NULL,@FieldName NVARCHAR(300) = NULL
		/*-----------------------------------------------*/
		SELECT TOP(1)			
            @NewCmpGroupId = [cmpGroupId]
           ,@NewCmpSubGroupId = [cmpSubGroupId]
           ,@NewCmpGroupIdII = [cmpGroupIdII]
           ,@NewCmpGroupIdIII = [cmpGroupIdIII]
           ,@NewCmpGroupIdIV = [cmpGroupIdIV]
           ,@NewCmpTypeId = [cmpTypeId]
           ,@NewCmpPresentationId = [cmpPresentationId]           
           ,@NewCmpReferUserId = [cmpReferUserId]           
		FROM [dbo].[CrmCompany]
		WHERE CompanyID = @InsertedCompanyId		
		/*-----------------------------------------------*/
		DECLARE @CmpChangeHistoryDescTbl_Lang TABLE(
			[Desc] NVARCHAR(MAX),
			LanguageID INT
		)
		INSERT INTO @CmpChangeHistoryDescTbl_Lang([Desc], [LanguageID])
		VALUES
			(N'ثبت اولیه توسط {0}', 1065),
			(N'التسجيل المبدئي بواسطة {0}', 1025),
			(N'Initial registration by {0}', 1033)

		UPDATE @CmpChangeHistoryDescTbl_Lang
		SET [Desc] = REPLACE([Desc],N'{0}',ISNULL((SELECT TOP(1) ISNULL(FName,'') + ' ' + ISNULL(LName,'') FROM [dbo].[Users] U WHERE U.UserId = @UserId),'')) 
		WHERE [LanguageID] = @DefaultLanguageID
		/**/	
		UPDATE @CmpChangeHistoryDescTbl_Lang
		SET [Desc] = REPLACE([Desc],N'{0}',ISNULL((SELECT TOP(1) ISNULL(FName,'') + ' ' + ISNULL(LName,'') FROM [dbo].[Users_Lang] U WHERE U.UserId = @UserId AND U.LanguageID = T.[LanguageID]),''))
		FROM @CmpChangeHistoryDescTbl_Lang T 
		WHERE T.[LanguageID] <> @DefaultLanguageID

		SET @CmpChangeHistoryDesc = ISNULL((SELECT TOP(1) [Desc] FROM @CmpChangeHistoryDescTbl_Lang T WHERE T.LanguageID = @DefaultLanguageID),'')
		/*-----------------------------------------------*/
		IF(ISNULL(@NewCmpGroupId,0) <> 0)
		BEGIN
		/**/
			SET @CmpChangeHistoryInsertedId  = 0
			SET @FieldName = NULL
			SET @OldValue = NULL
			SET @NewValue = NULL
			SET @CmpChangeHistory_Lang = NULL
			/**/
			SET @FieldName = N'cmpGroupId'
			SET @OldValue = NULL 
			SET @NewValue = CAST(@NewCmpGroupId AS NVARCHAR(MAX))
			SET @CmpChangeHistory_Lang = ISNULL('[' + ((STUFF((SELECT N',{' + 
															N'"LanguageID":' + CAST(L.[LanguageID] AS NVARCHAR(100)) +
															N',"OldValue":"' + ISNULL(@OldValue,'') + N'"' +
															N',"NewValue":"' + ISNULL(@NewValue,'') + N'"' +																
															N',"Desc":"' + ISNULL((SELECT TOP(1) [Desc] FROM @CmpChangeHistoryDescTbl_Lang T WHERE T.LanguageID = L.[LanguageID]),'')
														+ N'"}'					
														FROM [dbo].[Languages] L
														WHERE ISNULL(L.IsActive,0) = 1	
														AND L.[LanguageID] <> @DefaultLanguageID
														FOR XML PATH(''), TYPE).value(N'(./text())[1]', N'NVARCHAR(MAX)'), 1, 1, ''))				
											+ ']'),'')

															N',"OldValue":"' + ISNULL(@OldValue,'') + N'"' +
															N',"NewValue":"' + ISNULL(@NewValue,'') + N'"' +																
															N',"Desc":"' + ISNULL((SELECT TOP(1) [Desc] FROM @CmpChangeHistoryDescTbl_Lang T WHERE T.LanguageID = L.[LanguageID]),'')
														+ N'"}'					
														FROM [dbo].[Languages] L
														WHERE ISNULL(L.IsActive,0) = 1	
														AND L.[LanguageID] <> @DefaultLanguageID
														FOR XML PATH(''), TYPE).value(N'(./text())[1]', N'NVARCHAR(MAX)'), 1, 1, ''))				
											+ ']'),'')

															N',"Desc":"' + ISNULL((SELECT TOP(1) [Desc] FROM @CmpChangeHistoryDescTbl_Lang T WHERE T.LanguageID = L.[LanguageID]),'')

-- ==================================================

/*RZ980419*/
/*RZ990520- Add cmpMainPersonId*/
/*RZ14011018- Convert Arabic characters to Persian*/
Create PROCEDURE [dbo].[ApispInsertCompanyPersons]
/*
	exec ApispInsertCompanyPersons 		
*/
(
/*Company*/
@CompanyId INT = NULL,
@CmpNumber INT = NULL,
@CmpCode NVARCHAR(500) = NULL,
/*Person*/
@PrsLName NVARCHAR(100),
@PrsFName NVARCHAR(50) = NULL,
@PrsTitleId INT = NULL,
@PrsJobId INT = NULL,
@PrsTypeId INT = NULL,
@PrsGroupId INT = NULL,
@PrsSexId INT = NULL,
@PrsEducationId INT = NULL,
@PrsPreTel NVARCHAR(10) = NULL,
@PrsTel NVARCHAR(15) = NULL,
@PrsTelUpTo NVARCHAR(10) = NULL,
@PrsTelDesc NVARCHAR(50) = NULL,
@PrsTelII NVARCHAR(15) = NULL,
@PrsFax NVARCHAR(15) = NULL,
@PrsFaxDesc NVARCHAR(50) = NULL,
@PrsMobile NVARCHAR(15) = NULL,
@PrsWebSite NVARCHAR(50) = NULL,
@PrsEmail NVARCHAR(50) = NULL,
@PrsEmailII NVARCHAR(50) = NULL,
@PrsCountryId  INT = NULL,
@PrsProvinceId  INT = NULL,
@PrsCityId  INT = NULL,
@PrsAddress NVARCHAR(250) = NULL,
@PrsAddressDesc NVARCHAR(100) = NULL,
@PrsZipCode NVARCHAR(50) = NULL,
@PrsPostBox NVARCHAR(50) = NULL,
@PrsNationalCode NVARCHAR(30) = NULL,
@PrsIdentityNumber NVARCHAR(30) = NULL,
@PrsFatherName NVARCHAR(50) = NULL,
@PrsBirthDate NVARCHAR(8) = NULL,
@PrsBirthDateTime DATETIME = NULL,
@PrsBirthPlace NVARCHAR(50) = NULL,
@PrsIsMarried BIT = NULL,
@PrsMarriageDate NVARCHAR(8) = NULL,
@PrsMarriageDateTime DATETIME = NULL,
@PrsEducationField NVARCHAR(50) = NULL,
@PrsSkill NVARCHAR(50) = NULL,
@PrsStudyField NVARCHAR(50) = NULL,
@PrsInteresting NVARCHAR(500) = NULL,
@PrsSpecialPoint NVARCHAR(500) = NULL,
@PrsDescription NVARCHAR(500) = NULL,
@PrsDepartmentId INT = NULL,	
@PrsStopSendSms BIT = NULL,
@PrsOrderView INT = NULL,
@PrsWorkingOut BIT = NULL,
@PrsTelegramId INT = NULL,
/**/
@UserId INT,
@Month1 SMALLINT,
@RegisterDate DATETIME,
@RegisterDatePersian NVARCHAR(8),
@AppType VARCHAR(20), 
/**/
@InsertedPersonId INT OUTPUT,
@ErrorMessage NVARCHAR(MAX) OUTPUT
)
AS
BEGIN
/**/
/*-----------------------------------------------------------------------------*/
/*RZ14011018- Convert Arabic characters to Persian*/
SET @PrsFName = (SELECT dbo.[fnConvertArabicCharacterToPersian](@PrsFName))
SET @PrsLName = (SELECT dbo.[fnConvertArabicCharacterToPersian](@PrsLName))
/*RZ14011018- End*/
/*-----------------------------------------------------------------------------*/
/********************************************************************************/
/****************************** TRANSACTION *************************************/
/********************************************************************************/
BEGIN TRANSACTION Main
/**/
/********************************************************************************/
/**********************************Default LanguageID ***************************/
DECLARE @DefaultLanguageID INT 
SET @DefaultLanguageID = (SELECT TOP 1 [DefaultLanguageID] FROM [dbo].[CrmsysSetting])
/********************************************************************************/
/************************************ Year1 => FPId *****************************/
DECLARE @Year1 SMALLINT
SET @Year1 = (SELECT TOP 1 [ID] FROM [dbo].[WSFinancialPeriod] ORDER BY [ID] DESC)
/********************************************************************************/
/************************************ Error *************************************/
DECLARE @ErrorFrom VARCHAR(50), @ErrorNumber INT
/********************************************************************************/
/*********************** Check duplicate for mobile and email *******************/
/********************************************************************************/
DECLARE @ResultDupMobile NVARCHAR(50) = '', @ResultDupPrsEmail NVARCHAR(50) = '',@ResultDupPrsEmailII NVARCHAR(50) = ''
/*Mobile*/
IF(ISNULL((SELECT TOP 1 [CantSaveDupPrsMob]  FROM [dbo].[CrmsysSetting]),0) = 1)
BEGIN
	EXEC [dbo].[ApispCheckDuplicatePrsMobile]
		@prsMobile = @PrsMobile, 
		@CurrId = 0,
		@ResultDupcmpNumber = @ResultDupMobile OUTPUT 
	IF(@ResultDupMobile <> '')
		SET @ErrorMessage = @ErrorMessage + 'DupMob=' + @ResultDupMobile + '|'
END
/*Email*/
IF(ISNULL((SELECT TOP 1 [CantSaveDupPrsEmail]  FROM [dbo].[CrmsysSetting]),0) = 1)
BEGIN
	/*EmailI*/
	EXEC [dbo].[ApispCheckDuplicatePrsEmail]
	@prsEmail = @PrsEmail, 
	@CurrId = 0,
	@ResultDupcmpNumber = @ResultDupPrsEmail OUTPUT 
	IF(@ResultDupPrsEmail <> '')
		SET @ErrorMessage = @ErrorMessage + 'DupEmailI=' + @ResultDupPrsEmail + '|'	
	/*EmailII*/
	EXEC [dbo].[ApispCheckDuplicatePrsEmail]
	@prsEmail = @PrsEmailII, 
	@CurrId = 0,
	@ResultDupcmpNumber = @ResultDupPrsEmailII OUTPUT 
	IF(@ResultDupPrsEmailII <> '')
		SET @ErrorMessage = @ErrorMessage + 'DupEmailII=' + @ResultDupPrsEmail + '|'
END
/********************************************************************************/
/********************************* Check fields *********************************/
/********************************************************************************/
/*CompanyId*/
IF(@CompanyId IS NOT NULL AND @CompanyId > 0)
BEGIN
	SET @CompanyId = (SELECT TOP 1 [CompanyId] FROM [dbo].[CrmCompany] WHERE [CompanyId] = @CompanyId)
END
ELSE IF(@CmpNumber IS NOT NULL AND @CmpNumber > 0)
BEGIN	
	SET @CompanyId = (SELECT TOP 1 [CompanyId] FROM [dbo].[CrmCompany] WHERE [cmpNumber] = @CmpNumber)
END
ELSE IF(@CmpCode IS NOT NULL AND @CmpCode > 0)
BEGIN	
	SET @CompanyId = (SELECT TOP 1 [CompanyId] FROM [dbo].[CrmCompany] WHERE [cmpCode] = @CmpCode)
END
IF(@CompanyId IS NULL)
	SET @ErrorMessage = @ErrorMessage + 'No CmpId|'
/*PrsTitleId*/
IF(@PrsTitleId IS NOT NULL AND @PrsTitleId > 0)
BEGIN
	IF((SELECT TOP 1 [PersonTitleID] FROM [dbo].[CrmPersonTitle] WHERE [PersonTitleID] = @PrsTitleId) IS NULL)
		SET @ErrorMessage = @ErrorMessage + 'No PrsTitleId|'
END
/*PrsJobId*/
IF(@PrsJobId IS NOT NULL AND @PrsJobId > 0)
BEGIN
	IF((SELECT TOP 1 [PersonJobID] FROM [dbo].[CrmPersonJobs] WHERE [PersonJobID] = @PrsJobId) IS NULL)
		SET @ErrorMessage = @ErrorMessage + 'No PrsJobId|'
END
/*PrsTypeId*/
IF(@PrsTypeId IS NOT NULL AND @PrsTypeId > 0)
BEGIN
	IF((SELECT TOP 1 [PersonTypeID] FROM [dbo].[CrmPersonType] WHERE [PersonTypeID] = @PrsTypeId) IS NULL)
		SET @ErrorMessage = @ErrorMessage + 'No PrsTypeId|'
END
/*PrsGroupId*/
IF(@PrsGroupId IS NOT NULL AND @PrsGroupId > 0)
BEGIN
	IF((SELECT TOP 1 [PersonGroupID] FROM [dbo].[CrmPersonGroups] WHERE [PersonGroupID] = @PrsGroupId) IS NULL)
		SET @ErrorMessage = @ErrorMessage + 'No PrsGroupId|'
END
/*PrsSexId*/
IF(@PrsSexId IS NOT NULL AND @PrsSexId > 0)
BEGIN
	IF((SELECT TOP 1 [SexID] FROM [dbo].[Sex] WHERE [SexID] = @PrsSexId) IS NULL)
		SET @ErrorMessage = @ErrorMessage + 'No PrsSexId|'
END
/*PrsEducationId*/
IF(@PrsEducationId IS NOT NULL AND @PrsEducationId > 0)
BEGIN
	IF((SELECT TOP 1 [EducationID] FROM [dbo].[Education] WHERE [EducationID] = @PrsEducationId) IS NULL)
		SET @ErrorMessage = @ErrorMessage + 'No PrsEducationId|'
END
/*PrsCountryId*/
IF(@PrsCountryId IS NOT NULL AND @PrsCountryId > 0)
BEGIN
	IF((SELECT TOP 1 [CountryId] FROM [dbo].[Country] WHERE [CountryId] = @PrsCountryId) IS NULL)
		SET @ErrorMessage = @ErrorMessage + 'No PrsCountryId|'	
END
/*PrsProvinceId*/
IF(@PrsProvinceId IS NOT NULL AND @PrsProvinceId > 0)
BEGIN
	IF((SELECT TOP 1 [ProvinceID] FROM [dbo].[Province] WHERE [ProvinceID] = @PrsProvinceId) IS NULL)
		SET @ErrorMessage = @ErrorMessage + 'No PrsProvinceId|'	
	ELSE IF(@PrsCountryId IS NOT NULL AND @PrsCountryId > 0)
	BEGIN
		IF((SELECT TOP 1 [ProvinceID] FROM [dbo].[Province] 
			WHERE [ProvinceID] = @PrsProvinceId AND [CountryId]= @PrsCountryId) IS NULL)
		SET @ErrorMessage = @ErrorMessage + 'No PrsProvinceId|'	
	END
END
/*PrsCityId*/
IF(@PrsCityId IS NOT NULL AND @PrsCityId > 0)
BEGIN
	IF((SELECT TOP 1 [CityID] FROM [dbo].[City] WHERE [CityID] = @PrsCityId) IS NULL)
		SET @ErrorMessage = @ErrorMessage + 'No PrsCityId|'	
	ELSE IF(@PrsProvinceId IS NOT NULL AND @PrsProvinceId > 0)
	BEGIN
		IF((SELECT TOP 1 [CityID] FROM [dbo].[City] 
			WHERE [CityID] = @PrsCityId AND [ProvinceId]= @PrsProvinceId) IS NULL)
		SET @ErrorMessage = @ErrorMessage + 'No PrsCityId|'	
	END
END
/*PrsDepartmentId*/
IF(@PrsDepartmentId IS NOT NULL AND @PrsDepartmentId > 0)
BEGIN
	IF((SELECT TOP 1 [PersonDepartmentId] FROM [dbo].[CrmPersonDepartments] WHERE [PersonDepartmentId] = @PrsDepartmentId) IS NULL)
		SET @ErrorMessage = @ErrorMessage + 'No PrsDepartmentId|'
END
/*===============================================================================*/
IF(@ErrorMessage IS NULL OR @ErrorMessage = '')
BEGIN
	/********************************************************************************/
	/****************************** Insert Person ***********************************/
	/********************************************************************************/
	BEGIN TRAN
	/**/
		/*Main table*/
		INSERT INTO [dbo].[CrmCompanyPersons]
			   ([prsCompanyId]
			   ,[prsLName]
			   ,[prsFName]
			   ,[prsTitleId]
			   ,[prsJobId]
			   ,[prsTypeId]
			   ,[prsGroupId]
			   ,[prsSexId]
			   ,[prsEducationId]
			   ,[prsPreTel]
			   ,[prsTel]
			   ,[prsTelUpTo]
			   ,[prsTelDesc]
			   ,[prsTelII]
			   ,[prsFax]
			   ,[prsFaxDesc]
			   ,[prsMobile]
			   ,[prsWebSite]
			   ,[prsEmail]
			   ,[prsEmailII]
			   ,[prsCountryId]
			   ,[prsProvinceId]
			   ,[prsCityId]
			   ,[prsAddress]
			   ,[prsAddressDesc]
			   ,[prsZipCode]
			   ,[prsPostBox]
			   ,[prsNationalCode]
			   ,[prsIdentityNumber]
			   ,[prsFatherName]
			   ,[prsBirthDate]
			   ,[prsBirthDateTime]
			   ,[prsBirthPlace]
			   ,[prsIsMarried]
			   ,[prsMarriageDate]
			   ,[prsMarriageDateTime]
			   ,[prsEducationField]
			   ,[prsSkill]
			   ,[prsStudyField]
			   ,[prsInteresting]
			   ,[prsSpecialPoint]
			   ,[prsDescription]
			   ,[UserId]
			   ,[Year1]
			   ,[Month1]
			   ,[RegisterDate]
			   ,[RegisterDatePersian]
			   ,[ImportDate]
			   ,[ImportDateTime]
			   ,[prsFirstRegisterUserId]
			   ,[prsDepartmentId]
			   ,[prsStopSendSms]
			   ,[prsOrderView]
			   ,[prsWorkingOut]
			   ,[AppType]
			   ,[LanguageID]
			   ,[FirstRegisterUserId]
			   ,[FirstRegisterDatePersian]
			   ,[prsTelegramId]
			   ,[FirstRegisterDate])
		 VALUES
			   (@CompanyId
			   ,@PrsLName
			   ,@PrsFName
			   ,@PrsTitleId
			   ,@PrsJobId
			   ,@PrsTypeId
			   ,@PrsGroupId
			   ,@PrsSexId
			   ,@PrsEducationId
			   ,@PrsPreTel
			   ,@PrsTel
			   ,@PrsTelUpTo
			   ,@PrsTelDesc
			   ,@PrsTelII
			   ,@PrsFax
			   ,@PrsFaxDesc
			   ,@PrsMobile
			   ,@PrsWebSite
			   ,@PrsEmail
			   ,@PrsEmailII
			   ,@PrsCountryId
			   ,@PrsProvinceId
			   ,@PrsCityId
			   ,@PrsAddress
			   ,@PrsAddressDesc
			   ,@PrsZipCode
			   ,@PrsPostBox
			   ,@PrsNationalCode
			   ,@PrsIdentityNumber
			   ,@PrsFatherName
			   ,@PrsBirthDate
			   ,@PrsBirthDateTime
			   ,@PrsBirthPlace
			   ,@PrsIsMarried
			   ,@PrsMarriageDate
			   ,@PrsMarriageDateTime
			   ,@PrsEducationField
			   ,@PrsSkill
			   ,@PrsStudyField
			   ,@PrsInteresting
			   ,@PrsSpecialPoint
			   ,@PrsDescription
			   ,@UserId
			   ,@Year1
			   ,@Month1
			   ,@RegisterDate
			   ,@RegisterDatePersian
			   ,@RegisterDatePersian
			   ,@RegisterDate
			   ,@UserId
			   ,@PrsDepartmentId
			   ,@PrsStopSendSms
			   ,@PrsOrderView
			   ,@PrsWorkingOut
			   ,@AppType
			   ,@DefaultLanguageID
			   ,@UserId
			   ,@RegisterDatePersian
			   ,@PrsTelegramId
			   ,@RegisterDate)
		SET @InsertedPersonId = SCOPE_IDENTITY()
		/*********** Insert to lang table ***********/
		INSERT INTO [dbo].[CrmCompanyPersons_Lang]
			   ([CompanyPersonID]
			   ,[LanguageID]
			   ,[prsLName]
			   ,[prsFName]
			   ,[prsTelDesc]
			   ,[prsFaxDesc]
			   ,[prsAddress]
			   ,[prsAddressDesc]
			   ,[prsFatherName]
			   ,[prsBirthPlace]
			   ,[prsEducationField]
			   ,[prsSkill]
			   ,[prsStudyField]
			   ,[prsInteresting]
			   ,[prsSpecialPoint]
			   ,[prsDescription])
		 SELECT
			    @InsertedPersonId
			   ,LN.LanguageID
			   ,@PrsLName
			   ,@PrsFName
			   ,@PrsTelDesc
			   ,@PrsFaxDesc
			   ,@PrsAddress
			   ,@PrsAddressDesc
			   ,@PrsFatherName
			   ,@PrsBirthPlace
			   ,@PrsEducationField
			   ,@PrsSkill
			   ,@PrsStudyField
			   ,@PrsInteresting
			   ,@PrsSpecialPoint
			   ,@PrsDescription
		FROM dbo.Languages LN 
		WHERE LN.LanguageID <> @DefaultLanguageID	
		AND ISNULL(LN.IsActive,0) = 1	
		/*=============================================*/
		/*RZ990520- Add cmpMainPersonId*/
		DECLARE @CountPrs INT
		SET @CountPrs = (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM dbo.CrmCompanyPersons WHERE prsCompanyId = @CompanyId)
		IF(@CountPrs = 1 AND (ISNULL((SELECT TOP 1 CompanyPersonID 
										FROM dbo.CrmCompanyPersons 
										WHERE prsCompanyId = @CompanyId),0) = @InsertedPersonId))
		BEGIN
		/**/
			Update [dbo].[CrmCompany]
				SET cmpMainPersonId = @InsertedPersonId
			WHERE CompanyID = @CompanyId
		/**/
		END		
		/*RZ990520- End*/
		/*=============================================*/         					 	 	 
	/**/
	IF (@@ERROR > 0)
	BEGIN SET @ErrorFrom='Error In Insert Person' SET @ErrorNumber=@@ERROR ROLLBACK TRAN END		
	ELSE 
	COMMIT TRAN 	
END
/**/
/**/
IF (@ErrorNumber > 0)
BEGIN 
	RAISERROR('Error From TransAction %S', 16, 1, @ErrorFrom)
	ROLLBACK TRANSACTION Main
 	SELECT 0
END 
ELSE
BEGIN
	COMMIT TRANSACTION Main 
	SELECT 1
END	
/**/
END

-- [dbo].[ApispInsertOrder]

/*RZ980420*/
/*RZ980812- Add person when pass null*/
/*RZ14011109-Add ordDailyCounter*/
/*RZ14020419- Add ordTspSettingId*/
/*NKR14020729- Add Parameters*/
/*RZ14021004- Add OrderView to project*/
/*RZ14021005- Change orderView in project add desc to prjOrderView*/
Create PROCEDURE [dbo].[ApispInsertOrder]
/*
	exec ApispInsertOrder 				
*/
(
/*Order*/
@OrderOprId INT = NULL,
@PreOrderId INT = NULL,
@PreOrderVerifierId INT = NULL,
@OrdTitle NVARCHAR(1000) = NULL,
@OrdNumber BIGINT = NULL,
@OrdDate NVARCHAR(8),
@OrdDateTime DATETIME,
@OrdDay NVARCHAR(50),
@OrdTime NVARCHAR(50),
@OrdSubject NVARCHAR(1000) = NULL,
@OrdInstallAmount NUMERIC(18, 2) = NULL,
@OrdDiscount NUMERIC(18, 2) = NULL,
@OrdTax NUMERIC(18, 2) = NULL,
@OrdTaxIndex NUMERIC(18, 2) = NULL,
@OrdCalculateTax BIT = NULL,
@OrdIsVerified BIT = NULL,
@OrdVerifiedRegisterDate DATETIME = NULL,
@OrdVerifiedRegisterDatePersian NVARCHAR(8) = NULL,
@OrdIsFixed  BIT = NULL,
@OrdGuarantyId INT = NULL,
@OrdSpecialWord NVARCHAR(1000) = NULL,
@OrdDescriptionI NVARCHAR(4000) = NULL,
@OrdDescriptionII NVARCHAR(4000) = NULL,
@OrdDescriptionId INT = NULL,
@OrdPrintFormatId INT = NULL,
@OrdRLogoId  INT = NULL,
@OrdLLogoId  INT = NULL,
@OrdSigningI NVARCHAR(1000) = NULL,
@OrdSigningII NVARCHAR(1000) = NULL,
@OrdSigningIII NVARCHAR(1000) = NULL,
@OrdAccountNumberI NVARCHAR(1000) = NULL,
@OrdAccountShabaNumberI NVARCHAR(1000) = NULL,
@OrdAccountNumberII NVARCHAR(1000) = NULL,
@OrdAccountShabaNumberII NVARCHAR(1000) = NULL,
@OrdSignatureId INT = NULL,
@OrdVerifierId INT = NULL,
@OrdSettlementTypeId INT = NULL,
@OrdTransportation NUMERIC(18, 2) = NULL,
@OrdReferUserId INT = NULL,
@OrdDiscountPercent NUMERIC(18, 2) = NULL,
@OrdDiscountTitle NVARCHAR(1000) = NULL,
@OrdRound NUMERIC(18, 2) = NULL,
@OrdNote NVARCHAR(4000) = NULL,
@OrdcmpTitle NVARCHAR(1000) = NULL,
@OrdDeliveryDate NVARCHAR(8) = NULL,
@OrdDeliveryDateTime DATETIME = NULL,
@OrdDeliveryTime NVARCHAR(10) = NULL,
@OrdSerial NVARCHAR(50) = NULL,
@OrdVerifierRequestId INT = NULL,
@OrdStatusId INT = 1,
/*Company AND Person AND Project*/
@CompanyId INT = NULL,
@CompanyNumber INT = NULL,
@CompanyCode NVARCHAR(500) = NULL,
@CompanyPersonId INT = NULL,
@ProjectId INT = NULL,
@ProjectTitle NVARCHAR(100) = NULL,
/**/
@DefualtProjectTitle NVARCHAR(100),
/**/
@UserId INT,
@Month1 SMALLINT,
@RegisterDate DATETIME,
@RegisterDatePersian NVARCHAR(8),
@RegisterDay NVARCHAR(50),
@RegisterTime NVARCHAR(50),
@AppType VARCHAR(20),
@LanguageID INT = NULL, 
/**/
@InsertedOrderId INT OUTPUT,
@InsertedOrderNumber BIGINT OUTPUT,
@ErrorMessage NVARCHAR(MAX) OUTPUT,

@OrdDeliveryTermId INT = NULL,
@OrdDeliveryEndDate NVARCHAR(8) = NULL,
@OrdDeliveryEndDateTime DATETIME = NULL,
@OrdDeliveryEndTime NVARCHAR(10) = NULL,
@OrdDeliveryDay NVARCHAR(20) = NULL,
@OrdDeliveryEndDay NVARCHAR(20) = NULL

)
AS
BEGIN
/**/
/********************************************************************************/
/****************************** TRANSACTION *************************************/
/********************************************************************************/
BEGIN TRANSACTION Main
/**/
/********************************************************************************/
/**********************************Default LanguageID ***************************/
DECLARE @DefaultLanguageID INT 
SET @DefaultLanguageID = (SELECT TOP 1 [DefaultLanguageID] FROM [dbo].[CrmsysSetting])

/********************************************************************************/
/************************************ Year1 => FPId *****************************/
DECLARE @FPId INT
SET @FPId = (SELECT TOP 1 [ID] FROM [dbo].[WSFinancialPeriod] ORDER BY [ID] DESC)
/********************************************************************************/
/****************************** Error & Variables *******************************/
DECLARE @ErrorFrom VARCHAR(50), @ErrorNumber INT
/*RZ14020419- Add ordTspSettingId*/
DECLARE @ordTspSettingId INT 
SET @ordTspSettingId = (SELECT TOP(1) TspSettingId FROM [dbo].[TspSetting])
/*RZ14020419- End*/
/********************************************************************************/
/********************************* Check fields *********************************/
/********************************************************************************/
/*OPRID*/
IF(@OrderOprId IS NOT NULL AND @OrderOprId > 0)
BEGIN
	IF((SELECT TOP 1 [OrderOprID] FROM [dbo].[WSOrderOpr] WHERE [OrderOprID] = @OrderOprId) IS NULL)
		SET @ErrorMessage = @ErrorMessage + 'No OprId|'
END
ELSE
BEGIN
	SET @OrderOprId = (SELECT TOP 1 [OrderOprID] FROM dbo.WSOrderOpr OO ORDER BY OrderView, OrderOprId )
END
/*PREODERID*/
IF(@PreOrderId IS NOT NULL AND @PreOrderId > 0)
BEGIN
	IF((SELECT TOP 1 [PreOrderId] FROM [dbo].[WSPreOrder] WHERE [PreOrderId] = @PreOrderId) IS NULL)
		SET @ErrorMessage = @ErrorMessage + 'No PordId|'
END
/*PREORDERVERIFIERID*/
IF(@PreOrderVerifierId IS NOT NULL AND @PreOrderVerifierId > 0)
BEGIN
	IF((SELECT TOP 1 [UserId] FROM [dbo].[Users] WHERE [UserId] = @PreOrderVerifierId) IS NULL)
		SET @ErrorMessage = @ErrorMessage + 'No PordVerifyId|'
END
/*ORDGUARANTYID*/
IF(@OrdGuarantyId IS NOT NULL AND @OrdGuarantyId > 0)
BEGIN
	IF((SELECT TOP 1 [GuarantyID] FROM [dbo].[WSGuaranty] WHERE [GuarantyID] = @OrdGuarantyId) IS NULL)
		SET @ErrorMessage = @ErrorMessage + 'No OrdGuarantyId|'
END
/*ORDPRINTFORMATID*/
IF(@OrdPrintFormatId IS NOT NULL AND @OrdPrintFormatId > 0)
BEGIN
	IF((SELECT TOP 1 [PrintFormatID] FROM [dbo].[PrintFormat] WHERE [PrintFormatID] = @OrdPrintFormatId) IS NULL)
		SET @ErrorMessage = @ErrorMessage + 'No OrdPrintFormatId|'
END
/*ORDRLOGOID*/
IF(@OrdRLogoId IS NOT NULL AND @OrdRLogoId > 0)
BEGIN
	IF((SELECT TOP 1 [LogoID] FROM [dbo].[WSLogo] WHERE [LogoID] = @OrdRLogoId) IS NULL)
		SET @ErrorMessage = @ErrorMessage + 'No OrdRLogoId|'
END
/*ORDLLOGOID*/
IF(@OrdLLogoId IS NOT NULL AND @OrdLLogoId > 0)
BEGIN
	IF((SELECT TOP 1 [LogoID] FROM [dbo].[WSLogo] WHERE [LogoID] = @OrdLLogoId) IS NULL)
		SET @ErrorMessage = @ErrorMessage + 'No OrdLLogoId|'
END
/*ORDERVERIFIERID*/
IF(@OrdVerifierId IS NOT NULL AND @OrdVerifierId > 0)
BEGIN
	IF((SELECT TOP 1 [UserId] FROM [dbo].[Users] WHERE [UserId] = @OrdVerifierId) IS NULL)
		SET @ErrorMessage = @ErrorMessage + 'No OrdVerifyId|'
END
/*ORDSETTLEMENTTYPEID*/
IF(@OrdSettlementTypeId IS NOT NULL AND @OrdSettlementTypeId > 0)
BEGIN
	IF((SELECT TOP 1 [Id] FROM [dbo].[WSSettlementType] WHERE [Id] = @OrdSettlementTypeId) IS NULL)
		SET @ErrorMessage = @ErrorMessage + 'No OrdSettlementTypeId|'
END
/*ORDREFERUSERID*/
IF(@OrdReferUserId IS NOT NULL AND @OrdReferUserId > 0)
BEGIN
	IF((SELECT TOP 1 [UserId] FROM [dbo].[Users] WHERE [UserId] = @OrdReferUserId) IS NULL)
		SET @ErrorMessage = @ErrorMessage + 'No OrdReferUserId|'
END
ELSE
BEGIN
	SET @OrdReferUserId = @UserId
END
/*ORDVERIFIERREQUESTID*/
IF(@OrdVerifierRequestId IS NOT NULL AND @OrdVerifierRequestId > 0)
BEGIN
	IF((SELECT TOP 1 [UserId] FROM [dbo].[Users] WHERE [UserId] = @OrdVerifierRequestId) IS NULL)
		SET @ErrorMessage = @ErrorMessage + 'No OrdVerifierRequestId|'
END
/*ORDVERIFIERREQUESTID*/
IF(@OrdStatusId IS NOT NULL AND @OrdStatusId > 0)
BEGIN
	IF((SELECT TOP 1 [OrderStatusId] FROM [dbo].[WSOrderStatus] WHERE [OrderStatusId] = @OrdStatusId) IS NULL)
		SET @ErrorMessage = @ErrorMessage + 'No OrdStatusId|'
END
ELSE
	SET @OrdStatusId = 1
/*COMPANYID*/
IF(@CompanyId IS NOT NULL AND @CompanyId > 0)
BEGIN
	IF((SELECT TOP 1 [CompanyID] FROM [dbo].[CrmCompany] WHERE [CompanyID] = @CompanyId) IS NULL)
		SET @ErrorMessage = @ErrorMessage + 'No CmpId|'
END
ELSE IF (@CompanyNumber IS NOT NULL AND @CompanyNumber <> '')
BEGIN
	SET @CompanyId = (SELECT TOP 1 [CompanyID] FROM [dbo].[CrmCompany] WHERE [cmpNumber] = @CompanyNumber)
END
ELSE IF (@CompanyCode IS NOT NULL AND @CompanyCode <> '')
BEGIN
	SET @CompanyId = (SELECT TOP 1 [CompanyID] FROM [dbo].[CrmCompany] WHERE [cmpCode] = @CompanyCode)
END
IF(@CompanyId IS NULL OR @CompanyId <= 0)
	SET @ErrorMessage = @ErrorMessage + 'No CmpId|'
/*COMPANYPERSONID*/	
IF(@CompanyPersonId IS NOT NULL AND @CompanyPersonId > 0)
BEGIN
	DECLARE @PrsId INT
	SET @PrsId = (SELECT TOP 1 [CompanyPersonID] FROM [dbo].[CrmCompanyPersons] WHERE [CompanyPersonID] = @CompanyPersonId)
	IF(@PrsId IS NULL)
		SET @ErrorMessage = @ErrorMessage + 'No PrsId|'
	ELSE IF((@CompanyId IS NOT NULL AND @CompanyId > 0))
	BEGIN	
		SET @PrsId = (SELECT TOP 1 [CompanyPersonID] FROM [dbo].[CrmCompanyPersons] WHERE [CompanyPersonID] = @CompanyPersonId AND [prsCompanyId] = @CompanyId)
		IF(@PrsId IS NULL)
			SET @ErrorMessage = @ErrorMessage + 'No PrsId|'
	END
	/**/	
END
ELSE IF(ISNULL(@CompanyPersonId,0) = 0)/*RZ980812- Add person when pass null*/
BEGIN
/**/
	SET @CompanyPersonId = (SELECT TOP 1 [CompanyPersonID] FROM dbo.CrmCompanyPersons WHERE prsCompanyId = @CompanyId ORDER BY ISNULL(prsOrderView, CompanyPersonID))
	IF(ISNULL(@CompanyPersonId,0) = 0)
	BEGIN
	/**/
		INSERT INTO [dbo].[CrmCompanyPersons]
			([prsCompanyId]
			,[prsLName]           
			,[UserId]
			,[Year1]
			,[Month1]
			,[RegisterDate]
			,[RegisterDatePersian]
			,[ImportDate]
			,[ImportDateTime]
			,[prsFirstRegisterUserId]           
			,[AppType]
			,[LanguageID]
			,[FirstRegisterUserId]
			,[FirstRegisterDatePersian]           
			,[FirstRegisterDate])
		VALUES
			(@CompanyId
			,N'اینترنتی'           
			,@UserId
			,CAST(@FPId AS SMALLINT)
			,@Month1
			,@RegisterDate
			,@RegisterDatePersian
			,@RegisterDatePersian
			,@RegisterDate
			,@UserId
			,@AppType
			,@DefaultLanguageID
			,@UserId
			,@RegisterDatePersian
			,@RegisterDate)
	
		SET @CompanyPersonId = SCOPE_IDENTITY()
		/*MultiLanguage*/
		INSERT INTO [dbo].[CrmCompanyPersons_Lang]
			   ([CompanyPersonID]
			   ,[LanguageID]
			   ,[prsLName])
		SELECT
				@CompanyPersonId
			   ,L.LanguageID
			   ,N'اینترنتی'
		FROM dbo.Languages L
		WHERE ISNULL(IsActive,0) = 1 
		AND L.LanguageID <> @DefaultLanguageID
	/**/
	END		
/**/
END

/*PROJECTID*/		
IF(@ProjectId IS NOT NULL AND @ProjectId > 0)
BEGIN
	DECLARE @PrjId INT
	SET @PrjId = (SELECT TOP 1 [ProjectID] FROM [dbo].[CrmProjects] WHERE [ProjectID] = @ProjectId)
	IF(@PrjId IS NULL)
		SET @ErrorMessage = @ErrorMessage + 'No PrjId|'
	ELSE IF((@CompanyId IS NOT NULL AND @CompanyId > 0))
	BEGIN	
		SET @PrjId = (SELECT TOP 1 [ProjectID] FROM [dbo].[CrmProjects] WHERE [ProjectID] = @ProjectId AND [prjCompanyId] = @CompanyId)
		IF(@PrjId IS NULL)
			SET @ErrorMessage = @ErrorMessage + 'No PrjId|'
	END
END

/********************************************************************************/
/********************************* OrdNumber ************************************/
/********************************************************************************/
IF(@ErrorMessage IS NULL OR @ErrorMessage = '')
BEGIN
	IF(@OrdNumber IS NULL OR @OrdNumber = '')
	BEGIN
		SET @OrdNumber = (SELECT [dbo].[ApifnReturnOrderNumber] (@FPId, @OrderOprId))
	END 
	ELSE
	BEGIN
		DECLARE @DupOrdNumber BIGINT
		SET @DupOrdNumber = (SELECT TOP 1 [ordNumber] from [dbo].[ApifnCheckDuplicateOrderNumber](@OrdNumber, 0, @FPId,@OrderOprId))
		IF(@DupOrdNumber IS NOT NULL AND @DupOrdNumber > 0)
			SET @ErrorMessage = @ErrorMessage + 'DupOrdNumber= ' + CAST(ISNULL(@DupOrdNumber,0) AS NVARCHAR(200)) + '|'				
	END
END

/********************************************************************************/
/********************************* OrdSerial ************************************/
/********************************************************************************/
IF(@ErrorMessage IS NULL OR @ErrorMessage = '')
BEGIN
	IF(@OrdSerial IS NULL OR @OrdSerial = '')
	BEGIN
		SET @OrdSerial = (SELECT [dbo].[ApifnReturnOrderSerialNumber] (@FPId, @OrderOprId))
	END 	
END
/********************************************************************************/
/********************************* Insert Order *********************************/
/********************************************************************************/
BEGIN TRY  
    IF(@ErrorMessage IS NULL OR @ErrorMessage = '')/*No Error*/
	BEGIN
		/*============================== Project =========================*/
		BEGIN TRAN
			IF(@ProjectId IS NULL)
			BEGIN
				DECLARE @prjcnvsStatusGroupId INT			
				SET @prjcnvsStatusGroupId = (SELECT TOP 1 ConversationStatusGroupID FROM dbo.CrmConversationStatusGroups WHERE ConversationStatusGroupID > 0)
				IF(@ProjectTitle IS NULL OR @ProjectTitle = '')
					SET @ProjectTitle = @DefualtProjectTitle
				/**/
				SET @ProjectId =(SELECT TOP 1 P.ProjectID FROM [dbo].[CrmProjects] P 
						  WHERE P.prjCompanyId = @CompanyId 
						  AND (REPLACE(REPLACE(REPLACE(CAST(P.prjTitle AS NVARCHAR(2000)) ,
						  NCHAR(1740), NCHAR(1610)),NCHAR(1603),NCHAR(1705)),NCHAR(1609),NCHAR(1740)) Like '%' + 
						  REPLACE(REPLACE(REPLACE(CAST(@ProjectTitle AS NVARCHAR(2000)), NCHAR(1740),
						  NCHAR(1610)),NCHAR(1603),NCHAR(1705)),NCHAR(1609),NCHAR(1740)) +'%'))

				IF(@ProjectId IS NULL)
				BEGIN
					SET @ProjectId = (SELECT TOP 1 ProjectId From dbo.CrmProjects WHERE prjCompanyId = @CompanyId ORDER BY [prjOrderView] DESC,prjStartDate DESC,[ProjectID] DESC)/*RZ14021005- Change orderView in project add desc to prjOrderView*/
					IF(ISNULL(@ProjectId,0) = 0)
					BEGIN
					/**/
						/*-------------------------------------------------*/
						/*RZ14021004- Add OrderView to project*/
						DECLARE @prjOrderView INT = 0
						EXEC [dbo].[ApispReturnCompanyProjectOrderView]
						@CompanyId  = @CompanyId,
						@prjOrderView = @prjOrderView  OUTPUT					
						/*RZ14021004- End*/
						/*-------------------------------------------------*/
						/*Main table*/
						INSERT INTO [dbo].[CrmProjects]
							   ([prjCompanyId]
							   ,[prjTitle]
							   ,[prjStartDate]
							   ,[prjStartDay]
							   ,[prjStartTime]
							   ,[prjStartDateTime]
							   ,[prjStarterUserId]
							   ,[prjFirstRegisterDate]
							   ,[prjFirstRegisterUserId]
							   ,[UserId]
							   ,[Year1]
							   ,[Month1]
							   ,[RegisterDate]
							   ,[RegisterDatePersian]
							   ,[AppType]
							   ,[LanguageID]
							   ,[FirstRegisterUserId]
							   ,[FirstRegisterDateTime]
							   ,[FirstRegisterDatePersian]
							   ,[prjFirstRegisterDateTime]
							   ,[FirstRegisterDate]
							   ,[prjcnvsStatusGroupId]/*RZ980812*/
							   ,[prjOrderView]/*RZ14021004- Add OrderView to project*/)
						 VALUES
							   (@CompanyId
							   ,@ProjectTitle
							   ,@RegisterDatePersian
							   ,@RegisterDay
							   ,@RegisterTime
							   ,@RegisterDate
							   ,@UserId
							   ,@RegisterDatePersian
							   ,@UserId
							   ,@UserId
							   ,CAST(@FPId AS SMALLINT)
							   ,@Month1
							   ,@RegisterDate
							   ,@RegisterDatePersian
							   ,@AppType
							   ,@DefaultLanguageID
							   ,@UserId
							   ,@RegisterDate
							   ,@RegisterDatePersian
							   ,@RegisterDate
							   ,@RegisterDate
							   ,@prjcnvsStatusGroupId/*RZ980812*/
							   ,@prjOrderView/*RZ14021004- Add OrderView to project*/)
						/**/
						SET @ProjectId = SCOPE_IDENTITY()
						/*********** Insert to lang table ***********/
						INSERT INTO [dbo].[CrmProjects_Lang]
							([ProjectID]
							,[LanguageID]
							,[prjTitle])
						SELECT 
							 @ProjectId
							,L.LanguageID
							,@ProjectTitle			
						FROM [dbo].[Languages] L
						WHERE L.LanguageID <> @DefaultLanguageID
						AND ISNULL(L.IsActive,0) = 1
					/**/
					END					
				END
			END	
		IF (@@ERROR > 0)
		BEGIN SET @ErrorFrom='Error In Insert Project' SET @ErrorNumber=@@ERROR ROLLBACK TRAN END		
		ELSE 
		COMMIT TRAN
		/*=============================== Order ==========================*/		
		BEGIN TRAN
			/*---------------------------------------------------*/
			/*RZ14011109-Add ordDailyCounter*/
			DECLARE @ordDailyCounter BIGINT
			SET @ordDailyCounter = (SELECT [dbo].[ApifnReturnOrderDailyCounter](@OrderOprId, @ordDateTime))
			/*RZ14011109-End*/
			/*---------------------------------------------------*/
			/*Main table*/
			INSERT INTO [dbo].[WSOrder]
				   ([OrderOprID]
				   ,[PreOrderId]
				   ,[PreOrderVerifierId]
				   ,[ordTitle]
				   ,[ordNumber]
				   ,[ordDate]
				   ,[ordCompanyId]
				   ,[ordCompanyPersonId]
				   ,[ordProjectId]
				   ,[ordSubject]
				   ,[ordInstallAmount]
				   ,[ordDiscount]				   
				   ,[ordTax]
				   ,[ordTaxIndex]
				   ,[ordCalculateTax]
				   ,[ordIsVerified]
				   ,[ordVerifiedRegisterDate]
				   ,[ordVerifiedRegisterDatePersian]
				   ,[ordStatusId]
				   ,[ordIsFixed]
				   ,[ordGuarantyId]
				   ,[ordSpecialWord]
				   ,[ordDescriptionI]
				   ,[ordDescriptionII]
				   ,[ordDescriptionId]
				   ,[ordPrintFormatId]
				   ,[ordRLogoId]
				   ,[ordLLogoId]
				   ,[ordSigningI]
				   ,[ordSigningII]
				   ,[ordSigningIII]
				   ,[ordAccountNumberI]
				   ,[ordAccountShabaNumberI]
				   ,[ordAccountNumberII]
				   ,[ordAccountShabaNumberII]
				   ,[ordSignatureId]
				   ,[ordImportDate]
				   ,[ordFirstRegisterUserId]
				   ,[UserId]
				   ,[FPId]
				   ,[Month1]
				   ,[RegisterDate]
				   ,[RegisterDatePersian]
				   ,[ordVerifierId]
				   ,[ordSettlementTypeId]
				   ,[ordDay]
				   ,[ordTime]
				   ,[ordTransportation]
				   ,[ordReferUserId]
				   ,[ordDiscountPercent]
				   ,[ordDiscountTitle]
				   ,[ordRound]
				   ,[ordNote]
				   ,[ordcmpTitle]
				   ,[ordDateTime]
				   ,[ordImportDateTime]
				   ,[ordDeliveryDate]
				   ,[ordDeliveryDateTime]
				   ,[ordDeliveryTime]
				   ,[ordSerial]
				   ,[AppType]
				   ,[ordVerifierRequestId]
				   ,[LanguageID]
				   ,[FirstRegisterUserId]
				   ,[FirstRegisterDatePersian]
				   ,[FirstRegisterDate]
				   ,[ordDailyCounter]/*RZ14011109-Add ordDailyCounter*/
				   ,[ordTspSettingId]/*RZ14020419- Add ordTspSettingId*/
				   /*NKR14020730- Start*/
				   ,[ordDeliveryTermId]
				   ,[ordDeliveryEndDate]
				   ,[ordDeliveryEndDateTime]
				   ,[ordDeliveryEndTime]
				   ,[ordDeliveryDay]
				   ,[ordDeliveryEndDay]
				   /*NKR14020730- End*/
				   )
			 VALUES
				   (@OrderOprId
				   ,@PreOrderId
				   ,@PreOrderVerifierId
				   ,@OrdTitle
				   ,@OrdNumber
				   ,@OrdDate
				   ,@CompanyId
				   ,@CompanyPersonId
				   ,@ProjectId
				   ,@OrdSubject
				   ,@OrdInstallAmount
				   ,@OrdDiscount				   
				   ,@OrdTax
				   ,@OrdTaxIndex
				   ,@OrdCalculateTax
				   ,@OrdIsVerified
				   ,@OrdVerifiedRegisterDate
				   ,@OrdVerifiedRegisterDatePersian
				   ,@OrdStatusId
				   ,@OrdIsFixed
				   ,@OrdGuarantyId
				   ,@OrdSpecialWord
				   ,@OrdDescriptionI
				   ,@OrdDescriptionII
				   ,@OrdDescriptionId
				   ,@OrdPrintFormatId
				   ,@OrdRLogoId
				   ,@OrdLLogoId
				   ,@OrdSigningI
				   ,@OrdSigningII
				   ,@OrdSigningIII
				   ,@OrdAccountNumberI
				   ,@OrdAccountShabaNumberI
				   ,@OrdAccountNumberII
				   ,@OrdAccountShabaNumberII
				   ,@OrdSignatureId
				   ,@RegisterDatePersian
				   ,@UserId
				   ,@UserId
				   ,@FPId
				   ,@Month1
				   ,@RegisterDate
				   ,@RegisterDatePersian
				   ,@OrdVerifierId
				   ,@OrdSettlementTypeId
				   ,@OrdDay
				   ,@OrdTime
				   ,@OrdTransportation
				   ,@OrdReferUserId
				   ,@OrdDiscountPercent
				   ,@OrdDiscountTitle
				   ,@OrdRound
				   ,@OrdNote
				   ,@OrdcmpTitle
				   ,@OrdDateTime
				   ,@RegisterDate
				   ,@OrdDeliveryDate
				   ,@OrdDeliveryDateTime
				   ,@OrdDeliveryTime
				   ,@OrdSerial
				   ,@AppType
				   ,@OrdVerifierRequestId
				   ,@DefaultLanguageID
				   ,@UserId
				   ,@RegisterDatePersian
				   ,@RegisterDate
				   ,@ordDailyCounter/*RZ14011109-Add ordDailyCounter*/
				   ,@ordTspSettingId/*RZ14020419- Add ordTspSettingId*/
				   /*NKR14020730- Start*/
				   ,@OrdDeliveryTermId
				   ,@OrdDeliveryEndDate
				   ,@OrdDeliveryEndDateTime
				   ,@OrdDeliveryEndTime
				   ,@OrdDeliveryDay
				   ,@OrdDeliveryEndDay
				   /*NKR14020730- End*/
				   )
			/**/
			SET @InsertedOrderId = SCOPE_IDENTITY()
			SET @InsertedOrderNumber = @OrdNumber
			/*********** Insert to lang table ***********/
			INSERT INTO [dbo].[WSOrder_Lang]
				   ([OrderID]
				   ,[ordTitle]
				   ,[ordSubject]
				   ,[ordSpecialWord]
				   ,[ordDescriptionI]
				   ,[ordDescriptionII]
				   ,[ordSigningI]
				   ,[ordSigningII]
				   ,[ordSigningIII]
				   ,[ordDiscountTitle]
				   ,[ordNote]
				   ,[ordcmpTitle]
				   ,[LanguageID])
			 SELECT
				    @InsertedOrderId
				   ,@OrdTitle
				   ,@OrdSubject
				   ,@OrdSpecialWord
				   ,@OrdDescriptionI
				   ,@OrdDescriptionII
				   ,@OrdSigningI
				   ,@OrdSigningII
				   ,@OrdSigningIII
				   ,@OrdDiscountTitle
				   ,@OrdNote
				   ,@OrdcmpTitle
				   ,LN.LanguageID
			 FROM dbo.Languages LN 
			 WHERE LN.LanguageID <> @DefaultLanguageID	
			 AND ISNULL(LN.IsActive,0) = 1

		IF (@@ERROR > 0)
		BEGIN SET @ErrorFrom='Error In Insert Order' SET @ErrorNumber=@@ERROR ROLLBACK TRAN END		
		ELSE 
		COMMIT TRAN 				
		/*=========================== Calculate Order ====================*/
		EXEC [dbo].[ApispCalcAndUpdateOrderAmounts] 	@InsertedOrderId, @Month1, @LanguageID, @DefaultLanguageID				
		/**/
	END
	/**/	
	COMMIT TRANSACTION Main 
	SELECT 1

END TRY  
BEGIN CATCH  
	 /*RAISERROR('Error From TransAction %S', 16, 1, @ErrorFrom)*/
	 ROLLBACK TRANSACTION Main
     SET @InsertedOrderId = 0
	 SET @InsertedOrderNumber = 0
	 SET @ErrorMessage = 'Error|'
	 IF(@ErrorFrom IS NOT NULL AND @ErrorFrom <> '')
		SET @ErrorMessage = @ErrorMessage + @ErrorFrom
 	 SELECT 0	 
END CATCH  	
/********************************************************************************/
/**/
END








-- ==================================================
-- [dbo].[ApispInsertOrderItems]
-- ==================================================








/*RZ980420*/
/*RZ990206- Add ordIPrdPriceGroupID*/
/*RZ990520- Add check pordIsFixed*/
/*RZ990818- Add sum of tax,discount,price before tax*/
/*RZ14010926- Add OrdIValidityExpireDate*/
/*NKR14020727- Add Delivery Fields*/
CREATE PROCEDURE [dbo].[ApispInsertOrderItems]
/*
	exec ApispInsertOrderItems
*/
(
/*Order*/
@OrderId INT = NULL,
@OrdNumber BIGINT = NULL,
/*Order Item*/
@OrdIPrdId INT = NULL,
@OrdIPrdCode NVARCHAR(50) = NULL,
@OrdIQuantity FLOAT,
@OrdIPrice NUMERIC(18, 2) = NULL,
@OrdITotalPrice NUMERIC(18, 2) = NULL,
@OrdIDescription NVARCHAR(4000) = NULL,
@OrdIQuantityDesc NVARCHAR(1000) = NULL,
@OrdIDiscountI NUMERIC(18, 2) = NULL,
@OrdIDiscount NUMERIC(18, 2) = NULL,
@OrdIDiscountPercent NUMERIC(18, 2) = NULL,
@OrdIDiscountIPercent NUMERIC(18, 2) = NULL,
@OrdIPrdUnitId INT = NULL,
@OrdICurrencyUnitId INT = NULL,
@OrdIOrderView INT = NULL,
@OrdIFCR FLOAT = NULL,
@OrdITax NUMERIC(18,2) = NULL,
@OrdITaxIndex NUMERIC(18,2)	= NULL,
@OrdICalculateTax BIT = NULL,
@OrdINotVisible BIT = NULL,
@OrdIFCRI FLOAT = NULL,
@OrdINotInSum BIT = NULL,
@OrdIWHId INT = NULL,
@OrdIRound NUMERIC(18,2) = NULL,
@OrdIRoundPlus NUMERIC(18,2) = NULL,
@OrdIPricePartRate FLOAT = NULL,
@OrdIPricePartI NUMERIC(18,2) = NULL,
@OrdIPricePartII NUMERIC(18,2) = NULL,
@OrdIPrdPriceGroupID INT = NULL,/*RZ990206- Add ordIPrdPriceGroupID*/
@OrdITracing NVARCHAR(200) = NULL,/*RZ990818- Add sum of tax,discount,price before tax*/
/**/
@UserId INT,
@Month1 SMALLINT,
@RegisterDate DATETIME,
@RegisterDatePersian NVARCHAR(8),
@AppType VARCHAR(20),
@LanguageID INT = NULL, 
/**/
@InsertedOrderItemId INT OUTPUT,
@ErrorMessage NVARCHAR(MAX) OUTPUT,
/*RZ14010926- Add OrdIValidityExpireDate*/
@OrdIValidityExpireDate DATETIME = NULL,
@OrdIValidityExpireDay INT = NULL,
@OrdIValidityExpireHour INT = NULL,
@OrdIValidityExpireMinute INT = NULL,
/*RZ14010926- End*/
/*NKR14020727- Start*/
@OrdIPrdValidityCountingMin INT = NULL,
@OrdIPrdValidityCountingNum INT = NULL,
@OrdIDeliveryTermId INT = NULL,
@OrdIDeliveryDate DATETIME = NULL,
@OrdIDeliveryTime NVARCHAR(10) = NULL,
@OrdIDeliveryDay NVARCHAR(20) = NULL,
@OrdIDeliveryEndDate DATETIME = NULL,
@OrdIDeliveryEndTime NVARCHAR(10) = NULL,
@OrdIDeliveryEndDay NVARCHAR(20) = NULL,
@OrdIDeliveryUserId INT = NULL
/*NKR14020727- End*/
)
AS
BEGIN
/**/
/********************************************************************************/
/****************************** TRANSACTION *************************************/
/********************************************************************************/
BEGIN TRANSACTION Main
/**/
/********************************************************************************/
/**********************************Default LanguageID ***************************/
DECLARE @DefaultLanguageID INT 
SET @DefaultLanguageID = (SELECT TOP 1 [DefaultLanguageID] FROM [dbo].[CrmsysSetting])

/********************************************************************************/
/************************************ Year1 => FPId *****************************/
DECLARE @FPId INT
SET @FPId = (SELECT TOP 1 [ID] FROM [dbo].[WSFinancialPeriod] ORDER BY [ID] DESC)
/********************************************************************************/
/****************************** Error & Variables *******************************/
DECLARE @ErrorFrom VARCHAR(50), @ErrorNumber INT
/********************************************************************************/
/********************************* Check fields *********************************/
/********************************************************************************/
/*Order*/
IF(@OrderId IS NOT NULL AND @OrderId > 0)
BEGIN
	SET @OrderId = (SELECT TOP 1 [OrderID] FROM [dbo].[WSOrder] WHERE [OrderID] = @OrderId)
END
ELSE IF (@OrdNumber IS NOT NULL AND @OrdNumber <> '')
BEGIN
	SET @OrderId = (SELECT TOP 1 [OrderID] FROM [dbo].[WSOrder] WHERE [ordNumber] = @OrdNumber)
END
IF(@OrderId IS NULL OR @OrderId <= 0)
	SET @ErrorMessage = @ErrorMessage + 'No OrdId|'
/*Product*/
IF(@OrdIPrdId IS NOT NULL AND @OrdIPrdId > 0)
BEGIN
	SET @OrdIPrdId = (SELECT TOP 1 [ProductID] FROM [dbo].[WSProducts] WHERE [ProductID] = @OrdIPrdId)
END
ELSE IF (@OrdIPrdCode IS NOT NULL AND @OrdIPrdCode <> '')
BEGIN
	SET @OrdIPrdId = (SELECT TOP 1 [ProductID] FROM [dbo].[WSProducts] WHERE [PrdCode] = @OrdIPrdCode)
END
IF(@OrdIPrdId IS NULL OR @OrdIPrdId <= 0)
	SET @ErrorMessage = @ErrorMessage + 'No PrdId|'
/*OrdIPrdUnitId*/
IF(@OrdIPrdUnitId IS NOT NULL AND @OrdIPrdUnitId > 0)
BEGIN
	IF((SELECT TOP 1 [ProductUnitID] FROM [dbo].[WSProductUnits] WHERE [ProductUnitID] = @OrdIPrdUnitId) IS NULL)
		SET @ErrorMessage = @ErrorMessage + 'No OrdIPrdUnitId|'
END
/*OrdICurrencyUnitId*/
IF(@OrdICurrencyUnitId IS NOT NULL AND @OrdICurrencyUnitId > 0)
BEGIN
	IF((SELECT TOP 1 [CurrencyUnitID] FROM [dbo].[WSCurrencyUnits] WHERE [CurrencyUnitID] = @OrdICurrencyUnitId) IS NULL)
		SET @ErrorMessage = @ErrorMessage + 'No OrdICurrencyUnitId|'
END
/*OrdIWHId*/
IF(@OrdIWHId IS NOT NULL AND @OrdIWHId > 0)
BEGIN
	IF((SELECT TOP 1 [WareHouseID] FROM [dbo].[WsWareHouse] WHERE [WareHouseID] = @OrdIWHId) IS NULL)
		SET @ErrorMessage = @ErrorMessage + 'No OrdIWHId|'
END
ELSE 
	SET @OrdIWHId = (SELECT TOP 1 [WareHouseID] FROM [dbo].[WsWareHouse] WHERE ISNULL([WrhsDeActive],0) = 1)	
/*OrdIPrdPriceGroupID*/
/*RZ990206- Add ordIPrdPriceGroupID*/
IF(ISNULL(@OrdIPrdPriceGroupID,0) > 0)
BEGIN
	IF((SELECT TOP 1 [ProductPriceGroupID] FROM [dbo].[WSProductPriceGroup] WHERE [ProductPriceGroupID] = @OrdIPrdPriceGroupID) IS NULL)
		SET @ErrorMessage = @ErrorMessage + 'No OrdIPrdPriceGroupID|'
END
ELSE 
BEGIN
/**/
	SET @OrdIPrdPriceGroupID = (SELECT TOP 1 ProductPriceGroupId 
												FROM dbo.WSProductPrice PP
												WHERE PP.PrdId = @OrdIPrdId AND PP.Price = @OrdIPrice)
	IF(ISNULL(@OrdIPrdPriceGroupID,0) = 0)
	BEGIN
	/**/
		SET @OrdIPrdPriceGroupID = (SELECT TOP 1 ProductPriceGroupId 
									FROM dbo.WSProductPriceGroup PP
									ORDER BY PP.OrderView)													
	/**/
	END
/**/
END
/*RZ990206- End*/
/*RZ990520- Add check pordIsFixed*/
DECLARE @IsFixed BIT
SET @IsFixed = (SELECT TOP 1 ordIsFixed FROM dbo.WSOrder WHERE OrderID = @OrderId)
IF(ISNULL(@IsFixed,0) = 1)
BEGIN
	SET @ErrorMessage = @ErrorMessage + 'IsFixed|'
END
/*RZ990520- End*/
/********************************************************************************/
/******************** Insert Order Items ****************************************/
/********************************************************************************/
BEGIN TRY  
    IF(@ErrorMessage IS NULL OR @ErrorMessage = '')/*No Error*/
	BEGIN		
		/*======================== Order Item ==========================*/		
		BEGIN TRAN
			/*=====================================================*/
			/*RZ14010926- Add OrdIValidityExpireDate*/
			DECLARE @OrdIValidityExpireDatePersian NVARCHAR(8) = NULL,@OrdDateTime DATETIME = NULL
			IF(@OrdIValidityExpireDate IS NULL)
			BEGIN
			/**/
				IF(@OrdIValidityExpireDay IS NOT NULL OR @OrdIValidityExpireHour IS NOT NULL OR @OrdIValidityExpireMinute IS NOT NULL)
				BEGIN
				/**/		
					SET @OrdDateTime = (SELECT TOP(1) ordDateTime FROM [dbo].[WSOrder] WHERE OrderID = @OrderId)
					SET @OrdIValidityExpireDate = DATEADD(DAY, ISNULL(@OrdIValidityExpireDay,0), @OrdDateTime)
					SET @OrdIValidityExpireDate = DATEADD(HOUR, ISNULL(@OrdIValidityExpireHour,0), @OrdIValidityExpireDate)
					SET @OrdIValidityExpireDate = DATEADD(MINUTE, ISNULL(@OrdIValidityExpireMinute,0), @OrdIValidityExpireDate)
				/**/
				END
			/**/
			END							   
			/*--------------------------*/
			IF(@OrdIValidityExpireDate IS NOT NULL)
			BEGIN
			/**/
				SET @OrdIValidityExpireDatePersian = (SELECT dbo.[fnGregorianToPersianDateWithOutSlash](CAST(@OrdIValidityExpireDate AS DATE)))
			/**/
			END	
			/*RZ14010926- End*/
			/*NKR14020730- Start*/
			DECLARE @OrdIDeliveryDatePersian NVARCHAR(8) = NULL
			IF(@OrdIDeliveryDate IS NOT NULL)
				SET @OrdIDeliveryDatePersian = (SELECT dbo.[fnGregorianToPersianDateWithOutSlash](CAST(@OrdIDeliveryDate AS DATE)))

			DECLARE @OrdIDeliveryEndDatePersian NVARCHAR(8) = NULL
			IF(@OrdIDeliveryEndDate IS NOT NULL)
				SET @OrdIDeliveryEndDatePersian = (SELECT dbo.[fnGregorianToPersianDateWithOutSlash](CAST(@OrdIDeliveryEndDate AS DATE)))
			/*NKR14020730- End*/
			/*=============== Discount =================*/
			IF(ISNULL(@OrdIDiscount,0) = 0 AND ISNULL(@OrdIDiscountPercent,0) > 0 AND ISNULL(@OrdIPrice,0) > 0)
			BEGIN
				SET @OrdIDiscount = (ISNULL(@OrdIPrice,0) * ISNULL(@OrdIDiscountPercent,0)) / 100
			END	
			IF(ISNULL(@OrdIDiscount,0) > 0 AND ISNULL(@OrdIDiscountPercent,0) = 0 AND ISNULL(@OrdIPrice,0) > 0)
			BEGIN
				SET @OrdIDiscountPercent = (ISNULL(@OrdIDiscount,0) * 100) / ISNULL(@OrdIPrice,0)
			END	
			/*=============== Calculate ================*/
			DECLARE @TmpI NUMERIC(18,2) = 0, @TmpII NUMERIC(18,2) = 0, 
			@TotalPrice NUMERIC(18,2) = 0
			/*Tmp = Quantity * FCR * FCRI*//*FCRI =1 */
			SET @TmpI = @OrdIQuantity * ISNULL(@OrdIFCR,1) * ISNULL(@OrdIFCRI,1)/** 1 */
			/*TempII = price - discount - round + roundPlus*/
			SET @TmpII = ISNULL(@OrdIPrice,0)/*- 0 - 0 + 0 */
			SET @TmpII = ISNULL(@OrdIPrice,0) - ISNULL(@OrdIDiscount,0) - ISNULL(@OrdIRound,0) + ISNULL(@OrdIRoundPlus,0)/*- 0 - 0 + 0 */

			/*=============== Tax =================*/
			/*RZ990818*/
			IF(ISNULL(@OrdITax,0) = 0 AND ISNULL(@OrdITaxIndex,0) > 0 AND ISNULL(@TmpII,0) > 0)
			BEGIN
				SET @OrdITax = (ISNULL(@TmpII,0) * ISNULL(@OrdITaxIndex,0)) / 100
			END	
			IF(ISNULL(@OrdITax,0) > 0 AND ISNULL(@OrdITaxIndex,0) = 0 AND ISNULL(@TmpII,0) > 0)
			BEGIN
				SET @OrdITaxIndex = (ISNULL(@OrdITax,0) * 100) / ISNULL(@TmpII,0)
			END			
			/*RZ990818-End*/
			/*===================================*/
			/*TotalPrice = (@TmpI * @TmpII) + (@TmpI * tax)*/
			SET @TotalPrice = (@TmpI * @TmpII) + (@TmpI * ISNULL(@OrdITax,0))/*RZ990818*/
			IF(@OrdITotalPrice IS NULL )
				SET @OrdITotalPrice = @TotalPrice
			/**********************************************************/				
			/*Set OrderView*/
			IF(@OrdIOrderView IS NULL)
			BEGIN
				DECLARE @ItemCount INT
				SET @ItemCount = (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM [dbo].[WSOrderItems] OI WHERE OI.OrderId = @OrderId)
				SET @OrdIOrderView = (@ItemCount + 1) + (@ItemCount * 4 ) - 1		
			END
			/**********************************************************/
			/*RZ990818- Add sum of tax,discount,price before tax*/
			DECLARE @OrdIPriceSum Numeric(18, 2) = 0,
			@OrdIDiscountSum Numeric(18, 2) = 0,			
			@OrdITaxSum Numeric(18, 2) = 0

			SET @OrdIPriceSum = (@TmpI * @TmpII)
			SET @OrdIDiscountSum = ISNULL(@OrdIDiscount,0) * @TmpI
			SET @OrdITaxSum = ISNULL(@OrdITax,0) * @TmpI
			/*RZ990818- End*/	
			/**********************************************************/
			/*Main table*/
			INSERT INTO [dbo].[WSOrderItems]
				   ([OrderId]
				   ,[ordIPrdId]
				   ,[ordIQuantity]
				   ,[ordIPrice]
				   ,[ordITotalPrice]
				   ,[ordIDescription]
				   ,[UserId]
				   ,[FPId]
				   ,[Month1]
				   ,[RegisterDate]
				   ,[RegisterDatePersian]
				   ,[ordIQuantityDesc]
				   ,[ordIDiscountI]
				   ,[ordIDiscount]
				   ,[ordIDiscountPercent]
				   ,[ordIDiscountIPercent]
				   ,[ordIPrdUnitId]
				   ,[ordICurrencyUnitId]
				   ,[ordIOrderView]
				   ,[ordIFCR]
				   ,[ordITax]
				   ,[ordITaxIndex]
				   ,[ordICalculateTax]
				   ,[ordINotVisible]
				   ,[ordIFCRI]
				   ,[ordINotInSum]
				   ,[ordIWHId]
				   ,[ordIRound]
				   ,[AppType]
				   ,[LanguageID]
				   ,[FirstRegisterUserId]
				   ,[FirstRegisterDatePersian]
				   ,[FirstRegisterDate]
				   ,[ordIRoundPlus]
				   ,[ordIPricePartRate]
				   ,[ordIPricePartI]
				   ,[ordIPricePartII]
				   ,[ordIPrdPriceGroupID]/*RZ990206- Add ordIPrdPriceGroupID*/
				   /*RZ990818- Add sum of tax,discount,price before tax*/
				   ,[ordITracing]
				   ,[ordIPriceSum]
				   ,[ordIDiscountSum]				
				   ,[ordITaxSum]
				   /*RZ990818- End*/
				   /*RZ14010926- Add OrdIValidityExpireDate*/
					,[ordIValidityExpireDate]
					,[ordIValidityExpireDatePersian]
					/*RZ14010926- End*/
				   /*NKR14020730- Start*/
					,[ordIPrdValidityCountingMin]
					,[ordIPrdValidityCountingNum]
					,[ordIDeliveryTermId]
					,[ordIDeliveryDate]
					,[ordIDeliveryDatePersian]
					,[ordIDeliveryTime]
					,[ordIDeliveryDay]
					,[ordIDeliveryEndDate]
					,[ordIDeliveryEndDatePersian]
					,[ordIDeliveryEndTime]
					,[ordIDeliveryEndDay]
					,[ordIDeliveryUserId]
				   /*NKR14020730- End*/
				   )
			 VALUES
				   (@OrderId
				   ,@OrdIPrdId
				   ,@OrdIQuantity
				   ,@OrdIPrice
				   ,@OrdITotalPrice
				   ,@OrdIDescription
				   ,@UserId
				   ,@FPId
				   ,@Month1
				   ,@RegisterDate
				   ,@RegisterDatePersian
				   ,@OrdIQuantityDesc
				   ,@OrdIDiscountI
				   ,@OrdIDiscount
				   ,@OrdIDiscountPercent
				   ,@OrdIDiscountIPercent
				   ,@OrdIPrdUnitId
				   ,@OrdICurrencyUnitId
				   ,@OrdIOrderView
				   ,@OrdIFCR
				   ,@OrdITax
				   ,@OrdITaxIndex
				   ,@OrdICalculateTax
				   ,@OrdINotVisible
				   ,@OrdIFCRI
				   ,@OrdINotInSum
				   ,@OrdIWHId
				   ,@OrdIRound
				   ,@AppType
				   ,@DefaultLanguageID
				   ,@UserId
				   ,@RegisterDatePersian
				   ,@RegisterDate
				   ,@OrdIRoundPlus
				   ,@OrdIPricePartRate
				   ,@OrdIPricePartI
				   ,@OrdIPricePartII
				   ,@OrdIPrdPriceGroupID/*RZ990206- Add ordIPrdPriceGroupID*/
				   /*RZ990818- Add sum of tax,discount,price before tax*/
				   ,@OrdITracing
				   ,@OrdIPriceSum
				   ,@OrdIDiscountSum				
				   ,@OrdITaxSum
				   /*RZ990818- End*/
				   /*RZ14010926- Add OrdIValidityExpireDate*/
				   ,@OrdIValidityExpireDate
				   ,@OrdIValidityExpireDatePersian
				   /*RZ14010926- End*/
				   /*NKR14020730- Start*/
				   ,@OrdIPrdValidityCountingMin
				   ,@OrdIPrdValidityCountingNum
				   ,@OrdIDeliveryTermId
				   ,@OrdIDeliveryDate
				   ,@OrdIDeliveryDatePersian
				   ,@OrdIDeliveryTime
				   ,@OrdIDeliveryDay
				   ,@OrdIDeliveryEndDate
				   ,@OrdIDeliveryEndDatePersian
				   ,@OrdIDeliveryEndTime
				   ,@OrdIDeliveryEndDay
				   ,@OrdIDeliveryUserId
				   /*NKR14020730- End*/
				   )
			/**/
			SET @InsertedOrderItemId = SCOPE_IDENTITY()			
			/*********** Insert to lang table ***********/
			INSERT INTO [dbo].[WSOrderItems_Lang]
				   ([OrderItemID]
				   ,[ordIDescription]
				   ,[ordIQuantityDesc]
				   ,[LanguageID])
			SELECT
				    @InsertedOrderItemId
				   ,@OrdIDescription
				   ,@OrdIQuantityDesc
				   ,LN.LanguageID
			FROM dbo.Languages LN 
			WHERE LN.LanguageID <> @DefaultLanguageID	
			AND ISNULL(LN.IsActive,0) = 1
		IF (@@ERROR > 0)
		BEGIN SET @ErrorFrom='Error In Insert OrderItem' SET @ErrorNumber=@@ERROR ROLLBACK TRAN END		
		ELSE 
		COMMIT TRAN 				
		/*=========================== Calculate Order ====================*/
		EXEC [dbo].[ApispCalcAndUpdateOrderAmounts] @OrderId, @Month1, @LanguageID, @DefaultLanguageID				
		/**/
	END
	/**/	
	COMMIT TRANSACTION Main 
	SELECT 1

END TRY  
BEGIN CATCH  
	 /*RAISERROR('Error From TransAction %S', 16, 1, @ErrorFrom)*/
	 ROLLBACK TRANSACTION Main
     SET @InsertedOrderItemId = 0	 
	 SET @ErrorMessage = 'Error|'
	 IF(@ErrorFrom IS NOT NULL AND @ErrorFrom <> '')
		SET @ErrorMessage = @ErrorMessage + @ErrorFrom
 	 SELECT 0	 
END CATCH  	
/********************************************************************************/
/**/
END
