-- [dbo].[ApispInsertOrderPayments]
-- ==================================================




/*RZ980420*/
/*RZ990206- Add ordpayBankDocNumber*/
Create PROCEDURE [dbo].[ApispInsertOrderPayments]
/*
	exec ApispInsertOrderPayments 								
*/
(
/*Order*/
@OrderId INT = NULL,
@OrdNumber BIGINT = NULL,
/*Order Payment*/
@OrdpayTypeId INT = NULL,
@OrdpayDocId INT = NULL,
@OrdpayDocTypeId INT = NULL,
@OrdpayAmount NUMERIC(18,2),
@OrdpayDocNumber NVARCHAR(50) = NULL,
@OrdpayDocDate NVARCHAR(8) = NULL,
@OrdpayDocDateTime DATETIME = NULL,
@OrdpayDescription NVARCHAR(2000) = NULL,
@OrdpayDocAmount NUMERIC(18,2),
@PreOrderPaymentId INT = NULL,
@OrdpayBankDocNumber NVARCHAR(50) = NULL,/*RZ990206- Add ordpayBankDocNumber*/
/**/
@UserId INT,
@Month1 SMALLINT,
@RegisterDate DATETIME,
@RegisterDatePersian NVARCHAR(8),
@AppType VARCHAR(20),
@LanguageID INT = NULL, 
/**/
@InsertedOrderPaymentId INT OUTPUT,
@ErrorMessage NVARCHAR(MAX) OUTPUT
)
AS
BEGIN
/**/
/********************************************************************************/
/****************************** TRANSACTION *************************************/
/********************************************************************************/
BEGIN TRANSACTION Main
/**/
/********************************************************************************/
/**********************************Default LanguageID ***************************/
DECLARE @DefaultLanguageID INT 
SET @DefaultLanguageID = (SELECT TOP 1 [DefaultLanguageID] FROM [dbo].[CrmsysSetting])

/********************************************************************************/
/************************************ Year1 => FPId *****************************/
DECLARE @FPId INT
SET @FPId = (SELECT TOP 1 [ID] FROM [dbo].[WSFinancialPeriod] ORDER BY [ID] DESC)
/********************************************************************************/
/****************************** Error & Variables *******************************/
DECLARE @ErrorFrom VARCHAR(50), @ErrorNumber INT
/********************************************************************************/
/********************************* Check fields *********************************/
/********************************************************************************/
/*Order*/
IF(@OrderId IS NOT NULL AND @OrderId > 0)
BEGIN
	SET @OrderId = (SELECT TOP 1 [OrderID] FROM [dbo].[WSOrder] WHERE [OrderID] = @OrderId)
END
ELSE IF (@OrdNumber IS NOT NULL AND @OrdNumber <> '')
BEGIN
	SET @OrderId = (SELECT TOP 1 [OrderID] FROM [dbo].[WSOrder] WHERE [ordNumber] = @OrdNumber)
END
IF(@OrderId IS NULL OR @OrderId <= 0)
	SET @ErrorMessage = @ErrorMessage + 'No OrdId|'
/*OrdpayTypeId*/
IF(@OrdpayTypeId IS NOT NULL AND @OrdpayTypeId > 0)
BEGIN
	IF((SELECT TOP 1 [OrderPaymentTypeId] FROM [dbo].[WSOrderPaymentTypes] WHERE [OrderPaymentTypeId] = @OrdpayTypeId) IS NULL)
		SET @ErrorMessage = @ErrorMessage + 'No OrdpayTypeId|'
END
ELSE
	SET @OrdPayTypeId = (SELECT TOP 1 [OrderPaymentTypeId] FROM [dbo].[WSOrderPaymentTypes] ORDER BY OrderView)
/*OrdPayDocDateTime*/
IF(@OrdPayDocDateTime IS NULL)
	SET @OrdPayDocDateTime = @RegisterDate
/*OrdPayDocDate*/
IF(@OrdPayDocDate IS NULL)
	SET @OrdPayDocDate = @RegisterDatePersian
/*OrdpayDocAmount*/
IF(ISNULL(@OrdpayDocAmount,0) = 0)
BEGIN
	SET @OrdpayDocAmount = @OrdpayAmount
END
/********************************************************************************/
/******************** Insert Order Payments *************************************/
/********************************************************************************/
BEGIN TRY  
    IF(@ErrorMessage IS NULL OR @ErrorMessage = '')/*No Error*/
	BEGIN		
		/*======================== Order Paymant ==========================*/		
		BEGIN TRAN			
			/*Main table*/
			INSERT INTO [dbo].[WSOrderPayments]
				([ImportDate]
				,[ordpayTypeId]
				,[ordpayOrderId]
				,[ordpayDocId]
				,[ordpayDocTypeId]
				,[ordpayAmount]
				,[ordpayDocNumber]
				,[ordpayDocDate]
				,[ordpayDescription]
				,[ordpayFirstRegisterUserId]
				,[ordpayFirstRegisteDatePersian]
				,[ordpayFirstRegisteDate]
				,[UserId]
				,[FPId]
				,[Month1]
				,[RegisterDate]
				,[RegisterDatePersian]
				,[ImportDateTime]
				,[ordpayDocDateTime]
				,[ordpayDocAmount]
				,[PreOrderPaymentId]
				,[AppType]
				,[LanguageID]
				,[FirstRegisterUserId]
				,[FirstRegisterDatePersian]
				,[FirstRegisterDate]
				,[ordpayBankDocNumber]/*RZ990206- Add ordpayBankDocNumber*/)
			VALUES
				(@RegisterDatePersian
				,@OrdpayTypeId
				,@OrderId
				,@OrdpayDocId
				,@OrdpayDocTypeId
				,@OrdpayAmount
				,@OrdpayDocNumber
				,@OrdpayDocDate
				,@OrdpayDescription
				,@UserId
				,@RegisterDatePersian
				,@RegisterDate
				,@UserId
				,@FPId
				,@Month1
				,@RegisterDate
				,@RegisterDatePersian
				,@RegisterDate
				,@OrdpayDocDateTime
				,@OrdpayDocAmount
				,@PreOrderPaymentId
				,@AppType
				,@DefaultLanguageID
				,@UserId
				,@RegisterDatePersian
				,@RegisterDate
				,@OrdpayBankDocNumber/*RZ990206- Add ordpayBankDocNumber*/)
			/**/
			SET @InsertedOrderPaymentId = SCOPE_IDENTITY()			
			/*********** Insert to lang table ***********/
			INSERT INTO [dbo].[WSOrderPayments_Lang]
				([OrderPaymentId]
				,[ordpayDescription]
				,[LanguageID])
			SELECT
				 @InsertedOrderPaymentId
				,@OrdpayDescription
				,LN.LanguageID
			FROM dbo.Languages LN 
			WHERE LN.LanguageID <> @DefaultLanguageID	
			AND ISNULL(LN.IsActive,0) = 1
		IF (@@ERROR > 0)
		BEGIN SET @ErrorFrom='Error In Insert OrderPayment' SET @ErrorNumber=@@ERROR ROLLBACK TRAN END		
		ELSE 
		COMMIT TRAN 				
		/*=========================== Calculate Order ====================*/
		EXEC [dbo].[ApispCalcAndUpdateOrderAmounts] @OrderId, @Month1, @LanguageID, @DefaultLanguageID				
		/**/
	END
	/**/	
	COMMIT TRANSACTION Main 
	SELECT 1

END TRY  
BEGIN CATCH  
	 /*RAISERROR('Error From TransAction %S', 16, 1, @ErrorFrom)*/
	 ROLLBACK TRANSACTION Main
     SET @InsertedOrderPaymentId = 0	 
	 SET @ErrorMessage = 'Error|'
	 IF(@ErrorFrom IS NOT NULL AND @ErrorFrom <> '')
		SET @ErrorMessage = @ErrorMessage + @ErrorFrom
 	 SELECT 0	 
END CATCH  	
/********************************************************************************/
/**/
END

@RegisterDatePersian NVARCHAR(8),

@RegisterTime NVARCHAR(50),

ELSE
	SET @PordStatusId = 1
/*PordGuarantyId*/

		SET @ErrorMessage = @ErrorMessage + 'No PordCreditDurationId|'
END
/*PordPrintFormatId*/

BEGIN

		SET @ErrorMessage = @ErrorMessage + 'No PordPrintFormatId|'
END
/*PordRLogoId*/

BEGIN

		SET @ErrorMessage = @ErrorMessage + 'No PordRLogoId|'
END
/*PordLLogoId*/

BEGIN

		SET @ErrorMessage = @ErrorMessage + 'No PordLLogoId|'

/*PordSignatureId*/

BEGIN

		SET @ErrorMessage = @ErrorMessage + 'No PordReferUserId|'
END

BEGIN
	SET @PordReferUserId = @UserId
END
/*PreOrderOprId*/

		SET @ErrorMessage = @ErrorMessage + 'No PreOrderOprId|'

		SET @ErrorMessage = @ErrorMessage + 'No PordVerificationWorkFlowId|'
END
/*------------------------*/
/*COMPANYID*/
IF(ISNULL(@CompanyId,0) > 0)
BEGIN
	IF((SELECT TOP 1 [CompanyID] FROM [dbo].[CrmCompany] WHERE [CompanyID] = @CompanyId) IS NULL)
		SET @ErrorMessage = @ErrorMessage + 'No CmpId|'
END
ELSE IF (ISNULL(@CompanyNumber,0) > 0)
BEGIN
	SET @CompanyId = (SELECT TOP 1 [CompanyID] FROM [dbo].[CrmCompany] WHERE [cmpNumber] = @CompanyNumber)
END
ELSE IF (ISNULL(@CompanyCode,'') <> '')
BEGIN
	SET @CompanyId = (SELECT TOP 1 [CompanyID] FROM [dbo].[CrmCompany] WHERE [cmpCode] = @CompanyCode)
END
IF(ISNULL(@CompanyId,0) <= 0)
	SET @ErrorMessage = @ErrorMessage + 'No CmpId|'
/*COMPANYPERSONID*/	
IF(ISNULL(@CompanyPersonId,0) > 0)
BEGIN
	DECLARE @PrsId INT
	SET @PrsId = (SELECT TOP 1 [CompanyPersonID] FROM [dbo].[CrmCompanyPersons] WHERE [CompanyPersonID] = @CompanyPersonId)
	IF(@PrsId IS NULL)
		SET @ErrorMessage = @ErrorMessage + 'No PrsId|'
	ELSE IF((ISNULL(@CompanyId,0) > 0))
	BEGIN	
		SET @PrsId = (SELECT TOP 1 [CompanyPersonID] FROM [dbo].[CrmCompanyPersons] WHERE [CompanyPersonID] = @CompanyPersonId AND [prsCompanyId] = @CompanyId)
		IF(@PrsId IS NULL)
			SET @ErrorMessage = @ErrorMessage + 'No PrsId|'
	END
	/**/	
END
ELSE IF(ISNULL(@CompanyPersonId,0) = 0)
BEGIN
/**/
	SET @CompanyPersonId = (SELECT TOP 1 [CompanyPersonID] FROM dbo.CrmCompanyPersons WHERE prsCompanyId = @CompanyId ORDER BY ISNULL(prsOrderView, CompanyPersonID))
	IF(ISNULL(@CompanyPersonId,0) = 0 AND ISNULL(@ErrorMessage,'') = '')
	BEGIN
	/**/
		INSERT INTO [dbo].[CrmCompanyPersons]
			([prsCompanyId]
			,[prsLName]           
			,[UserId]
			,[Year1]

		VALUES

			,N'اینترنتی'           
			,@UserId

			,@Month1
			,@RegisterDate

			,@RegisterDatePersian
			,@RegisterDate
			,@UserId
			,@AppType
			,@DefaultLanguageID
			,@UserId
			,@RegisterDatePersian
			,@RegisterDate)
	
		SET @CompanyPersonId = SCOPE_IDENTITY()
		/*MultiLanguage*/
		INSERT INTO [dbo].[CrmCompanyPersons_Lang]
			   ([CompanyPersonID]
			   ,[LanguageID]
			   ,[prsLName])
		SELECT
				@CompanyPersonId
			   ,L.LanguageID
			   ,N'اینترنتی'
		FROM dbo.Languages L
		WHERE ISNULL(IsActive,0) = 1 
		AND L.LanguageID <> @DefaultLanguageID
	/**/
	END		
/**/
END
/*PROJECTID*/		
IF(ISNULL(@ProjectId,0) > 0)
BEGIN
	DECLARE @PrjId INT
	SET @PrjId = (SELECT TOP 1 [ProjectID] FROM [dbo].[CrmProjects] WHERE [ProjectID] = @ProjectId)
	IF(@PrjId IS NULL)
		SET @ErrorMessage = @ErrorMessage + 'No PrjId|'
	ELSE IF((ISNULL(@CompanyId,0) > 0))
	BEGIN	
		SET @PrjId = (SELECT TOP 1 [ProjectID] FROM [dbo].[CrmProjects] WHERE [ProjectID] = @ProjectId AND [prjCompanyId] = @CompanyId)
		IF(@PrjId IS NULL)
			SET @ErrorMessage = @ErrorMessage + 'No PrjId|'
	END
END
/*PordVerifiedRegisterDate*/
IF(@PordVerifiedRegisterDate IS NULL)
	SET @PordVerifiedRegisterDate = @RegisterDate
/********************************************************************************/
/********************************* PreOrdNumber *********************************/
/********************************************************************************/
IF(ISNULL(@ErrorMessage,'') = '')
BEGIN
/**/
	IF(ISNULL(@PordNumber,0) = 0)
	BEGIN
		SET @PordNumber = (SELECT [dbo].[ApifnReturnPreOrderNumber] (@FPId))
	END 
	ELSE
	BEGIN
		DECLARE @DupPreOrdNumber BIGINT
		SET @DupPreOrdNumber = (SELECT TOP 1 [pordNumber] from [dbo].[ApifnCheckDuplicatePreOrderNumber](@PordNumber, 0))
		IF(ISNULL(@DupPreOrdNumber,0) > 0)
			SET @ErrorMessage = @ErrorMessage + 'DupPordNumber= ' + CAST(ISNULL(@DupPreOrdNumber,0) AS NVARCHAR(200)) + '|'				
	END
/**/
END
/********************************************************************************/
/******************************* PordExpireDate *********************************/
/********************************************************************************/
IF(ISNULL(@ErrorMessage,'') = '' AND ISNULL(@PordCreditDurationId,0) > 0)/*@PordExpireDateTime IS NULL AND*/
BEGIN
/**/	
	DECLARE @CreditDurationHours INT
	SET @CreditDurationHours = (SELECT TOP 1 [CreditDurationHours] FROM [dbo].[WSCreditDuration] WHERE [CreditDurationID] = @PordCreditDurationId)
	IF(ISNULL(@CreditDurationHours,0) <> 0 AND @PordDateTime IS NOT NULL)
	BEGIN
	/**/
		SET @PordExpireDateTime = DATEADD(HOUR,@CreditDurationHours,@PordDateTime)
		SET @PordExpireDate = (SELECT dbo.[fnGregorianToPersianDateWithOutSlash](@PordExpireDateTime))		
	/**/
	END	
/**/		
END
/********************************************************************************/
/******************************** Insert PreOrder *******************************/
/********************************************************************************/
BEGIN TRY  
    IF(ISNULL(@ErrorMessage,'') = '')/*No Error*/
	BEGIN
		/*============================== Project =========================*/
		BEGIN TRAN
			IF(ISNULL(@ProjectId,0) = 0)
			BEGIN
				DECLARE @prjcnvsStatusGroupId INT			
				SET @prjcnvsStatusGroupId = (SELECT TOP 1 ConversationStatusGroupID FROM dbo.CrmConversationStatusGroups WHERE ConversationStatusGroupID > 0)
				IF(ISNULL(@ProjectTitle,'') = '')
					SET @ProjectTitle = @DefualtProjectTitle
				/**/
				SET @ProjectId =(SELECT TOP 1 P.ProjectID FROM [dbo].[CrmProjects] P 
						  WHERE P.prjCompanyId = @CompanyId 
						  AND (REPLACE(REPLACE(REPLACE(CAST(P.prjTitle AS NVARCHAR(2000)) ,
						  NCHAR(1740), NCHAR(1610)),NCHAR(1603),NCHAR(1705)),NCHAR(1609),NCHAR(1740)) Like '%' + 
						  REPLACE(REPLACE(REPLACE(CAST(@ProjectTitle AS NVARCHAR(2000)), NCHAR(1740),
						  NCHAR(1610)),NCHAR(1603),NCHAR(1705)),NCHAR(1609),NCHAR(1740)) +'%'))

				IF(ISNULL(@ProjectId,0) = 0)
				BEGIN
					SET @ProjectId = (SELECT TOP 1 ProjectId From dbo.CrmProjects WHERE prjCompanyId = @CompanyId ORDER BY [prjOrderView] DESC,prjStartDate DESC,[ProjectID] DESC)/*RZ14021005- Change orderView in project add desc to prjOrderView*/
					IF(ISNULL(@ProjectId,0) = 0)
					BEGIN
					/**/
						/*-------------------------------------------------*/
						/*RZ14021004- Add OrderView to project*/
						DECLARE @prjOrderView INT = 0
						EXEC [dbo].[ApispReturnCompanyProjectOrderView]
						@CompanyId  = @CompanyId,
						@prjOrderView = @prjOrderView  OUTPUT					
						/*RZ14021004- End*/
						/*-------------------------------------------------*/
						/*Main table*/
						INSERT INTO [dbo].[CrmProjects]
							   ([prjCompanyId]
							   ,[prjTitle]
							   ,[prjStartDate]
							   ,[prjStartDay]
							   ,[prjStartTime]
							   ,[prjStartDateTime]
							   ,[prjStarterUserId]
							   ,[prjFirstRegisterDate]
							   ,[prjFirstRegisterUserId]
							   ,[UserId]
							   ,[Year1]
							   ,[Month1]
							   ,[RegisterDate]
							   ,[RegisterDatePersian]
							   ,[AppType]
							   ,[LanguageID]
							   ,[FirstRegisterUserId]
							   ,[FirstRegisterDateTime]
							   ,[FirstRegisterDatePersian]
							   ,[prjFirstRegisterDateTime]
							   ,[FirstRegisterDate]
							   ,[prjcnvsStatusGroupId]
							   ,[prjOrderView]/*RZ14021004- Add OrderView to project*/)
						 VALUES
							   (@CompanyId
							   ,@ProjectTitle
							   ,@RegisterDatePersian
							   ,@RegisterDay
							   ,@RegisterTime
							   ,@RegisterDate
							   ,@UserId
							   ,@RegisterDatePersian
							   ,@UserId
							   ,@UserId
							   ,CAST(@FPId AS SMALLINT)
							   ,@Month1
							   ,@RegisterDate
							   ,@RegisterDatePersian
							   ,@AppType
							   ,@DefaultLanguageID
							   ,@UserId
							   ,@RegisterDate
							   ,@RegisterDatePersian
							   ,@RegisterDate
							   ,@RegisterDate
							   ,@prjcnvsStatusGroupId
							   ,@prjOrderView/*RZ14021004- Add OrderView to project*/)
						/**/
						SET @ProjectId = SCOPE_IDENTITY()
						/*********** Insert to lang table ***********/
						INSERT INTO [dbo].[CrmProjects_Lang]
							([ProjectID]
							,[LanguageID]
							,[prjTitle])
						SELECT 
							 @ProjectId
							,L.LanguageID
							,@ProjectTitle			
						FROM [dbo].[Languages] L
						WHERE L.LanguageID <> @DefaultLanguageID
						AND ISNULL(L.IsActive,0) = 1
					/**/
					END					
				END
			END	
		IF (@@ERROR > 0)
		BEGIN SET @ErrorFrom='Error In Insert Project' SET @ErrorNumber=@@ERROR ROLLBACK TRAN END		
		ELSE 
		COMMIT TRAN
		/*=============================== PreOrder ==========================*/		
		BEGIN TRAN
			/*Main table*/
			INSERT INTO [dbo].[WSPreOrder]
			   ([pordTitle]
			   ,[pordNumber]
			   ,[pordDate]
			   ,[pordCompanyId]

			   ,[pordTransportation]
			   ,[pordReferUserId]
			   ,[pordRound]
			   ,[pordcmpTitle]
			   ,[pordDateTime]
			   ,[pordImportDateTime]
			   ,[pordDeliveryDate]
			   ,[pordDeliveryDateTime]
			   ,[pordDeliveryTime]
			   ,[PreOrderOprID]
			   ,[AppType]
			   ,[LanguageID]
			   ,[FirstRegisterUserId]
			   ,[FirstRegisterDatePersian]
			   ,[FirstRegisterDate]
			   ,[pordVerificationWorkFlowId]
			   ,[pordExpireDate]
			   ,[pordExpireDateTime]
			   /*NKR14020730- Start*/
			   ,[pordDeliveryTermId]
			   ,[pordDeliveryEndDate]
			   ,[pordDeliveryEndDateTime]
			   ,[pordDeliveryEndTime]
			   ,[pordDeliveryDay]
			   ,[pordDeliveryEndDay]
			   /*NKR14020730- End*/)
		 VALUES
			   (@PordTitle
			   ,@PordNumber
			   ,@PordDate
			   ,@CompanyId
			   ,@CompanyPersonId
			   ,@ProjectId
			   ,@PordSubject
			   ,@PordInstallAmount
			   ,@PordDiscount
			   ,@PordTax
			   ,@PordTaxIndex
			   ,@PordCalculateTax
			   ,@PordIsVerified
			   ,@PordVerifierId
			   ,@PordVerifierRequestId
			   ,@PordVerifiedRegisterDate
			   ,@PordVerifiedRegisterDatePersian
			   ,@PordStatusId
			   ,@PordIsFixed
			   ,@PordGuarantyId
			   ,@PordPayConditionId
			   ,@PordDeliveryTimeId
			   ,@PordCreditDurationId
			   ,@PordSpecialWord
			   ,@PordDescriptionI
			   ,@PordDescriptionII
			   ,@PordDescriptionId
			   ,@PordPrintFormatId
			   ,@PordRLogoId
			   ,@PordLLogoId
			   ,@PordSigningI
			   ,@PordSigningII
			   ,@PordSigningIII
			   ,@PordAccountNumberI
			   ,@PordAccountShabaNumberI
			   ,@PordAccountNumberII
			   ,@PordAccountShabaNumberII
			   ,@PordAddress
			   ,@PordTelephone
			   ,@PordFax
			   ,@PordWebSite
			   ,@PordEmail
			   ,@PordSignatureId
			   ,@RegisterDatePersian
			   ,@UserId
			   ,@UserId
			   ,@FPId
			   ,@Month1
			   ,@RegisterDate
			   ,@RegisterDatePersian
			   ,@PordDay
			   ,@PordTime
			   ,@PordDiscountTitle
			   ,@PordDiscountPercent
			   ,@PordTransportation
			   ,@PordReferUserId
			   ,@PordRound
			   ,@PordcmpTitle
			   ,@PordDateTime
			   ,@RegisterDate
			   ,@PordDeliveryDate
			   ,@PordDeliveryDateTime
			   ,@PordDeliveryTime
			   ,@PreOrderOprId
			   ,@AppType
			   ,@DefaultLanguageID
			   ,@UserId
			   ,@RegisterDatePersian
			   ,@RegisterDate
			   ,@PordVerificationWorkFlowId
			   ,@PordExpireDate
			   ,@PordExpireDateTime
			   /*NKR14020730- Start*/
			   ,@PordDeliveryTermId
			   ,@PordDeliveryEndDate
			   ,@PordDeliveryEndDateTime
			   ,@PordDeliveryEndTime
			   ,@PordDeliveryDay
			   ,@PordDeliveryEndDay
			   /*NKR14020730- End*/
				)
			/**/
			SET @InsertedPreOrderId = SCOPE_IDENTITY()
			SET @InsertedPreOrderNumber = @PordNumber
			/*********** Insert to lang table ***********/						
			INSERT INTO [dbo].[WSPreOrder_Lang]
					([PreOrderID]
					,[LanguageID]
					,[pordTitle]
					,[pordSubject]
					,[pordSpecialWord]
					,[pordDescriptionI]
					,[pordDescriptionII]
					,[pordSigningI]
					,[pordSigningII]
					,[pordSigningIII]
					,[pordAddress]
					,[pordDiscountTitle]
					,[pordcmpTitle])
			 SELECT
				   @InsertedPreOrderId
				   ,LN.LanguageID
				   ,@PordTitle
				   ,@PordSubject				   
				   ,@PordSpecialWord
				   ,@PordDescriptionI
				   ,@PordDescriptionII
				   ,@PordSigningI
				   ,@PordSigningII
				   ,@PordSigningIII
				   ,@PordAddress
				   ,@PordDiscountTitle
				   ,@PordcmpTitle
			 FROM dbo.Languages LN 
			 WHERE LN.LanguageID <> @DefaultLanguageID	
			 AND ISNULL(LN.IsActive,0) = 1

		IF (@@ERROR > 0)
		BEGIN SET @ErrorFrom='Error In Insert PreOrder' SET @ErrorNumber=@@ERROR ROLLBACK TRAN END		
		ELSE 
		COMMIT TRAN 				
		/*=========================== Calculate PreOrder ====================*/
		EXEC [dbo].[ApispCalcAndUpdatePreOrderAmounts] 	@InsertedPreOrderId, @Month1, @LanguageID, @DefaultLanguageID						
		/**/
	END
	/**/	
	COMMIT TRANSACTION Main 
	SELECT 1

END TRY  
BEGIN CATCH  
	 /*RAISERROR('Error From TransAction %S', 16, 1, @ErrorFrom)*/
	 ROLLBACK TRANSACTION Main
     SET @InsertedPreOrderId = 0
	 SET @InsertedPreOrderNumber = 0
	 SET @ErrorMessage = 'Error|'
	 IF(ISNULL(@ErrorFrom,'') <> '')
		SET @ErrorMessage = @ErrorMessage + @ErrorFrom
 	 SELECT 0	 
END CATCH  	
/********************************************************************************/
/**/
END








-- ==================================================
-- [dbo].[ApispInsertPreOrderItems]
-- ==================================================








/*RZ990206*/
/*RZ990520- Add check pordIsFixed*/
/*RZ990818- Add sum of tax,discount,price before tax*/
/*NKR14020727- Add Validity Fields*/
CREATE PROCEDURE [dbo].[ApispInsertPreOrderItems]
/*
	exec ApispInsertPreOrderItems 								
*/
(
/*PreOrder*/
@PreOrderId INT = NULL,
@PordNumber BIGINT = NULL,
/*PreOrder Item*/
@PordIPrdId INT = NULL,
@PordIPrdCode NVARCHAR(50) = NULL,
@PordIQuantity FLOAT,
@PordIPrice Numeric(18, 2) = NULL,
@PordITotalPrice Numeric(18, 2) = NULL,
@PordIDescription NVARCHAR(4000) = NULL,
@PordIQuantityDesc NVARCHAR(1000) = NULL,
@PordIDiscount Numeric(18, 2) = NULL,
@PordIDiscountI Numeric(18, 2) = NULL,
@PordIDiscountPercent Numeric(18, 2) = NULL,
@PordIDiscountIPercent Numeric(18, 2) = NULL,
@PordIPrdUnitId INT = NULL,
@PordICurrencyUnitId INT = NULL,
@PordIOrderView INT = NULL,
@PordIFCR FLOAT = NULL,
@PordITax Numeric(18, 2) = NULL,
@PordITaxIndex Numeric(18, 2) = NULL,
@PordICalculateTax BIT = NULL,
@PordINotVisible BIT = NULL,
@PordIFCRI FLOAT = NULL,
@PordINotInSum BIT = NULL,
@PordIWHId INT = NULL,
@PordIRound Numeric(18, 2) = NULL,
@PordIRoundPlus Numeric(18, 2) = NULL,
@PordIPrdPriceGroupId INT = NULL,
/*RZ990818- Add sum of tax,discount,price before tax*/
@PordITracing NVARCHAR(200) = NULL,
/*RZ990818- End*/
/**/
@UserId INT,
@Month1 SMALLINT,
@RegisterDate DATETIME,
@RegisterDatePersian NVARCHAR(8),
@AppType VARCHAR(20),
@LanguageID INT = NULL, 
/**/
@InsertedPreOrderItemId INT OUTPUT,
@ErrorMessage NVARCHAR(MAX) OUTPUT,
/*NKR14020727- Start*/
@PordIValidityExpireDate DATETIME = NULL,
@PordIValidityExpireDatePersian NVARCHAR(8) = NULL,
@PordIDeliveryTermId INT = NULL,
@PordIDeliveryDate DATETIME = NULL,
@PordIDeliveryDatePersian NVARCHAR(8) = NULL,
@PordIDeliveryTime NVARCHAR(10) = NULL,
@PordIDeliveryDay NVARCHAR(20) = NULL,
@PordIDeliveryEndDate DATETIME = NULL,
@PordIDeliveryEndDatePersian NVARCHAR(8) = NULL,
@PordIDeliveryEndTime NVARCHAR(10) = NULL,
@PordIDeliveryEndDay NVARCHAR(20) = NULL
/*NKR14020727- End*/
)
AS
BEGIN
/**/
/********************************************************************************/
/****************************** TRANSACTION *************************************/
/********************************************************************************/
BEGIN TRANSACTION Main
/**/
/********************************************************************************/
/**********************************Default LanguageID ***************************/
DECLARE @DefaultLanguageID INT 
SET @DefaultLanguageID = (SELECT TOP 1 [DefaultLanguageID] FROM [dbo].[CrmsysSetting])

/********************************************************************************/
/************************************ Year1 => FPId *****************************/
DECLARE @FPId INT
SET @FPId = (SELECT TOP 1 [ID] FROM [dbo].[WSFinancialPeriod] ORDER BY [ID] DESC)
/********************************************************************************/
/****************************** Error & Variables *******************************/
DECLARE @ErrorFrom VARCHAR(50), @ErrorNumber INT
/********************************************************************************/
/********************************* Check fields *********************************/
/********************************************************************************/
/*PreOrder*/
IF(ISNULL(@PreOrderId,0) > 0)
BEGIN
	SET @PreOrderId = (SELECT TOP 1 [PreOrderID] FROM [dbo].[WSPreOrder] WHERE [PreOrderID] = @PreOrderId)
END
ELSE IF (ISNULL(@PordNumber,0) <> 0)
BEGIN
	SET @PreOrderId = (SELECT TOP 1 [PreOrderID] FROM [dbo].[WSPreOrder] WHERE [pordNumber] = @PordNumber)
END
IF(ISNULL(@PreOrderId,0) <= 0)
	SET @ErrorMessage = @ErrorMessage + 'No PordId|'
/*Product*/
IF(@PordIPrdId IS NOT NULL AND @PordIPrdId > 0)
BEGIN
	SET @PordIPrdId = (SELECT TOP 1 [ProductID] FROM [dbo].[WSProducts] WHERE [ProductID] = @PordIPrdId)
END
ELSE IF (ISNULL(@PordIPrdCode,'') <> '')
BEGIN
	SET @PordIPrdId = (SELECT TOP 1 [ProductID] FROM [dbo].[WSProducts] WHERE [PrdCode] = @PordIPrdCode)
END
IF(ISNULL(@PordIPrdId,0) <= 0)
	SET @ErrorMessage = @ErrorMessage + 'No PrdId|'
/*PordIPrdUnitId*/
IF(ISNULL(@PordIPrdUnitId,0) > 0)
BEGIN
	IF((SELECT TOP 1 [ProductUnitID] FROM [dbo].[WSProductUnits] WHERE [ProductUnitID] = @PordIPrdUnitId) IS NULL)
		SET @ErrorMessage = @ErrorMessage + 'No PordIPrdUnitId|'
END
/*PordICurrencyUnitId*/
IF(ISNULL(@PordICurrencyUnitId,0) > 0)
BEGIN
	IF((SELECT TOP 1 [CurrencyUnitID] FROM [dbo].[WSCurrencyUnits] WHERE [CurrencyUnitID] = @PordICurrencyUnitId) IS NULL)
		SET @ErrorMessage = @ErrorMessage + 'No PordICurrencyUnitId|'
END
/*PordIWHId*/
IF(ISNULL(@PordIWHId,0) > 0)
BEGIN
	IF((SELECT TOP 1 [WareHouseID] FROM [dbo].[WsWareHouse] WHERE [WareHouseID] = @PordIWHId) IS NULL)
		SET @ErrorMessage = @ErrorMessage + 'No PordIWHId|'
END
ELSE 
	SET @PordIWHId = (SELECT TOP 1 [WareHouseID] FROM [dbo].[WsWareHouse] WHERE ISNULL([WrhsDeActive],0) = 1)
/*PordIPrdPriceGroupId*/
IF(ISNULL(@PordIPrdPriceGroupId,0) > 0)
BEGIN
	IF((SELECT TOP 1 [ProductPriceGroupID] FROM [dbo].[WSProductPriceGroup] WHERE [ProductPriceGroupID] = @PordIPrdPriceGroupId) IS NULL)
		SET @ErrorMessage = @ErrorMessage + 'No PordIPrdPriceGroupId|'
END
ELSE 
BEGIN
/**/
	SET @PordIPrdPriceGroupId = (SELECT TOP 1 ProductPriceGroupId 
												FROM dbo.WSProductPrice PP
												WHERE PP.PrdId = @PordIPrdId AND PP.Price = @PordIPrice)
	IF(ISNULL(@PordIPrdPriceGroupId,0) = 0)
	BEGIN
	/**/
		SET @PordIPrdPriceGroupId = (SELECT TOP 1 ProductPriceGroupId 
									FROM dbo.WSProductPriceGroup PP
									ORDER BY PP.OrderView)													
	/**/
	END
/**/
END

/*RZ990520- Add check pordIsFixed*/
DECLARE @IsFixed BIT
SET @IsFixed = (SELECT TOP 1 pordIsFixed FROM dbo.WSPreOrder WHERE PreOrderID = @PreOrderId)
IF(ISNULL(@IsFixed,0) = 1)
BEGIN
	SET @ErrorMessage = @ErrorMessage + 'IsFixed|'
END
/*RZ990520- End*/
/********************************************************************************/
/******************** Insert PreOrder Items *************************************/
/********************************************************************************/
BEGIN TRY  
    IF(@ErrorMessage IS NULL OR @ErrorMessage = '')/*No Error*/
	BEGIN		
		/*======================== Order Item ==========================*/		
		BEGIN TRAN
			/*=============== Discount =================*/
			IF(ISNULL(@PordIDiscount,0) = 0 AND ISNULL(@PordIDiscountPercent,0) > 0 AND ISNULL(@PordIPrice,0) > 0)
			BEGIN
				SET @PordIDiscount = (ISNULL(@PordIPrice,0) * ISNULL(@PordIDiscountPercent,0)) / 100
			END	
			IF(ISNULL(@PordIDiscount,0) > 0 AND ISNULL(@PordIDiscountPercent,0) = 0 AND ISNULL(@PordIPrice,0) > 0)
			BEGIN
				SET @PordIDiscountPercent = (ISNULL(@PordIDiscount,0) * 100) / ISNULL(@PordIPrice,0)
			END						
			/*=============== Calculate ================*/
			DECLARE @TmpI NUMERIC(18,2) = 0, @TmpII NUMERIC(18,2) = 0, 
			@TotalPrice NUMERIC(18,2) = 0
			/*Tmp = Quantity * FCR * FCRI*/
			SET @TmpI = @PordIQuantity * ISNULL(@PordIFCR,1) * ISNULL(@PordIFCRI,1)
			/*TempII = price - discount - round + roundPlus*/
			SET @TmpII = ISNULL(@PordIPrice,0) - ISNULL(@PordIDiscount,0) - ISNULL(@PordIRound,0) + ISNULL(@PordIRoundPlus,0)/*- 0 - 0 + 0 */

			/*=============== Tax =================*/
			/*RZ990818*/
			IF(ISNULL(@PordITax,0) = 0 AND ISNULL(@PordITaxIndex,0) > 0 AND ISNULL(@TmpII,0) > 0)
			BEGIN
				SET @PordITax = (ISNULL(@TmpII,0) * ISNULL(@PordITaxIndex,0)) / 100
			END	
			IF(ISNULL(@PordITax,0) > 0 AND ISNULL(@PordITaxIndex,0) = 0 AND ISNULL(@TmpII,0) > 0)
			BEGIN
				SET @PordITaxIndex = (ISNULL(@PordITax,0) * 100) / ISNULL(@TmpII,0)
			END			
			/*RZ990818-End*/
			/*===================================*/
			/*TotalPrice = (@TmpI * @TmpII) + (@TmpI * tax)*/
			SET @TotalPrice = (@TmpI * @TmpII) + (@TmpI * ISNULL(@PordITax,0))/*RZ990818*/
			IF(@pordITotalPrice IS NULL )
				SET @pordITotalPrice = @TotalPrice
			/**********************************************************/				
			/*Set OrderView*/
			IF(@PordIOrderView IS NULL)
			BEGIN
				DECLARE @ItemCount INT
				SET @ItemCount = (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM [dbo].[WSPreOrderItems] OI WHERE OI.PreOrderId = @PreOrderId)
				SET @PordIOrderView = (@ItemCount + 1) + (@ItemCount * 4 ) - 1		
			END
			/**********************************************************/
			/*RZ990818- Add sum of tax,discount,price before tax*/
			DECLARE @PordIPriceSum Numeric(18, 2) = 0,
			@PordIDiscountSum Numeric(18, 2) = 0,			
			@PordITaxSum Numeric(18, 2) = 0

			SET @PordIPriceSum = (@TmpI * @TmpII)
			SET @PordIDiscountSum = ISNULL(@PordIDiscount,0) * @TmpI
			SET @PordITaxSum = ISNULL(@PordITax,0) * @TmpI
			/*RZ990818- End*/			
			/**********************************************************/
			/*Main table*/
			INSERT INTO [dbo].[WSPreOrderItems]
				([PreOrderId]
				,[pordIPrdId]
				,[pordIQuantity]
				,[pordIPrice]
				,[pordITotalPrice]
				,[pordIDescription]
				,[UserId]
				,[FPId]
				,[Month1]
				,[RegisterDate]
				,[RegisterDatePersian]
				,[pordIQuantityDesc]
				,[pordIDiscount]
				,[pordIDiscountI]
				,[pordIDiscountPercent]
				,[pordIDiscountIPercent]
				,[pordIPrdUnitId]
				,[pordICurrencyUnitId]
				,[pordIOrderView]
				,[pordIFCR]
				,[pordITax]
				,[pordITaxIndex]
				,[pordICalculateTax]
				,[pordINotVisible]
				,[pordIFCRI]
				,[pordINotInSum]
				,[pordIWHId]
				,[pordIRound]
				,[AppType]
				,[LanguageID]
				,[FirstRegisterUserId]
				,[FirstRegisterDatePersian]
				,[FirstRegisterDate]
				,[pordIRoundPlus]
				,[pordIPrdPriceGroupID]
				/*RZ990818- Add sum of tax,discount,price before tax*/
				,[pordITracing]
				,[pordIPriceSum]
				,[pordIDiscountSum]				
				,[pordITaxSum]
				/*RZ990818- End*/
				/*NKR14020727- Start*/
				,[pordIValidityExpireDate]
				,[pordIValidityExpireDatePersian]
				,[pordIDeliveryTermId]
				,[pordIDeliveryDate]
				,[pordIDeliveryDatePersian]
				,[pordIDeliveryTime]
				,[pordIDeliveryDay]
				,[pordIDeliveryEndDate]
				,[pordIDeliveryEndDatePersian]
				,[pordIDeliveryEndTime]
				,[pordIDeliveryEndDay]
				/*NKR14020727- End*/)
			VALUES
				(@PreOrderId
				,@PordIPrdId
				,@PordIQuantity
				,@PordIPrice
				,@PordITotalPrice
				,@PordIDescription
				,@UserId
				,@FPId
				,@Month1
				,@RegisterDate
				,@RegisterDatePersian
				,@PordIQuantityDesc
				,@PordIDiscount
				,@PordIDiscountI
				,@PordIDiscountPercent
				,@PordIDiscountIPercent
				,@PordIPrdUnitId
				,@PordICurrencyUnitId
				,@PordIOrderView
				,@PordIFCR
				,@PordITax
				,@PordITaxIndex
				,@PordICalculateTax
				,@PordINotVisible
				,@PordIFCRI
				,@PordINotInSum
				,@PordIWHId
				,@PordIRound
				,@AppType
				,@DefaultLanguageID
				,@UserId
				,@RegisterDatePersian
				,@RegisterDate
				,@PordIRoundPlus
				,@PordIPrdPriceGroupID
				/*RZ990818- Add sum of tax,discount,price before tax*/
				,@PordITracing
				,@pordIPriceSum
				,@pordIDiscountSum				
				,@pordITaxSum
				/*RZ990818- End*/
				/*NKR14020727- Start*/
				,@PordIValidityExpireDate
				,@PordIValidityExpireDatePersian
				,@PordIDeliveryTermId
				,@PordIDeliveryDate
				,@PordIDeliveryDatePersian
				,@PordIDeliveryTime
				,@PordIDeliveryDay
				,@PordIDeliveryEndDate
				,@PordIDeliveryEndDatePersian
				,@PordIDeliveryEndTime
				,@PordIDeliveryEndDay
				/*NKR14020727- End*/)
			/**/
			SET @InsertedPreOrderItemId = SCOPE_IDENTITY()			
			/*********** Insert to lang table ***********/
			INSERT INTO [dbo].[WSPreOrderItems_Lang]
				([PreOrderItemID]
				,[LanguageID]
				,[pordIDescription]
				,[pordIQuantityDesc])
			SELECT
				 @InsertedPreOrderItemId
				,LN.LanguageID
				,@PordIDescription
				,@PordIQuantityDesc
			FROM dbo.Languages LN 
			WHERE LN.LanguageID <> @DefaultLanguageID	
			AND ISNULL(LN.IsActive,0) = 1

		IF (@@ERROR > 0)
		BEGIN SET @ErrorFrom='Error In Insert PreOrderItem' SET @ErrorNumber=@@ERROR ROLLBACK TRAN END		
		ELSE 
		COMMIT TRAN 				
		/*==================== PreOrderItemSpecification ===============*/
		EXEC [dbo].[ApispInsertPreOrderItemSpecificationAll]
		@PrdId = @PordIPrdId,
		@PreOrderItemId = @InsertedPreOrderItemId,
		@UserId = @UserId,
		@Month1 = @Month1,
		@FPId = @FPId,
		@RegisterDatePersian = @RegisterDatePersian,
		@AppType = @AppType,
		@LanguageID = @DefaultLanguageID						
		/*=========================== Calculate PreOrder ====================*/		
		EXEC [dbo].[ApispCalcAndUpdatePreOrderAmounts] 	@PreOrderId, @Month1, @LanguageID, @DefaultLanguageID				
		/**/
	END
	/**/	
	COMMIT TRANSACTION Main 
	SELECT 1

END TRY  
BEGIN CATCH  
	 /*RAISERROR('Error From TransAction %S', 16, 1, @ErrorFrom)*/
	 ROLLBACK TRANSACTION Main
     SET @InsertedPreOrderItemId = 0	 
	 SET @ErrorMessage = 'Error|'
	 IF(ISNULL(@ErrorFrom,'') <> '')
		SET @ErrorMessage = @ErrorMessage + @ErrorFrom
 	 SELECT 0	 
END CATCH  	
/********************************************************************************/
/**/
END

(

	/*

		exec [dbo].[ApispUpdateCompany]
		@CompanyID = 6983,

		@cmpName = NULL,

		@cmpfaxDesc =NULL,
		@cmpCountryId = NULL,

		@cmpCityId  = NULL,

		@cmpZipCode = NULL,
		@cmpPostBox  = NULL,
		@cmpWebSite = NULL,

		@cmpSpecialPoint  = NULL,
		@cmpDescription = NULL,
		@cmpUserName = NULL,
		@cmpPassword  = NULL,       
		@cmpRegistrationNumber = NULL,

		@cmpNationalCode = NULL,

		 select @ErrorMessage

BEGIN
/**/

	/*RZ14011018- End*/

	BEGIN TRANSACTION Main


	DECLARE @ErrorFrom VARCHAR(50), @ErrorNumber INT

	/*========================== Start Checks ==========================*/


	BEGIN /* ChkDupUName */
		DECLARE @CompanyIdForCheckUName INT 
		SET @CompanyIdForCheckUName = (SELECT TOP 1 C.[CompanyID] FROM [dbo].[CrmCompany] C 
						WHERE C.[cmpUserName] = @CmpUserName AND CompanyID <> @CompanyID)/*RZ990512*/
		IF(@CompanyIdForCheckUName IS NOT NULL)
			SET @ErrorMessage = @ErrorMessage + 'DupUName=1|'
	END

	/*========== Checking ==========*/

	BEGIN /* ChkDupCmpCode */
		IF(@CmpCode IS NOT NULL AND @CmpCode <> '' AND ISNULL(@CmpCodeNotDup,0) = 1 )
		BEGIN
			DECLARE @CmpCodeFound INT
			SET @CmpCodeFound = (SELECT TOP 1 cmpNumber FROM dbo.CrmCompany C WHERE cmpCode = @cmpCode AND CompanyID <> @CompanyID)/*RZ990512*/
			IF(@CmpCodeFound IS NOT NULL)
				SET @ErrorMessage = @ErrorMessage + 'DupCmpCode=' + CAST(@CmpCodeFound AS NVARCHAR(200)) + '|'
		END
	END

	/*========== Checking ==========*/

	BEGIN /* Make A Table For check duplicate */
		DECLARE @TmpTableForCheckDuplicate TABLE (
			[CompanyID] INT,	
			[cmpId] INT,	
			[cmpName] NVARCHAR(MAX),
			[Title] NVARCHAR(MAX),	
			[CompanyWithTitle] NVARCHAR(MAX),	
			[cmpTel] NVARCHAR(50),
			[cmpTelII] NVARCHAR(50),	
			[cmpFax] NVARCHAR(50),

			[prsTelII] NVARCHAR(50),
			[prsFax] NVARCHAR(50),
			[prsMobile] NVARCHAR(50),
			[prsEmail] NVARCHAR(300),
			[prsEmailII] NVARCHAR(300),
			[cmpNumber] INT,	
			[ReferUser] NVARCHAR(MAX)			
		)
		IF(@CmpName IS NOT NULL AND @CmpName <> '' AND @CmpName <> @PersonLabel)
		BEGIN
			DECLARE @DupCompanyId INT , @DupCmpTitle NVARCHAR(MAX), @DupCmpName NVARCHAR(MAX), @DupCmpCity NVARCHAR(MAX), @DupCmpNumber INT
			, @TitleId INT , @CityId INT
			SELECT @TitleId = CASE WHEN @CmpTitleId IS NULL THEN 0 ELSE @CmpTitleId END
			SELECT @CityId = CASE WHEN @CmpCityId IS NULL THEN 0 ELSE @CmpCityId END
			/**/
			INSERT INTO @TmpTableForCheckDuplicate 
			EXEC [dbo].[ApispCheckDuplicateCompany]
					@Type  = 1, 
					@Id = @CompanyID, /*RZ990512*/
					@Val = @CmpName,

				@DupCmpTitle = [Title], 
				@DupCmpName = [cmpName], 
				@DupCmpCity = [City], 
				@DupCmpNumber = [cmpNumber]
			FROM @TmpTableForCheckDuplicate	 
		
			IF(@DupCompanyId IS NOT NULL)
			BEGIN
				SET @ErrorMessage = @ErrorMessage + 'DupCmp=' + CAST(@DupCmpNumber AS NVARCHAR(200)) + '|'		
			END			
		END
	END

	/*========== Checking ==========*/

	BEGIN /* No CmpTitleId */
		IF(@CmpTitleId IS NOT NULL AND @CmpTitleId > 0)
		BEGIN	
			IF((SELECT TOP 1 [CompanyTitleID] FROM [dbo].[CrmCompanyTitle] WHERE [CompanyTitleID] = @CmpTitleId) IS NULL)
				SET @ErrorMessage = @ErrorMessage + 'No CmpTitleId|'
		END
	END

	/*========== Checking ==========*/

	BEGIN /* No CmpGroupId */
		IF(@CmpGroupId IS NOT NULL AND @CmpGroupId > 0)

			IF((SELECT TOP 1 [CompanySubGroupID] FROM [dbo].[CrmCompanySubGroups] WHERE [CompanySubGroupID] = @CmpSubGroupId) IS NULL)
				SET @ErrorMessage = @ErrorMessage + 'No CmpSubGroupId|'

				IF((SELECT TOP 1 [CompanySubGroupID] FROM [dbo].[CrmCompanySubGroups]

					SET @ErrorMessage = @ErrorMessage + 'No CmpSubGroupId|'

			IF((SELECT TOP 1 [ProvinceID] FROM [dbo].[Province] WHERE [ProvinceID] = @CmpProvinceId) IS NULL)

	/*========== Checking ==========*/

	BEGIN /* No CmpCityId */

		/*RZ990511*/
		IF(@cmpMainPersonId IS NOT NULL AND @cmpMainPersonId > 0)

	/*RZ14010913- Insert Campaign*/

	BEGIN
	/**/
		SET @CompanyCampaignId = (SELECT TOP (1) [CompanyCampaignId]    
								  FROM [dbo].[CrmCompanyCampaign]
								  WHERE (REPLACE(REPLACE(REPLACE([CampaignName] ,NCHAR(1740), NCHAR(1610)),NCHAR(1603),NCHAR(1705)),NCHAR(1609),NCHAR(1740)))
										= 
										(REPLACE(REPLACE(REPLACE(@CmpCampaignName ,NCHAR(1740), NCHAR(1610)),NCHAR(1603),NCHAR(1705)),NCHAR(1609),NCHAR(1740)))
								  )
		IF(ISNULL(@CompanyCampaignId,0) = 0)
		BEGIN
		/**/
			SET @CampaignCount = (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM [dbo].[CrmCompanyCampaign])
			SET @CampaignOrderView = (@CampaignCount + 1) + (@CampaignCount * 9 ) - 1		

			INSERT INTO [dbo].[CrmCompanyCampaign]
				([ParentId]
				,[ParentPath]
				,[CampaignName]
				,[CampaignDepth]
				,[CampaignIsLeaf]
				,[OrderView]

				,[Year1]
				,[Month1]
				,[RegisterDate]
				,[RegisterDatePersian]			
				,[AppType]
				,[LanguageID])
			VALUES
				(0
				,'0'
				,@CmpCampaignName
				,0
				,1
				,@CampaignOrderView
				,@UserId
				,@RegisterDate
				,@RegisterDatePersian
				,@UserId
				,@Year1
				,@Month1
				,@RegisterDate
				,@RegisterDatePersian			
				,@AppType
				,@DefaultLanguageID)

			SET @CompanyCampaignId = SCOPE_IDENTITY()
			/*********** Insert to lang table ***********/
			IF(ISNULL(@CompanyCampaignId,0) <> 0)
			BEGIN
			/**/
				INSERT INTO [dbo].[CrmCompanyCampaign_Lang]
					([CompanyCampaignId]
					,[LanguageID]
					,[CampaignName])
				SELECT
					 @CompanyCampaignId
					 ,LN.LanguageID

				AND ISNULL(LN.IsActive,0) = 1

			/**/

		IF(ISNULL(@CompanyCampaignId,0) <> 0)

		/**/
			IF(ISNULL(@CmpCampaignIds,'') <> '')
					SET @CmpCampaignIds = ISNULL(@CmpCampaignIds,'') + ','

		/*-----------------------------------------------*/
	/**/
	END
	/*RZ14011222- End*/
	/*========================= Start Update =========================*/

	IF(@ErrorMessage IS NULL OR @ErrorMessage = '')
	BEGIN
		/**/
		BEGIN TRAN
		/**/
			DECLARE @QRY NVARCHAR(MAX) = '', @Columns NVARCHAR(MAX) = '', @Conditions NVARCHAR(MAX) = '',
			@NumberIsNULL INT = -9999, @StringISNill NVARCHAR(50) = N'NULL'
			
			SET @QRY =	' UPDATE[dbo].[CrmCompany]
							SET '
					
					/* Add Column */

					IF (@cmpNumber IS NOT NULL)
						IF (@cmpNumber <> @NumberIsNULL)
						BEGIN
							SET @Columns = @Columns + ' [cmpNumber] = ' + CAST(@cmpNumber AS NVARCHAR(50)) + ' , '
						END
						ELSE
						BEGIN
							SET @Columns = @Columns + ' [cmpNumber] = NULL , '
						END


					/* Add Column */

					IF (@cmpNameEng IS NOT NULL)
						IF (UPPER(LTRIM(RTRIM(@cmpNameEng))) <> @StringISNill)
						BEGIN
							SET @Columns = @Columns + ' [cmpNameEng] = ''' + @cmpNameEng + ''' , '
						END
						ELSE
						BEGIN
							SET @Columns = @Columns + ' [cmpNameEng] = NULL , '
						END


					/* Add Column */


					IF (@cmpTitleId IS NOT NULL)
						IF (@cmpTitleId <> @NumberIsNULL)
						BEGIN	
							SET @Columns = @Columns + ' [cmpTitleId] = ' + CAST(@cmpTitleId AS NVARCHAR(50)) + ' , '
						END
						ELSE
						BEGIN
							SET @Columns = @Columns + ' [cmpTitleId] = NULL , '
						END


					/* Add Column */


					IF (@cmpGroupId IS NOT NULL)
						IF (@cmpGroupId <> @NumberIsNULL)
						BEGIN	
							SET @Columns = @Columns + ' [cmpGroupId] = ' + CAST(@cmpGroupId AS NVARCHAR(50)) + ' , '
						END
						ELSE
						BEGIN
							SET @Columns = @Columns + ' [cmpGroupId] = NULL , '
						END


					/* Add Column */


					IF (@cmpSubGroupId IS NOT NULL)
						IF (@cmpSubGroupId <> @NumberIsNULL)
						BEGIN	
							SET @Columns = @Columns + ' [cmpSubGroupId] = ' + CAST(@cmpSubGroupId AS NVARCHAR(50)) + ' , '
						END
						ELSE
						BEGIN
							SET @Columns = @Columns + ' [cmpSubGroupId] = NULL , '

							SET @Columns = @Columns + ' [cmpGroupIdII] = ' + CAST(@cmpGroupIdII AS NVARCHAR(50)) + ' , '

							SET @Columns = @Columns + ' [cmpGroupIdII] = NULL , '
						END

						END

							SET @Columns = @Columns + ' [cmpGroupIdIII] = NULL , '

							SET @Columns = @Columns + ' [cmpTypeId] = ' + CAST(@cmpTypeId AS NVARCHAR(50)) + ' , '
						END
						ELSE

							SET @Columns = @Columns + ' [cmpTypeId] = NULL , '

					IF (@cmpAddressDesc IS NOT NULL)
						IF (UPPER(LTRIM(RTRIM(@cmpAddressDesc))) <> @StringISNill)
						BEGIN	
							SET @Columns = @Columns + ' , [cmpAddressDesc] = ''' + @cmpAddressDesc + ''' '
						END
						ELSE
						BEGIN
							SET @Columns = @Columns + ' , [cmpAddressDesc] = NULL '
						END


					/* Add Column */

					IF (@cmpInteresting IS NOT NULL)
						IF (UPPER(LTRIM(RTRIM(@cmpInteresting))) <> @StringISNill)
						BEGIN

						END
						ELSE

							SET @Columns = @Columns + ' , [cmpInteresting] = NULL '
						END

						END

						END

						END
						ELSE
						BEGIN
							SET @Columns = @Columns + ' , [cmpInquiry] = NULL '
						END


					WHILE (substring(LTRIM(@Columns), 0, 2) = ',')
						SET @Columns = LTRIM(substring(LTRIM(@Columns), 2, len(@Columns)));		

					SET @Conditions =	' WHERE CompanyID = ' + CAST(@CompanyID AS NVARCHAR(50)) + 
						' AND LanguageID = ' + CAST(@LanguageID AS NVARCHAR(50))

					EXECUTE(@QRY + @Columns + @Conditions)

					END

					SET @CmpChangeHistoryDesc = (SELECT TOP(1) [Desc] FROM @CmpChangeHistoryDescTbl_Lang T WHERE T.LanguageID = @DefaultLanguageID)					
					SET @CmpChangeHistoryDesc = REPLACE((REPLACE((REPLACE(@CmpChangeHistoryDesc,N'{0}',@OldValueName)),N'{1}',@NewValueName)),N'{2}',@UserName)	
										
					SET @CmpChangeHistory_Lang = ISNULL('[' + ((STUFF((SELECT N',{' + 
														N'"LanguageID":' + CAST(L.[LanguageID] AS NVARCHAR(100)) +
														N',"OldValue":"' + ISNULL(@OldValue,'') + N'"' +
														N',"NewValue":"' + ISNULL(@NewValue,'') + N'"' +																
														N',"Desc":"' +
															ISNULL((REPLACE((ISNULL(REPLACE((REPLACE((SELECT TOP(1) [Desc] FROM @CmpChangeHistoryDescTbl_Lang T WHERE T.LanguageID = L.[LanguageID]),N'{0}',																		
																	ISNULL((CASE WHEN ISNULL(@oldcmpCampaignIds,'') = ''
																			THEN (SELECT TOP(1) [Txt] FROM @CmpChangeHistoryDescNoValTbl_Lang T WHERE T.LanguageID = L.[LanguageID])
																			ELSE (
																				(ISNULL((STUFF((SELECT ',' + ISNULL(CCI.[CampaignName], '') 
																					  FROM  [dbo].[CrmCompanyCampaign_Lang] CCI
																					  WHERE CCI.[CompanyCampaignId] IN(SELECT CAST(ISNULL(splitdata,0) AS INT) FROM [dbo].[fnSplitString](@oldcmpCampaignIds,','))  							
																					  AND CCI.LanguageID = L.[LanguageID]
																					  FOR XML PATH(''), TYPE).value(N'(./text())[1]', N'NVARCHAR(MAX)'), 1, 1, '')),'') )
																			
																			)																		
																	END),'')
																	))																																				
																	,N'{1}',																		
																	ISNULL((CASE WHEN ISNULL(@cmpCampaignIds_ChgHis,'') = ''
																			THEN (SELECT TOP(1) [Txt] FROM @CmpChangeHistoryDescNoValTbl_Lang T WHERE T.LanguageID = L.[LanguageID])
																			ELSE (
																				(ISNULL((STUFF((SELECT ',' + ISNULL(CCI.[CampaignName], '') 
																					  FROM  [dbo].[CrmCompanyCampaign_Lang] CCI
																					  WHERE CCI.[CompanyCampaignId] IN(SELECT CAST(ISNULL(splitdata,0) AS INT) FROM [dbo].[fnSplitString](@cmpCampaignIds_ChgHis,','))  							
																					  AND CCI.LanguageID = L.[LanguageID]
																					  FOR XML PATH(''), TYPE).value(N'(./text())[1]', N'NVARCHAR(MAX)'), 1, 1, '')),'') )
																			)																		
																	END),'')
																		
																	),'')
																												
															),N'{2}',
															ISNULL((SELECT TOP(1) ISNULL(FName,'') + ' ' + ISNULL(LName,'') FROM [dbo].[Users_Lang] U WHERE U.UserId = @UserId AND U.LanguageID = L.[LanguageID]),'')
															)																												
															),'') +																												
													+ N'"}'					
													FROM [dbo].[Languages] L
													WHERE ISNULL(L.IsActive,0) = 1	
													AND L.[LanguageID] <> @DefaultLanguageID
													FOR XML PATH(''), TYPE).value(N'(./text())[1]', N'NVARCHAR(MAX)'), 1, 1, ''))				
										+ ']'),'')		
												   
					/*-----------*/
					EXEC [dbo].[ApispInsertCompanyChangeHistory]
					@CompanyId = @CompanyId,
					@FieldName = @FieldName,
					@OldValue = @OldValue,
					@NewValue = @NewValue,
					@Desc = @CmpChangeHistoryDesc, 
					@CmpChangeHistory_Lang = @CmpChangeHistory_Lang, 	
					@UserId = @UserId,
					@RegisterDate = @RegisterDate,
					@RegisterDatePersian = @RegisterDatePersian,
					@AppType = @AppType,
					@DefaultLanguageID = @DefaultLanguageID,		
					@InsertedId = @InsertedCmpChangeHistoryId OUTPUT

				/**/
				END
				/*-----------------------------------------------*/
			/**/
			END			
			/*RZ14011222- End*/
								 	 	 
		/**/
		IF (@@ERROR > 0)
		BEGIN SET @ErrorFrom='Error In Update Company' SET @ErrorNumber=@@ERROR ROLLBACK TRAN END		
		ELSE 
		COMMIT TRAN 	
	END
	/**/
	/**/
	IF (@ErrorNumber > 0)
	BEGIN 
		RAISERROR('Error From TransAction %S', 16, 1, @ErrorFrom)
		ROLLBACK TRANSACTION Main
 		SELECT 0
	END 
	ELSE
	BEGIN
		COMMIT TRANSACTION Main 
		SELECT 1
	END	

	/*========================== End Update ==========================*/
/**/
END

			END

			/*======================= End Add Columns =======================*/
			/*=================== End Multi Language [en] ===================*/
			

			IF (@@ERROR > 0)
				BEGIN SET @ErrorFrom='Error In Update Order' SET @ErrorNumber=@@ERROR ROLLBACK TRAN END		
			ELSE

	END TRY

	END CATCH  




	/*=================== End Update ===================*/


	/**/
END

/*NKR990513*/

CREATE PROCEDURE [dbo].[ApispUpdateOrderItems]
(
	/*
		declare @ErrorMessage NVARCHAR(MAX) = ''
		exec [dbo].[ApispUpdateOrderItems]
		@OrderItemID = 1452,

		@ordIDiscountIPercent = NULL,
		@ordIPrdUnitId  = NULL,
		@ordICurrencyUnitId  = NULL,
		@ordIOrderView  = NULL,
		@ordIFCR  = NULL,
		@ordITax  = NULL,
		@ordITaxIndex  = NULL,
		@ordICalculateTax  = NULL,
		@ordINotVisible  = NULL,

		@ordIPricePartII  = NULL,
		@ordIPrdPriceGroupID  = NULL,
		@ordITracing  = NULL,
		/**/
		@AppType = 'dasti',
		@Month1 = 5,
		@UserId = 3,
		@RegisterDate = '2020-08-06',
		@RegisterDatePersian = '13990516',
		@LanguageID = 1065,
		@DefaultLanguageID = 1065,
		@ErrorMessage = @ErrorMessage OUTPUT
		select @ErrorMessage

	*/
	@OrderItemID INT,
	@OrderId INT = NULL,
	@ordIPrdId INT = NULL,
	@ordIQuantity FLOAT = NULL,
	@ordIPrice NUMERIC(18,2) = NULL,
	@ordITotalPrice NUMERIC(18,2) = NULL,
	@ordIDescription NVARCHAR(4000) = NULL,
	@ordIQuantityDesc NVARCHAR(1000) = NULL,
	@ordIDiscountI NUMERIC(18,2) = NULL,
	@ordIDiscount NUMERIC(18,2) = NULL,
	@ordIDiscountPercent NUMERIC(18,2) = NULL,
	@ordIDiscountIPercent NUMERIC(18,2) = NULL,
	@ordIPrdUnitId INT = NULL,
	@ordICurrencyUnitId INT = NULL,
	@ordIOrderView INT = NULL,
	@ordIFCR FLOAT = NULL,
	@ordITax NUMERIC(18,2) = NULL,
	@ordITaxIndex NUMERIC(18,2) = NULL,
	@ordICalculateTax BIT = NULL,
	@ordINotVisible BIT = NULL,
	@ordIFCRI FLOAT = NULL,
	@ordINotInSum BIT = NULL,
	@ordIWHId INT = NULL,
	@ordIRound NUMERIC(18,2) = NULL,
	@ordIRoundPlus NUMERIC(18,2) = NULL,
	@ordIPricePartRate FLOAT = NULL,
	@ordIPricePartI NUMERIC(18,2) = NULL,
	@ordIPricePartII NUMERIC(18,2) = NULL,
	@ordIPrdPriceGroupID INT = NULL,
	@ordITracing NVARCHAR(200) = NULL,
	/**/
	@AppType VARCHAR(20),
	@Month1 SMALLINT,
	@UserId INT,
	@RegisterDate DATETIME,
	@RegisterDatePersian NVARCHAR(8),
	@LanguageID INT,
	@DefaultLanguageID INT,
	@ErrorMessage NVARCHAR(MAX) OUTPUT,
	/*RZ14010926- Add OrdIValidityExpireDate*/
	@OrdIValidityExpireDate DATETIME = NULL,
	@OrdIValidityExpireDatePersian NVARCHAR(8) = NULL,
	@OrdIValidityExpireDay INT = NULL,
	@OrdIValidityExpireHour INT = NULL,
	@OrdIValidityExpireMinute INT = NULL,
	/*RZ14010926- End*/
	/*NKR14020727- Start*/
	@OrdIPrdValidityCountingMin INT = NULL,
	@OrdIPrdValidityCountingNum INT = NULL,
	@OrdIDeliveryTermId INT = NULL,
	@OrdIDeliveryDate DATETIME = NULL,
	@OrdIDeliveryDatePersian NVARCHAR(8) = NULL,
	@OrdIDeliveryTime NVARCHAR(10) = NULL,
	@OrdIDeliveryDay NVARCHAR(20) = NULL,
	@OrdIDeliveryEndDate DATETIME = NULL,
	@OrdIDeliveryEndDatePersian NVARCHAR(8) = NULL,
	@OrdIDeliveryEndTime NVARCHAR(10) = NULL,
	@OrdIDeliveryEndDay NVARCHAR(20) = NULL,
	@OrdIDeliveryUserId INT = NULL
	/*NKR14020727- End*/
)
AS
BEGIN
	/**/
	
	BEGIN TRANSACTION Main
	DECLARE @ErrorFrom VARCHAR(50), @ErrorNumber INT
	
	/********************************************************************************/
	/************************************ Year1 => FPId *****************************/
	DECLARE @FPId INT
	SET @FPId = (SELECT TOP 1 [ID] FROM [dbo].[WSFinancialPeriod] ORDER BY [ID] DESC)

	/*================================== Start Checks ==================================*/
	/*RZ990516- Check isFixed*/
	DECLARE @IsFixed BIT
	SET @IsFixed = (SELECT TOP 1 ordIsFixed FROM dbo.WSOrder WHERE OrderID = 
							(SELECT TOP 1 OrderId FROM dbo.WSOrderItems WHERE OrderItemID = @OrderItemID))
	IF(ISNULL(@IsFixed,0) = 1)
	BEGIN
		SET @ErrorMessage = @ErrorMessage + 'IsFixed|'
	END
	/*RZ990516- End*/
	/*=================================================*/


	BEGIN/*No OrdId*/
		IF(@OrderId IS NOT NULL AND @OrderId > 0)/*RZ990516*/
		BEGIN
			IF((SELECT TOP 1 [OrderID] FROM [dbo].[WSOrder] WHERE [OrderID] = @OrderId) IS NULL)
				SET @ErrorMessage = @ErrorMessage + 'No OrdId|'
		END		
	END


	/*========== Checking ==========*/


	BEGIN/*No PrdId*/
		IF(@OrdIPrdId IS NOT NULL AND @OrdIPrdId > 0)/*RZ990516*/
		BEGIN
			IF((SELECT TOP 1 [ProductID] FROM [dbo].[WSProducts] WHERE [ProductID] = @OrdIPrdId) IS NULL)
				SET @ErrorMessage = @ErrorMessage + 'No PrdId|'
		END					
	END


	/*========== Checking ==========*/


	BEGIN/*No OrdIPrdUnitId*/
		IF(@OrdIPrdUnitId IS NOT NULL AND @OrdIPrdUnitId > 0)
		BEGIN
			IF((SELECT TOP 1 [ProductUnitID] FROM [dbo].[WSProductUnits] WHERE [ProductUnitID] = @OrdIPrdUnitId) IS NULL)
				SET @ErrorMessage = @ErrorMessage + 'No OrdIPrdUnitId|'
		END
	END


	/*========== Checking ==========*/


	BEGIN/*No OrdICurrencyUnitId*/
		IF(@OrdICurrencyUnitId IS NOT NULL AND @OrdICurrencyUnitId > 0)
		BEGIN
			IF((SELECT TOP 1 [CurrencyUnitID] FROM [dbo].[WSCurrencyUnits] WHERE [CurrencyUnitID] = @OrdICurrencyUnitId) IS NULL)
				SET @ErrorMessage = @ErrorMessage + 'No OrdICurrencyUnitId|'
		END
	END


	/*========== Checking ==========*/


	BEGIN/*No OrdIWHId*/
		IF(@OrdIWHId IS NOT NULL AND @OrdIWHId > 0)
		BEGIN
			IF((SELECT TOP 1 [WareHouseID] FROM [dbo].[WsWareHouse] WHERE [WareHouseID] = @OrdIWHId) IS NULL)
				SET @ErrorMessage = @ErrorMessage + 'No OrdIWHId|'
		END
		/*ELSE 
			SET @OrdIWHId = (SELECT TOP 1 [WareHouseID] FROM [dbo].[WsWareHouse] WHERE ISNULL([WrhsDeActive],0) = 1)	*/
	END


	/*========== Checking ==========*/
	
	BEGIN/*No OrdIPrdPriceGroupID*/
		IF(ISNULL(@OrdIPrdPriceGroupID,0) > 0)
		BEGIN
			IF((SELECT TOP 1 [ProductPriceGroupID] FROM [dbo].[WSProductPriceGroup] WHERE [ProductPriceGroupID] = @OrdIPrdPriceGroupID) IS NULL)
				SET @ErrorMessage = @ErrorMessage + 'No OrdIPrdPriceGroupID|'
		END
		ELSE 
		BEGIN
		/**/
			SET @OrdIPrdPriceGroupID = (SELECT TOP 1 ProductPriceGroupId 
														FROM dbo.WSProductPrice PP
														WHERE PP.PrdId = @OrdIPrdId AND PP.Price = @OrdIPrice)
			IF(ISNULL(@OrdIPrdPriceGroupID,0) = 0)
			BEGIN
			/**/
				SET @OrdIPrdPriceGroupID = (SELECT TOP 1 ProductPriceGroupId 
											FROM dbo.WSProductPriceGroup PP
											ORDER BY PP.OrderView)													
			/**/
			END
		/**/
		END
	END
	/*=====================================================*/
	/*RZ14010926- Add OrdIValidityExpireDate*/
	DECLARE @OrdDateTime DATETIME = NULL
	IF(@OrdIValidityExpireDate IS NULL)
	BEGIN
	/**/
		IF(@OrdIValidityExpireDay IS NOT NULL OR @OrdIValidityExpireHour IS NOT NULL OR @OrdIValidityExpireMinute IS NOT NULL)
		BEGIN
		/**/	
			IF(@OrderId IS NOT NULL)
			BEGIN
				SET @OrdDateTime = (SELECT TOP(1) ordDateTime FROM [dbo].[WSOrder] WHERE OrderID = @OrderId)
			END
			ELSE 
			BEGIN

		END
	/**/

	/**/
	END	
	/*RZ14010926- End*/
	/*NKR14020730- Start*/

					SET @Columns = @Columns + ' ordIValidityExpireDate = ''' + CAST(@OrdIValidityExpireDate AS NVARCHAR(50)) + ''' , '

					SET @Columns = @Columns + ' ordIValidityExpireDatePersian = NULL , '
					SET @Columns = @Columns + ' ordIValidityExpireDate = NULL , '
				END
				/*RZ14010926- End*/

				/*NKR14020730- Start*/
				IF (@OrdIPrdValidityCountingMin IS NOT NULL)
					IF (@OrdIPrdValidityCountingMin <> @NumberIsNULL)
					BEGIN
						SET @Columns = @Columns + ' ordIPrdValidityCountingMin = ' + CAST(@OrdIPrdValidityCountingMin AS NVARCHAR(50)) + ' , '
					END
					ELSE

					END

					END

				IF (@OrdIDeliveryTermId IS NOT NULL)
					IF (@OrdIDeliveryTermId <> @NumberIsNULL)
					BEGIN
						SET @Columns = @Columns + ' ordIDeliveryTermId = ' + CAST(@OrdIDeliveryTermId AS NVARCHAR(50)) + ' , '

						SET @Columns = @Columns + ' ordIDeliveryTermId = NULL , '

				IF (@OrdIDeliveryDatePersian IS NOT NULL)

					SET @Columns = @Columns + ' ordIDeliveryDate = NULL , '

				BEGIN

			/*=================== Start Multi Language [en] ===================*/

			IF (@LanguageID IS NOT NULL AND @LanguageID <> @DefaultLanguageID)
			BEGIN

				SET @QRY = ''
				SET @Columns = ''
				SET @Conditions = ''

				SET @QRY = ' UPDATE WSOrderItems_Lang 
							SET '
				
			
				/* Add Columns */


				IF (@ordIDescription IS NOT NULL)
					IF (UPPER(LTRIM(RTRIM(@ordIDescription))) <> @StringISNill)
					BEGIN
						SET @Columns = @Columns + ' , ordIDescription = ''' + @ordIDescription + ''' '
					END
					ELSE
					BEGIN
						SET @Columns = @Columns + ' , ordIDescription = NULL '
					END


				/* Add Columns */


				IF (@ordIQuantityDesc IS NOT NULL)
					IF (UPPER(LTRIM(RTRIM(@ordIQuantityDesc))) <> @StringISNill)
					BEGIN
						SET @Columns = @Columns + ' , ordIQuantityDesc = ''' + @ordIQuantityDesc + ''' '
					END
					ELSE
					BEGIN
						SET @Columns = @Columns + ' , ordIQuantityDesc = NULL '
					END


				/* Add Columns */


				WHILE (substring(LTRIM(@Columns), 0, 2) = ',')
					SET @Columns = LTRIM(substring(LTRIM(@Columns), 2, len(@Columns)));		

				SET @Conditions = ' WHERE OrderItemID = ' + CAST(@OrderItemID AS NVARCHAR(50)) + ' AND LanguageID = ' + CAST(@LanguageID AS NVARCHAR(50))


				IF (LTRIM(RTRIM(@Columns)) <> '')
					EXECUTE (@QRY + @Columns + @Conditions)
			END

			




			/*======================= End Add Columns =======================*/
			/*=================== End Multi Language [en] ===================*/
			/*========================= Calculate ===========================*/
			/*RZ990516- Modify this sp*/
			SELECT 
				 @OrdIQuantity = [ordIQuantity]
				,@OrdIPrice = [ordIPrice]
				,@OrdIDiscount = [ordIDiscount]
				,@OrdIDiscountPercent = [ordIDiscountPercent]
				,@OrdIFCR = [ordIFCR]
				,@OrdITax = [ordITax]
				,@OrdIFCRI = [ordIFCRI]
				,@OrdIRound = [ordIRound]
				,@OrdIRoundPlus = [ordIRoundPlus]			  
			FROM [dbo].[WSOrderItems]
			WHERE [OrderItemID] = @OrderItemID
			/*=============== Discount =================*/
			IF(ISNULL(@OrdIDiscount,0) = 0 AND ISNULL(@OrdIDiscountPercent,0) > 0 AND ISNULL(@OrdIPrice,0) > 0)
			BEGIN
				SET @OrdIDiscount = (ISNULL(@OrdIPrice,0) * ISNULL(@OrdIDiscountPercent,0)) / 100
			END	
			IF(ISNULL(@OrdIDiscount,0) > 0 AND ISNULL(@OrdIDiscountPercent,0) = 0 AND ISNULL(@OrdIPrice,0) > 0)
			BEGIN
				SET @OrdIDiscountPercent = (ISNULL(@OrdIDiscount,0) * 100) / ISNULL(@OrdIPrice,0)
			END	
			/*=============== Calculate ================*/
			DECLARE @TmpI NUMERIC(18,2) = 0, @TmpII NUMERIC(18,2) = 0, 
			@TotalPrice NUMERIC(18,2) = 0
			/*Tmp = Quantity * FCR * FCRI*//*FCRI =1 */
			SET @TmpI = @OrdIQuantity * ISNULL(@OrdIFCR,1) * ISNULL(@OrdIFCRI,1)/** 1 */
			/*TempII = price - discount - round + roundPlus*/
			SET @TmpII = ISNULL(@OrdIPrice,0)/*- 0 - 0 + 0 */
			SET @TmpII = ISNULL(@OrdIPrice,0) - ISNULL(@OrdIDiscount,0) - ISNULL(@OrdIRound,0) + ISNULL(@OrdIRoundPlus,0)/*- 0 - 0 + 0 */
			/*TotalPrice = (@TmpI * @TmpII) + (@TmpI * tax)*/
			SET @TotalPrice = (@TmpI * @TmpII) + (@TmpI * ISNULL(@OrdITax,0))
			IF(@OrdITotalPrice IS NULL )
				SET @OrdITotalPrice = @TotalPrice

			/*RZ990818- Add sum of tax,discount,price before tax*/	
			DECLARE @OrdIPriceSum NUMERIC(18,2) = 0, 
			@OrdIDiscountSum NUMERIC(18,2) = 0, 
			@OrdITaxSum NUMERIC(18,2) = 0
			SET @OrdIPriceSum = (@TmpI * @TmpII)
			SET @OrdIDiscountSum = ISNULL(@OrdIDiscount,0) * @TmpI	
			SET @OrdITaxSum = ISNULL(@OrdITax,0) * @TmpI			
			/*RZ990818- End*/
			/**********************************************************/	
			UPDATE [dbo].[WSOrderItems] 
			SET [ordITotalPrice] = @OrdITotalPrice	,
				[ordIDiscount] = @OrdIDiscount,
				[ordIDiscountPercent] = @OrdIDiscountPercent,
				/*RZ990818- Add sum of tax,discount,price before tax*/	
				[ordIPriceSum] = @OrdIPriceSum,
				[ordIDiscountSum] = @OrdIDiscountSum,				
				[ordITaxSum] = @OrdITaxSum
				/*RZ990818- End*/				 
			WHERE [OrderItemID] = @OrderItemID
			/*=========================== Calculate Order ====================*/
			/*RZ990516- Modify this sp*/
			SET @OrderId = (SELECT TOP 1 OrderId FROM dbo.WSOrderItems WHERE OrderItemId = @OrderItemID)
			IF(ISNULL(@OrderId,0) > 0)
				EXEC [dbo].[ApispCalcAndUpdateOrderAmounts] @OrderId, @Month1, @LanguageID, @DefaultLanguageID	
			/*RZ990516-End*/
			/*==============================================================*/
			IF (@@ERROR > 0)
				BEGIN SET @ErrorFrom='Error In Update WSOrderItems' SET @ErrorNumber=@@ERROR ROLLBACK TRAN END		
			ELSE 
				COMMIT TRAN 				

			/**/
		END
		/**/	
		COMMIT TRANSACTION Main 
		SELECT 1

	END TRY  
	BEGIN CATCH  
		 ROLLBACK TRANSACTION Main
		 SET @ErrorMessage = 'Error|'
		 IF(@ErrorFrom IS NOT NULL AND @ErrorFrom <> '')
			SET @ErrorMessage = @ErrorMessage + @ErrorFrom
 		 SELECT 0 
	END CATCH 
	/*=================================== End Update ===================================*/
/**/
END

			@pordcmpTitle ='pordcmpTitle',

			@pordExpireDateTime = NULL,

			@RegisterDate = '2020-08-06',
			@RegisterDatePersian ='13990516',
			@AppType ='dasti',
			@LanguageID = 1065,
			@DefaultLanguageID = 1065,
			@ErrorMessage = @ErrorMessage OUTPUT
			select @ErrorMessage
	*/
	@PreOrderID INT,
	@pordTitle NVARCHAR(1000) = NULL,
	@pordNumber BIGINT = NULL,
	@pordDate NVARCHAR(8) = NULL,
	@pordDateTime DATETIME = NULL,
	@pordCompanyId INT = NULL,
	@pordCompanyPersonId INT = NULL,

	@pordCalculateTax BIT = NULL,
	@pordTotalAmount NUMERIC(18,2) = NULL,
	@pordTotalAmountStr NVARCHAR(1000) = NULL,
	@pordIsVerified BIT = NULL,
	@pordVerifierId INT = NULL,
	@pordVerifierRequestId INT = NULL,
	@pordVerifiedRegisterDate DATETIME = NULL,
	@pordVerifiedRegisterDatePersian NVARCHAR(8) = NULL,
	@pordStatusId INT = NULL,
	@pordIsFixed BIT = NULL,
	@pordGuarantyId INT = NULL,
	@pordPayConditionId INT = NULL,
	@pordDeliveryTimeId INT = NULL,
	@pordCreditDurationId INT = NULL,
	@pordSpecialWord NVARCHAR(1000) = NULL,
	@pordDescriptionI NVARCHAR(4000) = NULL,
	@pordDescriptionII NVARCHAR(4000) = NULL,
	@pordDescriptionId INT = NULL,
	@pordPrintFormatId INT = NULL,
	@pordRLogoId INT = NULL,
	@pordLLogoId INT = NULL,
	@pordSigningI NVARCHAR(1000) = NULL,
	@pordSigningII NVARCHAR(1000) = NULL,
	@pordSigningIII NVARCHAR(1000) = NULL,
	@pordAccountNumberI NVARCHAR(1000) = NULL,
	@pordAccountShabaNumberI NVARCHAR(1000) = NULL,
	@pordAccountNumberII NVARCHAR(1000)  = NULL,
	@pordAccountShabaNumberII NVARCHAR(1000) = NULL,
	@pordAddress NVARCHAR(1000) = NULL,
	@pordTelephone NVARCHAR(1000) = NULL,
	@pordFax NVARCHAR(1000) = NULL,
	@pordWebSite NVARCHAR(1000) = NULL,
	@pordEmail NVARCHAR(1000) = NULL,
	@pordSignatureId INT = NULL,
	@pordDay NVARCHAR(50) = NULL,
	@pordTime NVARCHAR(50) = NULL,
	@pordDiscountTitle NVARCHAR(1000) = NULL,
	@pordDiscountPercent NUMERIC(18,2) = NULL,
	@pordTransportation NUMERIC(18,2) = NULL,
	@pordReferUserId INT = NULL,
	@pordRound NUMERIC(18,2) = NULL,
	@pordcmpTitle NVARCHAR(1000) = NULL,	
	@pordDeliveryDate NVARCHAR(8) = NULL,
	@pordDeliveryDateTime DATETIME = NULL,
	@pordDeliveryTime NVARCHAR(10) = NULL,
	@PreOrderOprID INT = NULL,	
	@pordVerificationWorkFlowId INT = NULL,
	@pordExpireDate VARCHAR(8) = NULL,
	@pordExpireDateTime DATETIME = NULL,
	/**/
	@UserId INT,
	@Month1 SMALLINT,
	@RegisterDate DATETIME,
	@RegisterDatePersian NVARCHAR(8),
	@AppType VARCHAR(20),
	@LanguageID INT,
	@DefaultLanguageID INT,
	@ErrorMessage NVARCHAR(MAX) OUTPUT,

	/*NKR14020729- Start*/
	@PordDeliveryTermId INT = NULL,
	@PordDeliveryEndDate NVARCHAR(8) = NULL,
	@PordDeliveryEndDateTime DATETIME = NULL,
	@PordDeliveryEndTime NVARCHAR(10) = NULL,
	@PordDeliveryDay NVARCHAR(20) = NULL,
	@PordDeliveryEndDay NVARCHAR(20) = NULL
	/*NKR14020729- End*/
)
AS
BEGIN
	/**/
	
	BEGIN TRANSACTION Main

	DECLARE @ErrorFrom VARCHAR(50), @ErrorNumber INT


	/********************************************************************************/
	/************************************ Year1 => FPId *****************************/
	DECLARE @FPId INT
	SET @FPId = (SELECT TOP 1 [ID] FROM [dbo].[WSFinancialPeriod] ORDER BY [ID] DESC)


	/*===================== Start Checks =====================*/
	/*RZ990516- Check isFixed*/
	DECLARE @IsFixed BIT
	SET @IsFixed = (SELECT TOP 1 pordIsFixed FROM dbo.WSPreOrder WHERE PreOrderID = @PreOrderID)
	IF(ISNULL(@IsFixed,0) = 1)
	BEGIN
		SET @ErrorMessage = @ErrorMessage + 'IsFixed|'
	END
	/*RZ990516- End*/

	/*========================================================*/
	BEGIN/*No PordVerifierId*/
		IF(ISNULL(@PordVerifierId,0) > 0)
		BEGIN
			IF((SELECT TOP 1 [UserId] FROM [dbo].[Users] WHERE [UserId] = @PordVerifierId) IS NULL)
				SET @ErrorMessage = @ErrorMessage + 'No PordVerifierId|'
		END
	END


	/*========== Checking ==========*/
	
	BEGIN/*No PordVerifierRequestId*/
		IF(ISNULL(@PordVerifierRequestId,0) > 0)

		END

		IF(ISNULL(@PordStatusId ,0) > 0)

				SET @ErrorMessage = @ErrorMessage + 'No PordStatusId|'

			SET @PordStatusId = 1*/
	END

	/*========== Checking ==========*/
	
	/*====================== End Checks ======================*/

	/*===================== Start Update =====================*/
	
	IF(ISNULL(@ErrorMessage,'') = '')/*No Error*/
	BEGIN
		/**/
		BEGIN TRAN
				
			DECLARE @QRY NVARCHAR(MAX) = '', @Columns NVARCHAR(MAX) = '', @Conditions NVARCHAR(MAX) = '',
			@NumberIsNULL INT = -9999, @NumericIsNULL NUMERIC(18,2) = -9999, @StringISNill NVARCHAR(50) = N'NULL'

				BEGIN
					SET @Columns = @Columns + ' pordCompanyId = NULL , '

	END /* END = IF(ISNULL(@ErrorMessage,'') = '') */
	IF (@ErrorNumber > 0)
	BEGIN 
		RAISERROR('Error From TransAction %S', 16, 1, @ErrorFrom)
		ROLLBACK TRANSACTION Main
 		SELECT 0
	END 
	ELSE
	BEGIN
		COMMIT TRANSACTION Main 
		SELECT 1
	END	


	/**/
END

				/* Add Columns */


				IF (@pordIQuantityDesc IS NOT NULL)
					IF (UPPER(LTRIM(RTRIM(@pordIQuantityDesc))) <> @StringISNill)
					BEGIN
						SET @Columns = @Columns + ' , pordIQuantityDesc = ''' + @pordIQuantityDesc + ''' '
					END
					ELSE
					BEGIN
						SET @Columns = @Columns + ' , pordIQuantityDesc = NULL '
					END



				SET @Conditions = ' WHERE PreOrderItemID = ' + CAST(@PreOrderItemID AS NVARCHAR(50)) +
					' AND LanguageID = ' + CAST(@LanguageID AS NVARCHAR(50))

				WHILE (substring(LTRIM(@Columns), 0, 2) = ',')
					SET @Columns = LTRIM(substring(LTRIM(@Columns), 2, len(@Columns)));		


				EXECUTE (@QRY + @Columns + @Conditions)

				
			END

			/*================= End Multi Language [en] =================*/
			/*======================== Calculate =============================*/
			/*RZ990516- Modify this sp*/
				SELECT 
					@PordIQuantity = [pordIQuantity]
					,@PordIPrice = [pordIPrice]
					,@PordIDiscount = [pordIDiscount]
					,@PordIDiscountPercent = [pordIDiscountPercent]
					,@PordIFCR = [pordIFCR]
					,@pordITax = [pordITax]					  					  
					,@PordIFCRI = [pordIFCRI]					  
					,@PordIRoundPlus = [pordIRoundPlus]		
					,@PordIRound = [pordIRound]	
					,@PreOrderId = [PreOrderId]		  
				FROM [dbo].[WSPreOrderItems] 
				WHERE [PreOrderItemID] = @PreOrderItemID
			/*=============== Discount =================*/
			IF(ISNULL(@PordIDiscount,0) = 0 AND ISNULL(@PordIDiscountPercent,0) > 0 AND ISNULL(@PordIPrice,0) > 0)
			BEGIN
				SET @PordIDiscount = (ISNULL(@PordIPrice,0) * ISNULL(@PordIDiscountPercent,0)) / 100
			END	
			IF(ISNULL(@PordIDiscount,0) > 0 AND ISNULL(@PordIDiscountPercent,0) = 0 AND ISNULL(@PordIPrice,0) > 0)
			BEGIN
				SET @PordIDiscountPercent = (ISNULL(@PordIDiscount,0) * 100) / ISNULL(@PordIPrice,0)
			END			
			/*=============== Calculate ================*/
			DECLARE @TmpI NUMERIC(18,2) = 0, @TmpII NUMERIC(18,2) = 0, 
			@TotalPrice NUMERIC(18,2) = 0
			/*Tmp = Quantity * FCR * FCRI*/
			SET @TmpI = @PordIQuantity * ISNULL(@PordIFCR,1) * ISNULL(@PordIFCRI,1)
			/*TempII = price - discount - round + roundPlus*/
			SET @TmpII = ISNULL(@PordIPrice,0) - ISNULL(@PordIDiscount,0) - ISNULL(@PordIRound,0) + ISNULL(@PordIRoundPlus,0)/*- 0 - 0 + 0 */
			/*TotalPrice = (@TmpI * @TmpII) + (@TmpI * tax)*/
			SET @TotalPrice = (@TmpI * @TmpII) + (@TmpI * ISNULL(@pordITax,0))
			IF(@pordITotalPrice IS NULL )
				SET @pordITotalPrice = @TotalPrice
			/*RZ990818- Add sum of tax,discount,price before tax*/	
			DECLARE @PordIPriceSum NUMERIC(18,2) = 0, 
			@PordIDiscountSum NUMERIC(18,2) = 0, 
			@PordITaxSum NUMERIC(18,2) = 0
			SET @PordIPriceSum = (@TmpI * @TmpII)
			SET @PordIDiscountSum = ISNULL(@PordIDiscount,0) * @TmpI	
			SET @PordITaxSum = ISNULL(@PordITax,0) * @TmpI			
			/*RZ990818- End*/
			/**********************************************************/
			UPDATE [dbo].[WSPreOrderItems] 
			SET [pordITotalPrice] =@pordITotalPrice,
				[pordIDiscount] = @PordIDiscount,
				[pordIDiscountPercent] = @PordIDiscountPercent,				
				/*RZ990818- Add sum of tax,discount,price before tax*/	
				[pordIPriceSum] = @PordIPriceSum,
				[pordIDiscountSum] = @PordIDiscountSum,				
				[pordITaxSum] = @PordITaxSum
				/*RZ990818- End*/						  				   
			WHERE [PreOrderItemID] = @PreOrderItemID
			/*=========================== Calculate PreOrder ====================*/		
			IF(ISNULL(@PreOrderId,0) > 0)
				EXEC [dbo].[ApispCalcAndUpdatePreOrderAmounts] 	@PreOrderId, @Month1, @LanguageID, @DefaultLanguageID
			/*RZ990516- End*/

			/*============================================================*/

		END	
		IF (@@ERROR > 0)
		BEGIN 
			SET @ErrorFrom='Error In Update PreOrderItems' SET @ErrorNumber=@@ERROR ROLLBACK TRAN END		
		ELSE 
			COMMIT TRAN 	
	/*================ End Update ================*/

	
	IF (@ErrorNumber > 0)
	BEGIN 
		RAISERROR('Error From TransAction %S', 16, 1, @ErrorFrom)
		ROLLBACK TRANSACTION Main
 		SELECT 0
	END 
	ELSE
	BEGIN
		COMMIT TRANSACTION Main 
		SELECT 1
	END	

/**/
END

SET @CurrencyUnitID = (SELECT TOP 1 [CurrencyUnitID] 
					   FROM [dbo].[WSCurrencyUnits] 
					   WHERE (REPLACE(REPLACE(REPLACE(CAST([CurrencyUnit] AS NVARCHAR(2000)) ,
								NCHAR(1740), NCHAR(1610)),NCHAR(1603),NCHAR(1705)),NCHAR(1609),NCHAR(1740))) = 
								(REPLACE(REPLACE(REPLACE(CAST(N'ریال' AS NVARCHAR(2000)) ,
								NCHAR(1740), NCHAR(1610)),NCHAR(1603),NCHAR(1705)),NCHAR(1609),NCHAR(1740))))*/
/************************************ Current Year ******************************/
DECLARE @CurrYear SMALLINT
SET @CurrYear = CAST(SUBSTRING(@RegisterDatePersian, 1, 4) AS SMALLINT)		
DECLARE @RegisterTime NVARCHAR(8)
SET @RegisterTime = (CONVERT(CHAR(8), @RegisterDate, 108))				
/************************************ Error *************************************/
DECLARE @ErrorFrom VARCHAR(50), @ErrorNumber INT
DECLARE @PreOrderIdInError INT,
		@PordItemIdsInError NVARCHAR(1000),
		@PordItemSpcfIdsInError NVARCHAR(1000) ,
		@PordPaymentIdsInError NVARCHAR(1000)				
SET @PreOrderIdInError = 0
SET @PordItemIdsInError = ''
SET @PordItemSpcfIdsInError = ''
SET @PordPaymentIdsInError = ''
/************************ Make A Table For PordItemList *************************/
DECLARE @PordItemTmpTable TABLE (
	[PreOrderItemID] INT,
	[pordIPreOrderId] INT,
	[pordIPrdId] INT,
	[pordIQuantity] FLOAT,
	[pordIPrice] NUMERIC(18,2),
	[pordITotalPrice] NUMERIC(18,2),
	[pordIDescription] NVARCHAR(4000),
	[pordIQuantityDesc] NVARCHAR(1000),

	[pordITaxIndex] NUMERIC(18,2),

	[pordINotInSum] BIT,

)
/************************ Make A Table For PordItemSpcfList **************************/
DECLARE @PordItemSpcfTmpTable TABLE (
	[PreOrderItemSpcflID] INT,
	[pordISpcfPreOrderItemId] INT,
	[Specification] NVARCHAR(300),

/************************ Make A Table For PordPaymentList *********************/
DECLARE @PordPaymentTmpTable TABLE (
	[PreOrderPaymentId] INT,
	[pordpayImportDate] NVARCHAR(8),
	[pordpayImportDateTime] DATETIME,
	[pordpayTypeId] INT,
	[pordpayPreOrderId] INT,
	[pordpayDocId] INT,
	[pordpayDocTypeId] INT,
	[pordpayAmount] NUMERIC(18, 2),
	[pordpayDocNumber] NVARCHAR(50),
	[pordpayDocDate] NVARCHAR(8),
	[pordpayDocDateTime] DATETIME,
	[pordpayDocAmount] NUMERIC(18, 2),
	[pordpayDescription] NVARCHAR(2000),
	[pordpayFirstRegisterUserId] INT,
	[pordpayFirstRegisteDatePersian] NVARCHAR(8),
	[pordpayFirstRegisteDate] DATETIME,
	[pordpayFirstRegisterTime] VARCHAR(10),
	/*AI14020403 - Add*/
	[pordpayCompanyMoneyBagId] INT,
	[pordpayDeActive] BIT,
	[pordpayAmountTemp] NUMERIC(18, 2),
	[pordpayFileTypeJson] NVARCHAR(MAX),
	/*AI14020403*/
	[pordpayAccountNumber] NVARCHAR(50), /*AI40120701*/
	/*GHO14030210 - Add firld to payment*/
	[pordpayDocDateTemp] NVARCHAR(8),
	[pordpayDocDateTimeTemp] DATETIME
	/*GHO14030210 - End*/
)    
/********************************************************************************/
/***************************** Split fields *************************************/
/********************************************************************************/
/********************************************************************************/
/****************************** PreOrder ****************************************/
/********************************************************************************/
BEGIN TRAN
/**/
DECLARE @PordField NVARCHAR(MAX) = NULL
WHILE LEN(@PreOrder) > 0
BEGIN
    IF PATINDEX('%|#|%', @PreOrder) > 0
    BEGIN
        SET @PordField = SUBSTRING(@PreOrder,
                                    0,
                                    PATINDEX('%|#|%', @PreOrder))
        /*--------------------------------------------------------------------------------*/
        /*Get parameter name and split value form parameter*/
        /*For example => pordTitle=102 => pordTitle= => pordTitle => Compare => value*/
        DECLARE @PordFieldValue NVARCHAR(MAX)
        DECLARE @PordFieldName NVARCHAR(200)
        SET @PordFieldName = SUBSTRING(@PordField, 1, CHARINDEX('=',@PordField)-1)                                    
		SET @PordFieldValue = SUBSTRING(@PordField, CHARINDEX('=', @PordField)+1, Len(@PordField))
        /*--------------------------------------------------------------------------------*/
        /*---------------------------- BEGIN Fill variabele ------------------------------*/
        /*BEGIN PREORDER*/
        IF @PordFieldName = 'PreOrderID'
        BEGIN
			SELECT @PreOrderID = CASE WHEN @PordFieldValue = 'null' THEN NULL ELSE CAST(@PordFieldValue AS INT) END
			SET @PreOrderIdInError =  @PreOrderID /*====> For rollback when have error*/
        END                                                                
        /*--------------------------*/
        IF @PordFieldName = 'pordTitle'
        BEGIN
			SET @pordTitle = CASE WHEN @PordFieldValue = '' OR @PordFieldValue = 'null' THEN NULL ELSE @PordFieldValue END		
        END
        /*--------------------------*/
        IF @PordFieldName = 'pordDate'
        BEGIN
			SET @pordDate = CASE WHEN @PordFieldValue = '' OR @PordFieldValue = 'null' THEN NULL ELSE REPLACE(@PordFieldValue, '/', '') END
			SET @pordDateTime = CASE WHEN @pordDate IS NULL THEN NULL ELSE dbo.fnPersianToGregorianDate(@pordDate, @RegisterTime) END		 			
        END
        /*--------------------------*/
		IF @PordFieldName = 'pordCompanyId'
        BEGIN
			SELECT @pordCompanyId = CASE WHEN @PordFieldValue = 'null' THEN NULL ELSE CAST(@PordFieldValue AS INT) END
        END
        /*--------------------------*/
		IF @PordFieldName = 'pordCompanyPersonId'
        BEGIN
			SELECT @pordCompanyPersonId = CASE WHEN @PordFieldValue = 'null' THEN NULL ELSE CAST(@PordFieldValue AS INT) END
        END
        /*--------------------------*/
        IF @PordFieldName = 'pordProjectId'
        BEGIN
			SELECT @pordProjectId = CASE WHEN @PordFieldValue = 'null' THEN NULL ELSE CAST(@PordFieldValue AS INT) END
        END
        /*--------------------------*/
		IF @PordFieldName = 'pordSubject'
        BEGIN
			SET @pordSubject = CASE WHEN @PordFieldValue = '' THEN NULL ELSE @PordFieldValue END
		END
        /*--------------------------*/
		IF @PordFieldName = 'pordItemCount'
		BEGIN
			SELECT @pordItemCount = CASE WHEN @PordFieldValue = '0.0' OR @PordFieldValue = 'null' THEN NULL ELSE CAST(@PordFieldValue AS INT) END
		END
		/*--------------------------*/
		IF @PordFieldName = 'pordItemsAmount'
		BEGIN
			SELECT @pordItemsAmount = CASE WHEN @PordFieldValue = '0.0' OR @PordFieldValue = 'null' THEN NULL ELSE CAST(@PordFieldValue AS NUMERIC(18,2)) END
		END
		/*--------------------------*/
		IF @PordFieldName = 'pordInstallAmount'
		BEGIN
			SELECT @pordInstallAmount = CASE WHEN @PordFieldValue = '0.0' OR @PordFieldValue = 'null' THEN NULL ELSE CAST(@PordFieldValue AS NUMERIC(18,2)) END
		END
		/*--------------------------*/
		IF @PordFieldName = 'pordDiscount'
		BEGIN

/************************** Set date time in PreOrder ************************/
IF(@pordTime IS NOT NULL)
BEGIN
	IF(@pordDate IS NOT NULL)
	BEGIN
		SET @pordDateTime = CASE WHEN @pordDate IS NULL THEN NULL ELSE dbo.fnPersianToGregorianDate(@pordDate, @pordTime) END			 	
	END
	
	IF(@pordImportDate IS NOT NULL)
	BEGIN
		SET @pordImportDateTime = CASE WHEN @pordImportDate IS NULL THEN NULL ELSE dbo.fnPersianToGregorianDate(@pordImportDate, @pordTime) END			 	
	END
	
	IF(@pordFirstRegisterDatePersian IS NOT NULL)
	BEGIN
		SET @pordFirstRegisterDate = CASE WHEN @pordFirstRegisterDatePersian IS NULL THEN NULL ELSE dbo.fnPersianToGregorianDate(@pordFirstRegisterDatePersian, @pordTime) END
	END	
END

WHILE LEN(@PordItemList) > 0 AND (@PordItemList IS NOT NULL)
BEGIN
	/*@PordItemList = N'pordIPrdId=7|#|pordIQuantity=1|#||^#^|pordIPrdId=3|#|pordIQuantity=2|#||^#^|'*/
    IF PATINDEX('%|^#^|%', @PordItemList) > 0
    BEGIN
        SET @PordIAllFields = SUBSTRING(@PordItemList,
                                    0,
                                    PATINDEX('%|^#^|%', @PordItemList))
        /*--------------------------------------------------------------------------------*/
        /*For example => @PordIAllFields = pordIPrdId=7|#|pordIQuantity=1|#|*/
        /*--------------------------------------------------------------------------------*/
        /*---------------------------- BEGIN Fill fields  --------------------------------*/
        /*BEGIN PORDITEM*/
        DECLARE @PItemAllFields NVARCHAR(MAX)
        SET @PItemAllFields = @PordIAllFields
        
        /****************  set null field **************/  
			SET @PreOrderItemID = NULL
			SET @pordIPreOrderId = NULL
			SET @pordIPrdId = NULL

			SET @pordIDeliveryDatePersian = NULL
			SET @pordIDeliveryDate = NULL
			SET @pordIDeliveryTime = NULL
			SET @pordICostSheetItemId = NULL
			/*AI991226 End*/
			SET @insertLinkedProducts = NULL /*AI14000204 - Add*/
			SET @pordIQuantityTotal = NULL /*AI14010606*/

			SET @pordIDeliveryEndDatePersian = NULL

			SET @pordIDeliveryEndDay = NULL
			SET @pordIValidityExpireDate = NULL

			/*AI14020710-End*/
        /**/

        WHILE LEN(@PItemAllFields) > 0
		BEGIN
			IF PATINDEX('%|#|%', @PItemAllFields) > 0
			BEGIN
				SET @PordIField = SUBSTRING(@PItemAllFields,
											0,
											PATINDEX('%|#|%', @PItemAllFields))
				/*--------------------------------------------------------------------------------*/
				/*Get parameter name and split value form parameter*/
				/*For example => pordIPrdId=7 => pordIPrdId= => pordIPrdId => Compare => value*/
				DECLARE @PordIFieldValue NVARCHAR(MAX)
				DECLARE @PordIFieldName NVARCHAR(200)
				SET @PordIFieldName = SUBSTRING(@PordIField, 1, CHARINDEX('=',@PordIField)-1)                                    
				SET @PordIFieldValue = SUBSTRING(@PordIField, CHARINDEX('=', @PordIField)+1, Len(@PordIField))
				/*--------------------------------------------------------------------------------*/
				/*---------------------------- BEGIN Fill variabele ------------------------------*/
				/*BEGIN PORDITEM*/
				/*--------------------------*/
				IF @PordIFieldName = 'PreOrderItemID'
				BEGIN
					SELECT @PreOrderItemID = CASE WHEN @PordIFieldValue = 'null' THEN NULL ELSE CAST(@PordIFieldValue AS INT) END
					SET @PordItemIdsInError = @PordItemIdsInError + CAST(@PreOrderItemID AS NVARCHAR(10)) + ',0|' /*rollback when have error*/					
				END
				/*--------------------------*/
				IF @PordIFieldName = 'pordIPreOrderId'
				BEGIN
					SELECT @pordIPreOrderId = CASE WHEN @PordIFieldValue = 'null' THEN NULL ELSE CAST(@PordIFieldValue AS INT) END
				END

				IF @PordIFieldName = 'pordIPrdId'
				BEGIN
					SELECT @pordIPrdId = CASE WHEN @PordIFieldValue = 'null' THEN NULL ELSE CAST(@PordIFieldValue AS INT) END
				END
				/*--------------------------*/
				IF @PordIFieldName = 'pordIQuantity'
				BEGIN
					SELECT @pordIQuantity = CASE WHEN @PordIFieldValue = '0.0' OR @PordIFieldValue = 'null' THEN NULL ELSE CAST(@PordIFieldValue AS FLOAT) END
				END
				/*--------------------------*/
				IF @PordIFieldName = 'pordIPrice'
				BEGIN
					SELECT @pordIPrice = CASE WHEN @PordIFieldValue = '0.0' OR @PordIFieldValue = 'null' THEN NULL ELSE CAST(@PordIFieldValue AS NUMERIC(18,2)) END
				END
				/*--------------------------*/
				IF @PordIFieldName = 'pordITotalPrice'
				BEGIN
					SELECT @pordITotalPrice = CASE WHEN @PordIFieldValue = '0.0' OR @PordIFieldValue = 'null' THEN NULL ELSE CAST(@PordIFieldValue AS NUMERIC(18,2)) END
				END
				/*--------------------------*/
				IF @PordIFieldName = 'pordIDescription'
				BEGIN
					SET @pordIDescription = CASE WHEN @PordIFieldValue = '' OR @PordIFieldValue = 'null' THEN NULL ELSE @PordIFieldValue END				
				END
				/*--------------------------*/
				IF @PordIFieldName = 'pordIQuantityDesc'
				BEGIN
					SET @pordIQuantityDesc = CASE WHEN @PordIFieldValue = '' OR @PordIFieldValue = 'null' THEN NULL ELSE @PordIFieldValue END				
				END
				/*--------------------------*/
				IF @PordIFieldName = 'pordIDiscount'
				BEGIN
					SELECT @pordIDiscount = CASE WHEN @PordIFieldValue = '0.0' OR @PordIFieldValue = 'null' THEN NULL ELSE CAST(@PordIFieldValue AS NUMERIC(18,2)) END
				END

				IF @PordIFieldName = 'pordIDiscountI'

					SELECT @pordIDiscountI = CASE WHEN @PordIFieldValue = '0.0' OR @PordIFieldValue = 'null' THEN NULL ELSE CAST(@PordIFieldValue AS NUMERIC(18,2)) END

				/*--------------------------*/
				IF @PordIFieldName = 'pordIPrdUnitId'
				BEGIN
					SELECT @pordIPrdUnitId = CASE WHEN @PordIFieldValue = 'null' THEN NULL ELSE CAST(@PordIFieldValue AS INT) END
				END

				IF @PordIFieldName = 'pordICurrencyUnitId'
