				END
				/*--------------------------*/
				IF @PordIFieldName = 'pordIOrderView'
				BEGIN
					SELECT @pordIOrderView = CASE WHEN @PordIFieldValue = 'null' THEN NULL ELSE CAST(@PordIFieldValue AS INT) END /*AI14010302*/
				END
				/*--------------------------*/
				IF @PordIFieldName = 'pordIFCR'
				BEGIN

				IF @PordIFieldName = 'pordITax'

				END
				/*--------------------------*/
				IF @PordIFieldName = 'pordITaxIndex'
				BEGIN
					SELECT @pordITaxIndex = CASE WHEN @PordIFieldValue = '0.0' OR @PordIFieldValue = 'null' THEN NULL ELSE CAST(@PordIFieldValue AS NUMERIC(18,2)) END
				END

				IF @PordIFieldName = 'pordICalculateTax'
				BEGIN
					SET @pordICalculateTax = CASE WHEN @PordIFieldValue = 'null' THEN NULL 
										WHEN @PordIFieldValue = 'false' THEN 0 
										ELSE 1 END	
				END
				/*--------------------------*/
				IF @PordIFieldName = 'pordINotVisible'

				IF @PordIFieldName = 'pordIFirstRegisterUserId'

				IF @PordIFieldName = 'pordIFirstRegisterDatePersian'

					SET @pordIFirstRegisterDate = CASE WHEN @pordIFirstRegisterDatePersian IS NULL THEN NULL ELSE dbo.fnPersianToGregorianDate(@pordIFirstRegisterDatePersian, @RegisterTime) END

				IF @PordIFieldName = 'pordIFirstRegisterTime'
				BEGIN
					SET @pordIFirstRegisterTime = CASE WHEN @PordIFieldValue = '' OR @PordIFieldValue = 'null' THEN NULL ELSE @PordIFieldValue END

				/*AI990210*/

				IF @PordIFieldName = 'pordITracing'

				END
				/*--------------------------*/
				IF @PordIFieldName = 'pordIDiscountSum'

					SELECT @pordIDiscountSum = CASE WHEN @PordIFieldValue = '0.0' OR @PordIFieldValue = 'null' THEN NULL ELSE CAST(@PordIFieldValue AS NUMERIC(18,2)) END/*AI991119*/

				IF @PordIFieldName = 'pordIPriceSum'

				/*--------------------------*/
				/*AI991226 - Add fields*/
				IF @PordIFieldName = 'pordIDeliveryTermId'

				/*--------------------------*/
				/*AI14000204 - Add*/
				IF @PordIFieldName = 'insertLinkedProducts'
				BEGIN
					SET @insertLinkedProducts = CASE WHEN @PordIFieldValue = 'null' THEN 0 
					WHEN @PordIFieldValue = 'false' THEN 0 
					ELSE 1 END
				END
				/*AI14000204 End*/
				/*AI991228 End*/
				/*--------------------------*/
				/*AI14010606*/
				IF @PordIFieldName = 'pordIQuantityTotal'

				/*--------------------------*/

				IF @PordIFieldName = 'pordIPrdNickname'

				/*AI14020710*/
				IF @PordIFieldName = 'pordIDeliveryDay'
				BEGIN
					SET @pordIDeliveryDay = CASE WHEN @PordIFieldValue = '' OR @PordIFieldValue = 'null' THEN NULL ELSE @PordIFieldValue END	
				END
				/*--------------------------*/
				IF @PordIFieldName = 'pordIDeliveryEndDate'
				BEGIN
					SET @pordIDeliveryEndDatePersian = CASE WHEN @PordIFieldValue = '' OR @PordIFieldValue = 'null' THEN NULL ELSE REPLACE(@PordIFieldValue, '/', '') END
					SET @pordIDeliveryEndDate = CASE WHEN @pordIDeliveryEndDatePersian IS NULL THEN NULL ELSE dbo.fnPersianToGregorianDate(@pordIDeliveryEndDatePersian, null) END		
				END
				/*--------------------------*/
				IF @PordIFieldName = 'pordIDeliveryEndTime'
				BEGIN
					SET @pordIDeliveryEndTime = CASE WHEN @PordIFieldValue = '' OR @PordIFieldValue = 'null' THEN NULL ELSE @PordIFieldValue END				
				END	
				/*--------------------------*/
				IF @PordIFieldName = 'pordIDeliveryEndDay'
				BEGIN

				/*END PORDITEM*/		
				/*END GENERAL*/
				/*---------------------------- END Fill variabele ---------------------------------*/
				/*---------------------------- Remove current parameter from listparam ------------*/
				SET @PItemAllFields = SUBSTRING(@PItemAllFields,
                                  LEN(@PordIField + '|#|') + 1,
                                  LEN(@PItemAllFields))                                                                                                      
			END
			ELSE
			BEGIN
				SET @PordIField = @PItemAllFields
				SET @PItemAllFields = NULL			
			END
		END  
		/************************** Set date time in PreOrderItem ************************/
		IF(@pordIFirstRegisterTime IS NOT NULL AND @pordIFirstRegisterDatePersian IS NOT NULL)
		BEGIN
			SET @pordIFirstRegisterDate = CASE WHEN @pordIFirstRegisterDatePersian IS NULL THEN NULL ELSE dbo.fnPersianToGregorianDate(@pordIFirstRegisterDatePersian, @pordIFirstRegisterTime) END

		INSERT INTO @PordItemTmpTable

					@pordITaxSum,/*AI991119*/

					/*AI991226*/
					@pordIDeliveryTermId,
					@pordIDeliveryDate,
					@pordIDeliveryDatePersian,
					@pordICostSheetItemId,
					/*AI991226 End*/
					@insertLinkedProducts, /*AI14000204 - Add*/
					@pordIQuantityTotal, /*AI14010606*/
					@pordIPrdColorId, /*AI14010614*/
					/*AI14010706*/
					@pordIPrdUnitIdI,
					@pordIPrdUnitIRateResult,
					/*AI14010706-End*/
					@pordIPrdNickname, /*AI14020119*/
					/*AI14020710*/
					@pordIDeliveryTime,
					@pordIDeliveryDay,
					@pordIDeliveryEndDate,
					@pordIDeliveryEndDatePersian,
					@pordIDeliveryEndTime,
					@pordIDeliveryEndDay,
					@pordIValidityExpireDate,
					@pordIValidityExpireDatePersian,
					@pordIPrdAutoCalcRate,
					@pordIDescriptionI
					/*AI14020710-End*/
				)                                     
		/*END PORDITEM*/		
        /*END GENERAL*/
        /*---------------------------- END Fill fields ------------------------------------*/
        /*---------------------------- Remove current parameter from listparam ------------*/
        SET @PordItemList = SUBSTRING(@PordItemList,
                                  LEN(@PordIAllFields + '|^#^|') + 1,
                                  LEN(@PordItemList))                                  
    END
    ELSE
    BEGIN
        SET @PordIAllFields = @PordItemList
        SET @PordItemList = NULL        
    END
END

IF (@@ERROR > 0)
BEGIN SET @ErrorFrom='Error In Input Preorder Item' SET @ErrorNumber=@@ERROR ROLLBACK TRAN END

/********************************************************************************/
/****************************** PordItemSpcf ************************************/
/********************************************************************************/
BEGIN TRAN
/**/
DECLARE @PordISpcfAllFields NVARCHAR(MAX) = NULL
DECLARE @PordISpcfField NVARCHAR(MAX) = NULL
/**************** Define & set null field **************/
DECLARE 
	@PreOrderItemSpcflID INT = NULL,
	@pordISpcfPreOrderItemId INT = NULL,
	@Specification NVARCHAR(300) = NULL,
	@SpecificationI NVARCHAR(300) = NULL,
	@SpecificationII NVARCHAR(300) = NULL,
	@pordISpcfFirstRegisterUserId INT = NULL,
	@pordISpcfFirstRegisterDatePersian VARCHAR(8) = NULL,
	@pordISpcfFirstRegisterDate DATETIME = NULL,
	@pordISpcfFirstRegisterTime VARCHAR(10) = NULL	
WHILE LEN(@PordItemSpecificationList) > 0 AND (@PordItemSpecificationList IS NOT NULL)
BEGIN
	/*@PordItemSpecificationList = N'PreOrderItemId=1|#|Specification=s1|#||^#^|PreOrderItemId=2|#|Specification=s2|#||^#^|'*/
    IF PATINDEX('%|^#^|%', @PordItemSpecificationList) > 0
    BEGIN
        SET @PordISpcfAllFields = SUBSTRING(@PordItemSpecificationList,
                                    0,
                                    PATINDEX('%|^#^|%', @PordItemSpecificationList))
        /*--------------------------------------------------------------------------------*/
        /*For example => @PordISpcfAllFields = PreOrderItemId=1|#|Specification=s1|#|*/
        /*--------------------------------------------------------------------------------*/
        /*---------------------------- BEGIN Fill fields  --------------------------------*/
        /*BEGIN PORDITEMSPCF*/
        DECLARE @PoISpcfAllFields NVARCHAR(MAX)
        SET @PoISpcfAllFields = @PordISpcfAllFields
        
        /**************** set null field **************/
        SET @PreOrderItemSpcflID = NULL
		SET @pordISpcfPreOrderItemId = NULL
		SET @Specification = NULL
		SET @SpecificationI = NULL
		SET @SpecificationII = NULL
		SET @pordISpcfFirstRegisterUserId = NULL
		SET @pordISpcfFirstRegisterDatePersian = NULL
		SET @pordISpcfFirstRegisterDate = NULL
		SET @pordISpcfFirstRegisterTime = NULL
        /**/
        WHILE LEN(@PoISpcfAllFields) > 0
		BEGIN
			IF PATINDEX('%|#|%', @PoISpcfAllFields) > 0
			BEGIN
				SET @PordISpcfField = SUBSTRING(@PoISpcfAllFields,
											0,
											PATINDEX('%|#|%', @PoISpcfAllFields))
				/*--------------------------------------------------------------------------------*/
				/*Get parameter name and split value form parameter*/
				/*For example => PreOrderItemId=1 => PreOrderItemId= => PreOrderItemId => Compare => value*/
				DECLARE @PordISpcfFieldValue NVARCHAR(MAX)
				DECLARE @PordISpcfFieldName NVARCHAR(200)
				SET @PordISpcfFieldName = SUBSTRING(@PordISpcfField, 1, CHARINDEX('=',@PordISpcfField)-1)                                    
				SET @PordISpcfFieldValue = SUBSTRING(@PordISpcfField, CHARINDEX('=', @PordISpcfField)+1, Len(@PordISpcfField))
				/*--------------------------------------------------------------------------------*/
				/*---------------------------- BEGIN Fill variabele ------------------------------*/
				/*BEGIN PORDITEMSPCF*/
				/*--------------------------*/
				IF @PordISpcfFieldName = 'PreOrderItemSpcflID'
				BEGIN
					SELECT @PreOrderItemSpcflID = CASE WHEN @PordISpcfFieldValue = 'null' THEN NULL ELSE CAST(@PordISpcfFieldValue AS INT) END
					SET @PordItemSpcfIdsInError = @PordItemSpcfIdsInError + CAST(@PreOrderItemSpcflID AS NVARCHAR(10)) + ',0|' /*rollback when have error*/
				END
				/*--------------------------*/
				IF @PordISpcfFieldName = 'pordISpcfPreOrderItemId'
				BEGIN
					SELECT @pordISpcfPreOrderItemId = CASE WHEN @PordISpcfFieldValue = 'null' THEN NULL ELSE CAST(@PordISpcfFieldValue AS INT) END
				END
				/*--------------------------*/
				IF @PordISpcfFieldName = 'Specification'
				BEGIN
					SET @Specification = CASE WHEN @PordISpcfFieldValue = '' OR @PordISpcfFieldValue = 'null' THEN NULL ELSE @PordISpcfFieldValue END
				END
				/*--------------------------*/
				IF @PordISpcfFieldName = 'SpecificationI'
				BEGIN
					SET @SpecificationI = CASE WHEN @PordISpcfFieldValue = '' OR @PordISpcfFieldValue = 'null' THEN NULL ELSE @PordISpcfFieldValue END				
				END
				/*--------------------------*/
				IF @PordISpcfFieldName = 'SpecificationII'
				BEGIN
					SET @SpecificationII = CASE WHEN @PordISpcfFieldValue = '' OR @PordISpcfFieldValue = 'null' THEN NULL ELSE @PordISpcfFieldValue END				
				END
				/*--------------------------*/
				IF @PordISpcfFieldName = 'pordISpcfFirstRegisterUserId'
				BEGIN
					SELECT @pordISpcfFirstRegisterUserId = CASE WHEN @PordISpcfFieldValue = 'null' THEN NULL ELSE CAST(@PordISpcfFieldValue AS INT) END
				END
				/*--------------------------*/
				IF @PordISpcfFieldName = 'pordISpcfFirstRegisterDatePersian'
				BEGIN
					SET @pordISpcfFirstRegisterDatePersian = CASE WHEN @PordISpcfFieldValue = '' OR @PordISpcfFieldValue = 'null' THEN NULL ELSE REPLACE(@PordISpcfFieldValue, '/', '') END
					SET @pordISpcfFirstRegisterDate = CASE WHEN @pordISpcfFirstRegisterDatePersian IS NULL THEN NULL ELSE dbo.fnPersianToGregorianDate(@pordISpcfFirstRegisterDatePersian, @RegisterTime) END			 			
				END
				/*--------------------------*/
				IF @PordISpcfFieldName = 'pordISpcfFirstRegisterTime'
				BEGIN
					SET @pordISpcfFirstRegisterTime = CASE WHEN @PordISpcfFieldValue = '' OR @PordISpcfFieldValue = 'null' THEN NULL ELSE @PordISpcfFieldValue END
				END
				/*--------------------------*/																																
				/*END PORDITEMSPCF*/		
				/*END GENERAL*/
				/*---------------------------- END Fill variabele --------------------------------*/
				/*---------------------------- Remove current parameter from listparam ------------*/
				SET @PoISpcfAllFields = SUBSTRING(@PoISpcfAllFields,
                                  LEN(@PordISpcfField + '|#|') + 1,
                                  LEN(@PoISpcfAllFields))                                                                                                      
			END
			ELSE
			BEGIN
				SET @PordISpcfField = @PoISpcfAllFields
				SET @PoISpcfAllFields = NULL
				
			END
		END  
		/************* Insert to tmp table***************/
		/*set date time*/
		IF(@pordISpcfFirstRegisterTime IS NOT NULL AND @pordISpcfFirstRegisterDatePersian IS NOT NULL)
		BEGIN
			SET @pordISpcfFirstRegisterDate = CASE WHEN @pordISpcfFirstRegisterDatePersian IS NULL THEN NULL ELSE dbo.fnPersianToGregorianDate(@pordISpcfFirstRegisterDatePersian, @pordISpcfFirstRegisterTime) END			 	
		END
		/**/		
		INSERT INTO @PordItemSpcfTmpTable 
			(
				[PreOrderItemSpcflID],
				[pordISpcfPreOrderItemId],
				[Specification],
				[SpecificationI],
				[SpecificationII],
				[pordISpcfFirstRegisterUserId],
				[pordISpcfFirstRegisterDatePersian],
				[pordISpcfFirstRegisterDate],
				[pordISpcfFirstRegisterTime]
			)
			VALUES
			(
				@PreOrderItemSpcflID,
				@pordISpcfPreOrderItemId,
				@Specification,
				@SpecificationI,
				@SpecificationII,
				@pordISpcfFirstRegisterUserId,
				@pordISpcfFirstRegisterDatePersian,
				@pordISpcfFirstRegisterDate,
				@pordISpcfFirstRegisterTime
			) 										                      
		/*END PORDITEMSPCF*/		
        /*END GENERAL*/
        /*---------------------------- END Fill fields ------------------------------------*/
        /*---------------------------- Remove current parameter from listparam ------------*/
        SET @PordItemSpecificationList = SUBSTRING(@PordItemSpecificationList,
                                  LEN(@PordISpcfAllFields + '|^#^|') + 1,
                                  LEN(@PordItemSpecificationList))                                  
    END
    ELSE
    BEGIN
        SET @PordISpcfAllFields = @PordItemSpecificationList
        SET @PordItemSpecificationList = NULL
        
    END
END
/**/
IF (@@ERROR > 0)
BEGIN SET @ErrorFrom='Error In Input PreOrder Item Specification' SET @ErrorNumber=@@ERROR ROLLBACK TRAN END		
ELSE 
COMMIT TRAN

/********************************************************************************/
/****************************** PordPayment *************************************/
/********************************************************************************/
BEGIN TRAN
/**/
DECLARE @PordPayAllFields NVARCHAR(MAX) = NULL
DECLARE @PordPayField NVARCHAR(MAX) = NULL
/**************** Define & set null field **************/
DECLARE 
	@PreOrderPaymentId INT = NULL,
	@pordpayImportDate NVARCHAR(8) = NULL,
	@pordpayImportDateTime DATETIME = NULL,
	@pordpayTypeId INT = NULL,
	@pordpayPreOrderId INT = NULL,
	@pordpayDocId INT = NULL,
	@pordpayDocTypeId INT = NULL,
	@pordpayAmount NUMERIC(18, 2) = NULL,
	@pordpayDocNumber NVARCHAR(50) = NULL,
	@pordpayDocDate NVARCHAR(8) = NULL,
	@pordpayDocDateTime DATETIME = NULL,
	@pordpayDocAmount NUMERIC(18,2) = NULL,
	@pordpayDescription NVARCHAR(2000) = NULL,
	@pordpayFirstRegisterUserId INT = NULL,
	@pordpayFirstRegisteDatePersian NVARCHAR(8) = NULL,
	@pordpayFirstRegisteDate DATETIME = NULL,
	@pordpayFirstRegisterTime VARCHAR(10)  = NULL,
	/*AI14020403 - Add*/
	@pordpayCompanyMoneyBagId INT = NULL,
	@pordpayDeActive BIT = NULL,
	@pordpayAmountTemp NUMERIC(18, 2) = NULL,
	@pordpayFileTypeJson NVARCHAR(MAX) = NULL,
	/*AI14020403*/
	@pordpayAccountNumber NVARCHAR(50) = NULL,
	/*GHO14030210 - Add firld to payment*/
	@pordpayDocDateTemp NVARCHAR(8) = NULL,
	@pordpayDocDateTimeTemp DATETIME = NULL
	/*GHo14030210 - End*/
/**/
WHILE LEN(@PordPaymentList) > 0 AND (@PordPaymentList IS NOT NULL)
BEGIN
	/*@PordPaymentList = N'PreOrderPaymentId=1|#|pordpayTypeId=1|#||^#^|PreOrderPaymentId=2|#|pordpayTypeId=2|#||^#^|'*/
    IF PATINDEX('%|^#^|%', @PordPaymentList) > 0
    BEGIN
        SET @PordPayAllFields = SUBSTRING(@PordPaymentList,
                                    0,
                                    PATINDEX('%|^#^|%', @PordPaymentList))
        /*--------------------------------------------------------------------------------*/
        /*For example => @PordPayAllFields = PreOrderPaymentId=1|#|pordpayTypeId=1|#|*/
        /*--------------------------------------------------------------------------------*/
        /*---------------------------- BEGIN Fill fields  --------------------------------*/
        /*BEGIN PORDPAYMENT*/
        DECLARE @POPayAllFields NVARCHAR(MAX)
        SET @POPayAllFields = @PordPayAllFields
        
        /**************** set null field **************/
        SET @PreOrderPaymentId = NULL
		SET @pordpayImportDate = NULL
		SET @pordpayImportDateTime = NULL
		SET @pordpayTypeId = NULL
		SET @pordpayPreOrderId = NULL
		SET @pordpayDocId = NULL
		SET @pordpayDocTypeId = NULL
		SET @pordpayAmount = NULL
		SET @pordpayDocNumber = NULL
		SET @pordpayDocDate = NULL
		SET @pordpayDocDateTime = NULL
		SET @pordpayDocAmount = NULL
		SET @pordpayDescription = NULL
		SET @pordpayFirstRegisterUserId = NULL
		SET @pordpayFirstRegisteDatePersian = NULL
		SET @pordpayFirstRegisteDate = NULL
		SET @pordpayFirstRegisterTime = NULL
		/*AI14020403 - Add*/
		SET @pordpayCompanyMoneyBagId = NULL
		SET @pordpayDeActive = NULL
		SET @pordpayAmountTemp = NULL
		SET @pordpayFileTypeJson  = NULL
		/*AI14020403*/
		SET @pordpayAccountNumber = NULL /*AI14020701*/
		/*GHO14030210 - Add firld to payment*/
		SET @pordpayDocDate = NULL
		SET @pordpayDocDateTimeTemp = NULL
        /*GHO14030210 - End*/
        WHILE LEN(@POPayAllFields) > 0
		BEGIN
			IF PATINDEX('%|#|%', @POPayAllFields) > 0
			BEGIN
				SET @PordPayField = SUBSTRING(@POPayAllFields,
											0,
											PATINDEX('%|#|%', @POPayAllFields))
				/*--------------------------------------------------------------------------------*/
				/*Get parameter name and split value form parameter*/
				/*For example => PreOrderPaymentId=1 => PreOrderPaymentId= => PreOrderPaymentId => Compare => value*/
				DECLARE @PordPayFieldValue NVARCHAR(MAX)
				DECLARE @PordPayFieldName NVARCHAR(200)
				SET @PordPayFieldName = SUBSTRING(@PordPayField, 1, CHARINDEX('=',@PordPayField)-1)                                    
				SET @PordPayFieldValue = SUBSTRING(@PordPayField, CHARINDEX('=', @PordPayField)+1, Len(@PordPayField))
				/*--------------------------------------------------------------------------------*/
				/*---------------------------- BEGIN Fill variabele ------------------------------*/
				/*BEGIN PORDPAYMENT*/
				/*--------------------------*/
				IF @PordPayFieldName = 'PreOrderPaymentId'
				BEGIN
					SELECT @PreOrderPaymentId = CASE WHEN @PordPayFieldValue = 'null' THEN NULL ELSE CAST(@PordPayFieldValue AS INT) END					
					SET @PordPaymentIdsInError = @PordPaymentIdsInError + CAST(@PreOrderPaymentId AS NVARCHAR(10)) + ',0|' /*rollback when have error*/					
				END
				/*--------------------------*/
				IF @PordPayFieldName = 'pordpayImportDate'
				BEGIN
					SET @pordpayImportDate = CASE WHEN @PordPayFieldValue = '' OR @PordPayFieldValue = 'null' THEN NULL ELSE REPLACE(@PordPayFieldValue, '/', '') END
					SET @pordpayImportDateTime = CASE WHEN @pordpayImportDate IS NULL THEN NULL ELSE dbo.fnPersianToGregorianDate(@pordpayImportDate, @RegisterTime) END			 			
				END
				/*--------------------------*/
				IF @PordPayFieldName = 'pordpayTypeId'
				BEGIN
					SELECT @pordpayTypeId = CASE WHEN @PordPayFieldValue = 'null' THEN NULL ELSE CAST(@PordPayFieldValue AS INT) END
				END
				/*--------------------------*/
				IF @PordPayFieldName = 'pordpayPreOrderId'
				BEGIN
					SELECT @pordpayPreOrderId = CASE WHEN @PordPayFieldValue = 'null' THEN NULL ELSE CAST(@PordPayFieldValue AS INT) END
				END
				/*--------------------------*/
				IF @PordPayFieldName = 'pordpayDocId'
				BEGIN
					SELECT @pordpayDocId = CASE WHEN @PordPayFieldValue = 'null' THEN NULL ELSE CAST(@PordPayFieldValue AS INT) END
				END
				/*--------------------------*/
				IF @PordPayFieldName = 'pordpayDocTypeId'
				BEGIN
					SELECT @pordpayDocTypeId = CASE WHEN @PordPayFieldValue = 'null' THEN NULL ELSE CAST(@PordPayFieldValue AS INT) END
				END
				/*--------------------------*/
				IF @PordPayFieldName = 'pordpayAmount'
				BEGIN
					SELECT @pordpayAmount = CASE WHEN /*AI14020502 @PordPayFieldValue = '0.0' OR*/ @PordPayFieldValue = 'null' THEN NULL ELSE CAST(@PordPayFieldValue AS NUMERIC(18, 2)) END
				END
				/*--------------------------*/
				IF @PordPayFieldName = 'pordpayDocNumber'
				BEGIN
					SET @pordpayDocNumber = CASE WHEN @PordPayFieldValue = '' OR @PordPayFieldValue = 'null' THEN NULL ELSE @PordPayFieldValue END				
				END
				/*--------------------------*/
				IF @PordPayFieldName = 'pordpayDocDate'
				BEGIN
					SET @pordpayDocDate = CASE WHEN @PordPayFieldValue = '' OR @PordPayFieldValue = 'null' THEN NULL ELSE REPLACE(@PordPayFieldValue, '/', '') END
					SET @pordpayDocDateTime = CASE WHEN @pordpayDocDate IS NULL THEN NULL ELSE dbo.fnPersianToGregorianDate(@pordpayDocDate, @RegisterTime) END			 			
				END
				/*--------------------------*/
				IF @PordPayFieldName = 'pordpayDocAmount'
				BEGIN
					SELECT @pordpayDocAmount = CASE WHEN @PordPayFieldValue = '0.0' OR @PordPayFieldValue = 'null' THEN NULL ELSE CAST(@PordPayFieldValue AS NUMERIC(18, 2)) END
				END
				/*--------------------------*/
				IF @PordPayFieldName = 'pordpayDescription'
				BEGIN
					SET @pordpayDescription = CASE WHEN @PordPayFieldValue = '' OR @PordPayFieldValue = 'null' THEN NULL ELSE @PordPayFieldValue END				
				END
				/*--------------------------*/
				IF @PordPayFieldName = 'pordpayFirstRegisterUserId'
				BEGIN
					SELECT @pordpayFirstRegisterUserId = CASE WHEN @PordPayFieldValue = 'null' THEN NULL ELSE CAST(@PordPayFieldValue AS INT) END
				END
				/*--------------------------*/
				IF @PordPayFieldName = 'pordpayFirstRegisterDatePersian'
				BEGIN
					SET @pordpayFirstRegisteDatePersian = CASE WHEN @PordPayFieldValue = '' OR @PordPayFieldValue = 'null' THEN NULL ELSE REPLACE(@PordPayFieldValue, '/', '') END
					SET @pordpayFirstRegisteDate = CASE WHEN @pordpayFirstRegisteDatePersian IS NULL THEN NULL ELSE dbo.fnPersianToGregorianDate(@pordpayFirstRegisteDatePersian, @RegisterTime) END			 			
				END
				/*--------------------------*/
				IF @PordPayFieldName = 'pordpayFirstRegisterTime'
				BEGIN
					SET @pordpayFirstRegisterTime = CASE WHEN @PordPayFieldValue = '' OR @PordPayFieldValue = 'null' THEN NULL ELSE @PordPayFieldValue END				
				END
				/*--------------------------*/	
				/*AI14020403-Add*/
				IF @PordPayFieldName = 'pordpayCompanyMoneyBagId'
				BEGIN
					SELECT @pordpayCompanyMoneyBagId = CASE WHEN @PordPayFieldValue = 'null' THEN NULL ELSE CAST(@PordPayFieldValue AS INT) END
				END
				/*--------------------------*/
				IF @PordPayFieldName = 'pordpayDeActive'
				BEGIN
					SET @pordpayDeActive = CASE WHEN @PordPayFieldValue = 'null' THEN NULL 
										WHEN @PordPayFieldValue = 'false' THEN 0 
										ELSE 1 END	
				END
				/*--------------------------*/
				IF @PordPayFieldName = 'pordpayAmountTemp'
				BEGIN
					SELECT @pordpayAmountTemp = CASE WHEN @PordPayFieldValue = '0.0' OR @PordPayFieldValue = 'null' THEN NULL ELSE CAST(@PordPayFieldValue AS NUMERIC(18, 2)) END
				END
				/*--------------------------*/
				IF @PordPayFieldName = 'pordpayFileTypeJson'
				BEGIN
					SET @pordpayFileTypeJson = CASE WHEN @PordPayFieldValue = '' OR @PordPayFieldValue = 'null' THEN NULL ELSE @PordPayFieldValue END				
				END
				/*AI14020403-End*/
				/*--------------------------*/
				IF @PordPayFieldName = 'pordpayAccountNumber' /*AI14020701*/
				BEGIN
					SET @pordpayAccountNumber = CASE WHEN @PordPayFieldValue = '' OR @PordPayFieldValue = 'null' THEN NULL ELSE @PordPayFieldValue END				
				END
				/*--------------------------*/	
				/*GHO14030210 - Add firld to payment*/
				IF @PordPayFieldName = 'pordpayDocDateTemp'
				BEGIN
					SET @pordpayDocDateTemp = CASE WHEN @PordPayFieldValue = '' OR @PordPayFieldValue = 'null' THEN NULL ELSE REPLACE(@PordPayFieldValue, '/', '') END
					SET @pordpayDocDateTimeTemp = CASE WHEN @pordpayDocDateTemp IS NULL THEN NULL ELSE dbo.fnPersianToGregorianDate(@pordpayDocDateTemp, @RegisterTime) END			 			
				END
				/*GHO14030210 - End*/
				/*--------------------------*/
				/*END PORDPAYMENT*/		
				/*END GENERAL*/
				/*---------------------------- END Fill variabele --------------------------------*/
				/*---------------------------- Remove current parameter from listparam ------------*/
				SET @POPayAllFields = SUBSTRING(@POPayAllFields,
                                  LEN(@PordPayField + '|#|') + 1,
                                  LEN(@POPayAllFields))                                                                                                      
			END
			ELSE
			BEGIN
				SET @PordPayField = @POPayAllFields
				SET @POPayAllFields = NULL
				
			END
		END  
		/************* Insert to tmp table***************/						
		/******************** set date time *********************/
		IF(@pordpayFirstRegisterTime IS NOT NULL )
		BEGIN
			IF(@pordpayImportDate IS NOT NULL)
			BEGIN
				SET @pordpayImportDateTime = CASE WHEN @pordpayImportDate IS NULL THEN NULL ELSE dbo.fnPersianToGregorianDate(@pordpayImportDate, @pordpayFirstRegisterTime) END			 	
			END
			
			IF(@pordpayDocDate IS NOT NULL)
			BEGIN
				SET @pordpayDocDateTime = CASE WHEN @pordpayDocDate IS NULL THEN NULL ELSE dbo.fnPersianToGregorianDate(@pordpayDocDate, @pordpayFirstRegisterTime) END			 	
			END
			/*GHO14030210 - Add firld to payment*/
			IF(@pordpayDocDateTemp IS NOT NULL)
			BEGIN
			SET @pordpayDocDateTimeTemp = CASE WHEN @pordpayDocDateTemp IS NULL THEN NULL ELSE dbo.fnPersianToGregorianDate(@pordpayDocDateTemp, @pordpayFirstRegisterTime) END
			END
			/*GHO14030210 - End*/
			
			IF(@pordpayFirstRegisteDatePersian IS NOT NULL)
			BEGIN
				SET @pordpayFirstRegisteDate = CASE WHEN @pordpayFirstRegisteDatePersian IS NULL THEN NULL ELSE dbo.fnPersianToGregorianDate(@pordpayFirstRegisteDatePersian, @pordpayFirstRegisterTime) END			 	
			END							
		END				
		/**/
		INSERT INTO @PordPaymentTmpTable 
			(
				[PreOrderPaymentId],
				[pordpayImportDate],
				[pordpayImportDateTime],
				[pordpayTypeId],
				[pordpayPreOrderId],
				[pordpayDocId],
				[pordpayDocTypeId],
				[pordpayAmount],
				[pordpayDocNumber],
				[pordpayDocDate],
				[pordpayDocDateTime],
				[pordpayDocAmount],
				[pordpayDescription],
				[pordpayFirstRegisterUserId],
				[pordpayFirstRegisteDatePersian],
				[pordpayFirstRegisteDate],
				[pordpayFirstRegisterTime],
				/*AI14020403 - Add*/
				[pordpayCompanyMoneyBagId],
				[pordpayDeActive],
				[pordpayAmountTemp],
				[pordpayFileTypeJson],
				/*AI14020403*/
				[pordpayAccountNumber], /*AI14020701*/
				/*GHO14030210 - Add firld to payment*/
				[pordpayDocDateTemp],
				[pordpayDocDateTimeTemp]
				/*GHO14030210 - End*/
			)
			VALUES
			(
				@PreOrderPaymentId,
				@pordpayImportDate,
				@pordpayImportDateTime,
				@pordpayTypeId,
				@pordpayPreOrderId,
				@pordpayDocId,
				@pordpayDocTypeId,
				@pordpayAmount,
				@pordpayDocNumber,
				@pordpayDocDate,
				@pordpayDocDateTime,
				@pordpayDocAmount,
				@pordpayDescription,
				@pordpayFirstRegisterUserId,
				@pordpayFirstRegisteDatePersian,
				@pordpayFirstRegisteDate,
				@pordpayFirstRegisterTime,
				/*AI14020403 - Add*/
				@pordpayCompanyMoneyBagId,
				@pordpayDeActive,
				@pordpayAmountTemp,
				@pordpayFileTypeJson,
				/*AI14020403*/
				@pordpayAccountNumber, /*AI14020701*/
				/*GHO14030210 - Add firld to payment*/
				@pordpayDocDateTemp,
				@pordpayDocDateTimeTemp
				/*GHO14030210 - End*/
			) 									                      
		/*END PORDPAYMENT*/		
        /*END GENERAL*/
        /*---------------------------- END Fill fields ------------------------------------*/
        /*---------------------------- Remove current parameter from listparam ------------*/
        SET @PordPaymentList = SUBSTRING(@PordPaymentList,
                                  LEN(@PordPayAllFields + '|^#^|') + 1,
                                  LEN(@PordPaymentList))                                  
    END
    ELSE
    BEGIN
        SET @PordPayAllFields = @PordPaymentList
        SET @PordPaymentList = NULL
        
    END

/*---------------------- PordNumber ---------------------------------*/
DECLARE @InsertPordId INT, @InsertPordIId INT, @InsertPordISecfId INT, @InsertPordPayId INT,

/**/
/** Get Preorder number and check **/
SET @pordNumber = (SELECT [dbo].[MobfnReturnPreOrderNumber](@FPId, @pordTypeId)) /*AI14000204 - Replace NUll with @pordTypeId*/
DECLARE @CheckDupPorderNumber BIGINT
SET @CheckDupPorderNumber = (SELECT TOP 1 pordNumber from dbo.MobfnCheckDuplicatePreOrderNumber(@pordNumber, 0, @pordTypeId)) /*AI14000204 - Replace NUll with @pordTypeId*/
IF(@CheckDupPorderNumber IS NOT NULL)
BEGIN
	SET @ErrorMessage = @ErrorMessage + '|pordNumberIsDup' 	
END
/*---------------------- PordNumRef ---------------------------------*/
IF EXISTS (
SELECT * FROM dbo.CrmsysRollPermission
WHERE FormId = (SELECT ID FROM dbo.CrmsysForms WHERE FormName = N'frmPreOrderDataEntry' )
AND FormActionId = (SELECT Id FROM dbo.CrmsysFormActions WHERE ActionName = N'actAutoCalcNumRef'
					AND FormId = (SELECT ID FROM dbo.CrmsysForms WHERE FormName = N'frmPreOrderDataEntry' ))
AND RollId = (SELECT TOP 1 RollId FROM dbo.UsersPermission WHERE UserId = @UserId AND PrjId = 11/*CRM*/))
BEGIN	
	EXEC dbo.MobspReturnPreOrderNumRef
		@PordCompanyId = @pordCompanyId,
		@PordProjectId = @pordProjectId,
		@PordReferUserId = @pordReferUserId,
		@PordNumber = @pordNumber,
		@PordNumberRevision = 0,
		@PreOrderOprId = @PreOrderOprID,
		@PordTypeId = @pordTypeId,
		@LanguageID = @LanguageID,
		@DefaultLanguageID = @DefaultLanguageID,
		@selectedNumRef = @pordNumRef OUTPUT
END

IF EXISTS (
SELECT * FROM dbo.CrmsysRollPermission
WHERE FormId = (SELECT ID FROM dbo.CrmsysForms WHERE FormName = N'frmPreOrderDataEntry' )
AND FormActionId = (SELECT Id FROM dbo.CrmsysFormActions WHERE ActionName = N'actNotDupNumRef'
					AND FormId = (SELECT ID FROM dbo.CrmsysForms WHERE FormName = N'frmPreOrderDataEntry' ))
AND RollId = (SELECT TOP 1 RollId FROM dbo.UsersPermission WHERE UserId = @UserId AND PrjId = 11/*CRM*/)
AND @pordNumRef IS NOT NULL)
BEGIN

	DECLARE @DupPordNumber INT
	DECLARE @TempTableForPordNumRefCheckDuplicate TABLE (
	[pordNumber] INT,
	[pordNumberRevision] BIGINT,	
	[pordNumRef] NVARCHAR(150))

	INSERT INTO @TempTableForPordNumRefCheckDuplicate 
	EXEC dbo.[MobspCheckDuplicatePreOrderNumRef]
		@PordNumRef = @pordNumRef,
		@CurrPreOrderId = 0

	SELECT TOP 1
		@DupPordNumber = [pordNumber]
	FROM @TempTableForPordNumRefCheckDuplicate	 

	IF(@DupPordNumber IS NOT NULL)
	BEGIN
		SET @ErrorMessage = @ErrorMessage + '@pordNumRef=Dup' +
			',@DupPordNumber=' + ISNULL(CAST(@DupPordNumber AS NVARCHAR(10)),'') + '|^|'	
	END
END

/*check preOrder is inserted*/
IF(@InsertedPreOrderId > 0) 
BEGIN
	IF (@PordItemList IS NOT NULL)
	BEGIN	
		DECLARE CrsrPordI Cursor For

					[PreOrderItemID], [pordIPreOrderId] , [pordIPrdId], [pordIQuantity], [pordIPrice], [pordITotalPrice],

					[pordITaxSum], [pordIPriceSum],  /*AI991119*/

					,[pordIPrdUnitIdI] ,[pordIPrdUnitIRateResult] /*AI14010706*/ ,[pordIPrdNickname] /*AI14020119*/
					/*AI14020710*/ ,[pordIDeliveryTime] ,[pordIDeliveryDay] , [pordIDeliveryEndDate]
					,[pordIDeliveryEndDatePersian] ,[pordIDeliveryEndTime] ,[pordIDeliveryEndDay] ,[pordIValidityExpireDate] ,[pordIValidityExpireDatePersian]
					,[pordIPrdAutoCalcRate] ,[pordIDescriptionI] /*AI14020710-End*/
				FROM @PordItemTmpTable		
		/**/
		OPEN CrsrPordI
		FETCH NEXT FROM CrsrPordI INTO @PreOrderItemID, @pordIPreOrderId , @pordIPrdId, @pordIQuantity, @pordIPrice, @pordITotalPrice,
									   @pordIDescription, @pordIQuantityDesc, @pordIDiscount, @pordIDiscountI, @pordIDiscountPercent,
									   @pordIDiscountIPercent, @pordIPrdUnitId, @pordICurrencyUnitId, @pordIOrderView, @pordIFCR,

									   @pordIFirstRegisterDate, @pordIRoundPlus, @pordIPrdPriceGroupID, @pordITracing, @pordIDiscountSum, @pordIDiscountISum, @pordITaxSum, @pordIPriceSum,	/*AI990210*/ /*AI991119*/
									   @pordIDeliveryTermId, @pordIDeliveryDate, @pordIDeliveryDatePersian, @pordICostSheetItemId, /*AI991226*/
									   @insertLinkedProducts /*AI14000204 - Add*/, @pordIQuantityTotal /*AI14010606*/ ,@pordIPrdColorId /*AI14010614*/
										,@pordIPrdUnitIdI ,@pordIPrdUnitIRateResult /*AI14010706*/ ,@pordIPrdNickname /*AI14020119*/
										/*AI14020710*/ ,@pordIDeliveryTime ,@pordIDeliveryDay , @pordIDeliveryEndDate

										,@pordIValidityExpireDatePersian ,@pordIPrdAutoCalcRate ,@pordIDescriptionI /*AI14020710-End*/

		WHILE @@Fetch_Status = 0
		BEGIN
		/**/
			IF(@pordIPreOrderId = @PreOrderID)
			BEGIN		
				/*------*/
				/********* Check quantity ************/
				IF(@ActCanNotSelectWithNoStock = 1)
				BEGIN
					SELECT 1 /*===> because error in return from sp in visual studio*/
					DECLARE @QuantityRemain FLOAT = 0
					EXEC dbo.MobspReturnPrdQuantity 
					@PrdId = @pordIPrdId, 
					@FPId = @FPId, 
					@WHId = @pordIWHId, 
					@CurrId = 0, 
					@QuantityRemain = @QuantityRemain OUTPUT,
					@LanguageID  = @LanguageID,
					@DefaultLanguageID  = @DefaultLanguageID,
					/*AI14020530*/
					@FCR = @pordIFCR,
					@FCRI = @pordIFCRI,
					@Tracing = @pordITracing
					/*AI14020530-End*/
					IF(@QuantityRemain <= 0 OR @QuantityRemain < @pordIQuantity)
					BEGIN
						SET @ErrorMessage = @ErrorMessage + 'Msg=5<PRDID>' + CAST(@pordIPrdId AS NVARCHAR(10)) + '<\PRDID>' + '<QuantityRemain>' + CAST(@QuantityRemain AS NVARCHAR(20)) +'<\QuantityRemain>'
					END																
				END		

				/******* Insert to main table ********/
				INSERT INTO [dbo].[WSPreOrderItems]
					([PreOrderId]
					,[pordIPrdId]

					,[RegisterDatePersian]

					,[pordIDiscountI]
					,[pordIDiscountPercent]
					,[pordIDiscountIPercent]
					,[pordIPrdUnitId]
					,[pordICurrencyUnitId]
					,[pordIOrderView]
					,[pordIFCR]
					,[pordITax]
					,[pordITaxIndex]
					,[pordICalculateTax]
					,[pordINotVisible]
					,[pordIFCRI]
					,[pordINotInSum]
					,[pordIWHId]
					,[pordIRound]
					,[AppType]
					,[LanguageID]
					,[FirstRegisterUserId]
					,[FirstRegisterDatePersian]
					,[FirstRegisterDate]
					,[pordIRoundPlus]
					,[pordIPrdPriceGroupID]	/*AI990210*/
					,[pordITracing]/*AI991119*/
					,[pordIDiscountSum] /*AI991119*/
					,[pordIDiscountISum] /*AI991119*/
					,[pordITaxSum] /*AI991119*/

					/*AI991226*/
					,[pordIDeliveryTermId]

					,[pordICostSheetItemId]
					/*AI991226 End*/
					,[pordIQuantityTotal] /*AI14010606*/
					,[pordIPrdColorId] /*AI14010614*/
					/*AI14010706*/
					,[pordIPrdUnitIdI]
					,[pordIPrdUnitIRateResult]
					/*AI14010706-End*/
					,[pordIPrdNickname] /*AI14020119*/

					,[pordIDeliveryTime]

					,@pordIRoundPlus

					,@pordITracing /*AI991119*/			   				   				   							         
					,@pordIDiscountSum /*AI991119*/
					,@pordIDiscountISum /*AI991119*/
					,@pordITaxSum /*AI991119*/
					,@pordIPriceSum /*AI991119*/
					/*AI991226*/

					,@pordIDeliveryDate
					,@pordIDeliveryDatePersian
					,@pordICostSheetItemId
					/*AI991226 End*/
					,@pordIQuantityTotal /*AI14010606*/
					,@pordIPrdColorId /*AI14010614*/
					/*AI14010706*/
					,@pordIPrdUnitIdI
					,@pordIPrdUnitIRateResult
					/*AI14010706-End*/

				/******* Insert to lang table ********/
				INSERT INTO [dbo].[WSPreOrderItems_Lang]
					   ([PreOrderItemID]
					   ,[LanguageID]
					   ,[pordIDescription]
					   ,[pordIQuantityDesc])
				SELECT
					@InsertPordIId
				    ,LN.LanguageID
				    ,@pordIDescription
				    ,@pordIQuantityDesc
				FROM dbo.Languages LN 
				WHERE LN.LanguageID <> @DefaultLanguageID	
				AND ISNULL(LN.IsActive,0) =1
								AND ISNULL(LN.IsActive,0) =1					   	
				/******* Insert linked prd ********/
				/*AI14000204 - Add*/
				IF(ISNULL(@insertLinkedProducts,0) = 1)
				BEGIN
					EXEC [dbo].[MobspInsertPreOrderItemFromLinkedPrd] 
					@ProductId = @pordIPrdId,
					@PreOrderId = @InsertPordId,
					@ProductPriceGroupId = @pordIPrdPriceGroupID,

				END									
				/*AI14000204 End*/				   																			
				/*************** Ids inserted ********/				
				SET @InsertedPordIIdsInMobAndDb = @InsertedPordIIdsInMobAndDb + CAST(@PreOrderItemID AS NVARCHAR(10)) + ',' + CAST(@InsertPordIId AS NVARCHAR(10)) + '|'
				SET @InsertedPordItemIds = @InsertedPordIIdsInMobAndDb
				/*------*/
			END
			ELSE /*when preOrderid <> @pordIPreOrderId that come from mobile*/
				/*SET @ErrorMessage = @ErrorMessage + '<PORDIID>' + CAST(@PreOrderItemID AS NVARCHAR(10)) + '<\PORDIID>' + 'Msg=1'*/	
				SET @ErrorMessage = @ErrorMessage + 'Msg=1'					
		/**/					 					
		FETCH NEXT FROM CrsrPordI INTO @PreOrderItemID, @PreOrderId , @pordIPrdId, @pordIQuantity, @pordIPrice, @pordITotalPrice,
									   @pordIDescription, @pordIQuantityDesc, @pordIDiscount, @pordIDiscountI, @pordIDiscountPercent,
									   @pordIDiscountIPercent, @pordIPrdUnitId, @pordICurrencyUnitId, @pordIOrderView, @pordIFCR,

									   @pordIWHId,@pordIRound, @pordIFirstRegisterUserId, @pordIFirstRegisterDatePersian, @pordIFirstRegisterTime,
									   @pordIFirstRegisterDate, @pordIRoundPlus, @pordIPrdPriceGroupID, @pordITracing, @pordIDiscountSum, @pordIDiscountISum, @pordITaxSum, @pordIPriceSum,	/*AI990210*/ /*AI991119*/
									   @pordIDeliveryTermId, @pordIDeliveryDate, @pordIDeliveryDatePersian, @pordICostSheetItemId, /*AI991226*/
									   @insertLinkedProducts /*AI14000204 - Add*/, @pordIQuantityTotal /*AI14010606*/ ,@pordIPrdColorId /*AI14010614*/
										,@pordIPrdUnitIdI ,@pordIPrdUnitIRateResult /*AI14010706*/ ,@pordIPrdNickname /*AI14020119*/
										/*AI14020710*/ ,@pordIDeliveryTime ,@pordIDeliveryDay , @pordIDeliveryEndDate
										,@pordIDeliveryEndDatePersian ,@pordIDeliveryEndTime ,@pordIDeliveryEndDay ,@pordIValidityExpireDate 
										,@pordIValidityExpireDatePersian ,@pordIPrdAutoCalcRate ,@pordIDescriptionI /*AI14020710-End*/


		END
		CLOSE CrsrPordI
		DEALLOCATE CrsrPordI    					 	 	 
	
	END
END
ELSE /*Not insert preOrder*/
	SET @ErrorMessage = @ErrorMessage + '|Msg=0' 	
/**/
IF (@@ERROR > 0)
BEGIN SET @ErrorFrom='Error In Insert PreOrder Item' SET @ErrorNumber=@@ERROR ROLLBACK TRAN END		
ELSE 
COMMIT TRAN 
/**/
/**/
/********************************************************************************/
/****************************** Insert PordItemSpcf *****************************/
/********************************************************************************/
BEGIN TRAN
/**/
/*check preorder is inserted*/
IF(@InsertedPreOrderId > 0)
BEGIN
	DECLARE @PordIdInMob INT , @PordIdInDb INT, @PordIdsStr NVARCHAR(500)	 
	IF(@PordItemSpecificationList IS NOT NULL)
	BEGIN
	/*******************************************************/
	/*check for preOrder item is inserted*/
	IF(@InsertedPordIIdsInMobAndDb IS NOT NULL AND @InsertedPordIIdsInMobAndDb <> '')
	BEGIN
		/*******************************************************/				
			DECLARE CrsrPordISpcf Cursor For
				SELECT 
					[PreOrderItemSpcflID], [pordISpcfPreOrderItemId], [Specification], [SpecificationI], [SpecificationII],
					[pordISpcfFirstRegisterUserId], [pordISpcfFirstRegisterDatePersian], [pordISpcfFirstRegisterDate],
					[pordISpcfFirstRegisterTime]
				FROM @PordItemSpcfTmpTable																				
			/**/
			OPEN CrsrPordISpcf
			FETCH NEXT FROM CrsrPordISpcf INTO @PreOrderItemSpcflID, @pordISpcfPreOrderItemId, @Specification, @SpecificationI, @SpecificationII,
											   @pordISpcfFirstRegisterUserId, @pordISpcfFirstRegisterDatePersian, @pordISpcfFirstRegisterDate,
											   @pordISpcfFirstRegisterTime
			WHILE @@Fetch_Status = 0
			BEGIN
			/**/				
				/******************* check for pordItem insert in db or not **********************/
				SET @PordIdInMob = NULL 
				SET @PordIdInDb = NULL
				SET @PordIdsStr = NULL									
				IF(@InsertedPordIIdsInMobAndDb IS NOT NULL AND @InsertedPordIIdsInMobAndDb <> '')
				BEGIN
					IF CHARINDEX(CAST(@pordISpcfPreOrderItemId AS NVARCHAR(10)) + ',',@InsertedPordIIdsInMobAndDb) > 0
					BEGIN					
						SET @PordIdsStr =   (SUBSTRING(@InsertedPordIIdsInMobAndDb,CHARINDEX(CAST(@pordISpcfPreOrderItemId AS NVARCHAR(10)) + ',',@InsertedPordIIdsInMobAndDb), LEN(@InsertedPordIIdsInMobAndDb)))
						SET @PordIdsStr= (SUBSTRING(@PordIdsStr,0, PATINDEX('%|%', @PordIdsStr)))/*pordiIdMob,pordiIdDb ====> 3,1520*/
						SET @PordIdInMob = CAST((SUBSTRING(@PordIdsStr,0, PATINDEX('%,%', @PordIdsStr))) AS INT)
						SET @PordIdInDb = CAST((SUBSTRING(@PordIdsStr,PATINDEX('%,%', @PordIdsStr) + 1, LEN(@PordIdsStr) )) AS INT)

						IF(@PordIdInMob = @pordISpcfPreOrderItemId)
						BEGIN
							/*------*/
							/******* Insert to main table ********/
							INSERT INTO [dbo].[WSPreOrderItemSpecification]
								   ([PreOrderItemId]
								   ,[Specification]
								   ,[UserId]
								   ,[Month1]
								   ,[FPId]
								   ,[RegisterDate]
								   ,[RegisterDatePersian]
								   ,[SpecificationI]
								   ,[SpecificationII]
								   ,[AppType]
								   ,[LanguageID]
								   ,[FirstRegisterUserId]
								   ,[FirstRegisterDatePersian]
								   ,[FirstRegisterDate])
							 VALUES
								   (@PordIdInDb
								   ,@Specification
								   ,@UserId
								   ,@Month1
								   ,@FPId
								   ,@RegisterDate
								   ,@RegisterDatePersian
								   ,@SpecificationI
								   ,@SpecificationII
								   ,@AppType
								   ,@DefaultLanguageID
								   ,@pordISpcfFirstRegisterUserId
								   ,@RegisterDatePersian /*AI14010621 - @pordISpcfFirstRegisterDatePersian*/
								   ,@RegisterDate /*AI14010621 - @pordISpcfFirstRegisterDate*/
								   )	
								   																											   				   							         
							SET @InsertPordISecfId = SCOPE_IDENTITY()					
							/******* Insert to lang table ********/
							INSERT INTO [dbo].[WSPreOrderItemSpecification_Lang]
								   ([PreOrderItemSpcflID]
								   ,[LanguageID]
								   ,[Specification]

								   ,[SpecificationII])

									,LN.LanguageID
									,@Specification
								    ,@SpecificationI
								    ,@SpecificationII
								FROM dbo.Languages LN 
								WHERE LN.LanguageID <> @DefaultLanguageID	
								AND ISNULL(LN.IsActive,0) =1																													   																			
							/*************** Ids inserted ********/				
							SET @InsertedPordItemSpcfIds = @InsertedPordItemSpcfIds + CAST(@PreOrderItemSpcflID AS NVARCHAR(10)) + ',' + CAST(@InsertPordISecfId AS NVARCHAR(10)) + '|'

							/*------*/																																							
						END
						ELSE
						 /*SET @ErrorMessage = @ErrorMessage + '<PORDISPCFID>' + CAST(@PreOrderItemSpcflID AS NVARCHAR(10)) + '<\PORDISPCFID>' + '<PORDIID>' + CAST(@pordISpcfPreOrderItemId AS NVARCHAR(10)) + '<\PORDIID>' + 'Msg=3'	/*not insert pordItemSpcf */*/
						 SET @ErrorMessage = @ErrorMessage + 'Msg=3'	/*not insert pordItemSpcf */
					END
					ELSE
						/*SET @ErrorMessage = @ErrorMessage + '<PORDISPCFID>' + CAST(@PreOrderItemSpcflID AS NVARCHAR(10)) + '<\PORDISPCFID>' + '<PORDIID>' + CAST(@pordISpcfPreOrderItemId AS NVARCHAR(10)) + '<\PORDIID>' + 'Msg=3'	/*not insert pordItemSpcf */*/
						SET @ErrorMessage = @ErrorMessage + 'Msg=3'	/*not insert pordItemSpcf */																			
				END																       																														
			/**/	
			FETCH NEXT FROM CrsrPordISpcf INTO @PreOrderItemSpcflID, @pordISpcfPreOrderItemId, @Specification, @SpecificationI, @SpecificationII,
											   @pordISpcfFirstRegisterUserId, @pordISpcfFirstRegisterDatePersian, @pordISpcfFirstRegisterDate,
											   @pordISpcfFirstRegisterTime
			END
			CLOSE CrsrPordISpcf
			DEALLOCATE CrsrPordISpcf	
		/*******************************************************/																												
	END
	ELSE /*Not insert PordItem*/
		SET @ErrorMessage = @ErrorMessage + '|Msg=2'	
	/*******************************************************/
	END
END
ELSE /*Not insert preOrder*/
	SET @ErrorMessage = @ErrorMessage + '|Msg=0' 
/**/
IF (@@ERROR > 0)
BEGIN SET @ErrorFrom='Error In Insert PreOrder Item Specification' SET @ErrorNumber=@@ERROR ROLLBACK TRAN END
ELSE 
COMMIT TRAN
/**/
/**/
/********************************************************************************/
/****************************** Insert  PordPayment *****************************/
/********************************************************************************/
BEGIN TRAN
/**/
/*check preOrder is inserted*/
IF(@InsertedPreOrderId > 0)
BEGIN
	IF (@PordPaymentList IS NOT NULL)
	BEGIN
		DECLARE CrsrPordPay Cursor For
		SELECT 
			[PreOrderPaymentId], [pordpayImportDate], [pordpayImportDateTime], [pordpayTypeId], [pordpayPreOrderId],
			[pordpayDocId], [pordpayDocTypeId], [pordpayAmount], [pordpayDocNumber], [pordpayDocDate],
			[pordpayDocDateTime], [pordpayDocAmount], [pordpayDescription], [pordpayFirstRegisterUserId], 
			[pordpayFirstRegisteDatePersian], [pordpayFirstRegisteDate], [pordpayFirstRegisterTime]	,
			/*AI14020403 - Add*/[pordpayCompanyMoneyBagId], [pordpayDeActive], [pordpayAmountTemp], [pordpayFileTypeJson] /*AI1402043*/
			,[pordpayAccountNumber] /*AI14020701*/,[pordpayDocDateTemp] /*GHO14030210*/,[pordpayDocDateTimeTemp]/*GHO14030210*/
		FROM @PordPaymentTmpTable		
		/**/
		OPEN CrsrPordPay
		FETCH NEXT FROM CrsrPordPay INTO @PreOrderPaymentId, @pordpayImportDate, @pordpayImportDateTime, @pordpayTypeId, @pordpayPreOrderId,
										 @pordpayDocId, @pordpayDocTypeId, @pordpayAmount, @pordpayDocNumber, @pordpayDocDate,
										 @pordpayDocDateTime, @pordpayDocAmount, @pordpayDescription, @pordpayFirstRegisterUserId, 
										 @pordpayFirstRegisteDatePersian, @pordpayFirstRegisteDate, @pordpayFirstRegisterTime,
										 /*AI14020403 - Add*/@pordpayCompanyMoneyBagId, @pordpayDeActive, @pordpayAmountTemp, @pordpayFileTypeJson /*AI1402043*/
										 ,@pordpayAccountNumber /*AI14020701*/,@pordpayDocDateTemp/*GHO14030210*/,@pordpayDocDateTimeTemp/*GHO14030210*/
		WHILE @@Fetch_Status = 0
		BEGIN
			/**/
			IF(@pordpayPreOrderId = @PreOrderID)
			BEGIN
				/*----*/
				/******* Insert to main table ********/
				INSERT INTO [dbo].[WSPreOrderPayments]
						([ImportDate]
						,[pordpayTypeId]
						,[pordpayPreOrderId]
						,[pordpayDocId]
						,[pordpayDocTypeId]
						,[pordpayAmount]
						,[pordpayDocNumber]
						,[pordpayDocDate]
						,[pordpayDocAmount]
						,[pordpayDescription]
						,[pordpayFirstRegisterUserId]
						,[pordpayFirstRegisteDatePersian]
						,[pordpayFirstRegisteDate]
						,[UserId]
						,[FPId]
						,[Month1]
						,[RegisterDate]
						,[RegisterDatePersian]
						,[ImportDateTime]
						,[pordpayDocDateTime]
						,[AppType]
						,[LanguageID]
						,[FirstRegisterUserId]
						,[FirstRegisterDatePersian]
						,[FirstRegisterDate]
						/*AI14020403-Add*/
						,[pordpayCompanyMoneyBagId]
						,[pordpayDeActive]
						,[pordpayAmountTemp]
						,[pordpayFileTypeJson]
						/*AI14020403-end*/
						,[pordpayAccountNumber] /*AI14020701*/
						/*GHO14030210 - Add firld to payment*/
						,[pordpayDocDateTemp]
						,[pordpayDocDateTimeTemp]
						/*GHO14030210 - End*/

					   )
				 VALUES
						(@pordpayImportDate
						,@pordpayTypeId
						,@InsertPordId
						,@pordpayDocId
						,@pordpayDocTypeId
						,@pordpayAmount
						,@pordpayDocNumber
						,@pordpayDocDate
						,@pordpayDocAmount
						,@pordpayDescription
						,@pordpayFirstRegisterUserId
						,@RegisterDatePersian /*AI14010621 - @pordpayFirstRegisteDatePersian*/
						,@RegisterDate /*AI14010621 - @pordpayFirstRegisteDate*/
						,@UserId
						,@FPId
						,@Month1
						,@RegisterDate
						,@RegisterDatePersian
						,@pordpayImportDateTime
						,@pordpayDocDateTime
						,@AppType
						,@DefaultLanguageID
						,@pordpayFirstRegisterUserId
						,@RegisterDatePersian /*AI14010621 - @pordpayFirstRegisteDatePersian*/
						,@RegisterDate /*AI14010621 - @pordpayFirstRegisteDatePersian*/
						/*AI14020403-Add*/
						,@pordpayCompanyMoneyBagId
						,@pordpayDeActive
						,@pordpayAmountTemp
						,@pordpayFileTypeJson
						/*AI14020403-end*/
						,@pordpayAccountNumber /*AI14020701*/
						/*GHO14030210 - Add firld to payment*/
						,@pordpayDocDateTemp
						,@pordpayDocDateTimeTemp
						/*GHO14030210 - End*/
						)																																			          													
				SET @InsertPordPayId = SCOPE_IDENTITY()
				/******* Insert to lang table ********/
				INSERT INTO [dbo].[WSPreOrderPayments_Lang]
					   ([PreOrderPaymentId]
					   ,[LanguageID]
					   ,[pordpayDescription])
				SELECT
					   @InsertPordPayId
					   ,LN.LanguageID
					   ,@pordpayDescription
				FROM dbo.Languages LN 
				WHERE LN.LanguageID <> @DefaultLanguageID	
				AND ISNULL(LN.IsActive,0) = 1																			     				
				/*************** Ids inserted ********/								
				SET @InsertedPordPaymentIds = @InsertedPordPaymentIds + CAST(@PreOrderPaymentId AS NVARCHAR(10)) + ',' + CAST(@InsertPordPayId AS NVARCHAR(10)) + '|'				
				/*----*/
			END
			ELSE /*when preorderid <> @pordpayPreOrderId that come from mobile*/
				/*SET @ErrorMessage = @ErrorMessage + '<PORDPAYID>' + CAST(@PreOrderPaymentId AS NVARCHAR(10)) + '<\PORDPAYID>'+ 'Msg=1'	*/	    	
				SET @ErrorMessage = @ErrorMessage + 'Msg=1'			 					
			/**/
		FETCH NEXT FROM CrsrPordPay INTO @PreOrderPaymentId, @pordpayImportDate, @pordpayImportDateTime, @pordpayTypeId, @pordpayPreOrderId,
										 @pordpayDocId, @pordpayDocTypeId, @pordpayAmount, @pordpayDocNumber, @pordpayDocDate,
										 @pordpayDocDateTime, @pordpayDocAmount, @pordpayDescription, @pordpayFirstRegisterUserId, 
										 @pordpayFirstRegisteDatePersian, @pordpayFirstRegisteDate, @pordpayFirstRegisterTime,
										 /*AI14020403 - Add*/@pordpayCompanyMoneyBagId, @pordpayDeActive, @pordpayAmountTemp, @pordpayFileTypeJson /*AI1402043*/
										 ,@pordpayAccountNumber /*AI14020701*/,@pordpayDocDateTemp /*GHO14030210*/ ,@pordpayDocDateTimeTemp /*GHO14030210*/
		END
		CLOSE CrsrPordPay
		DEALLOCATE CrsrPordPay  
	END
END
ELSE /*Not insert preOrder*/
	SET @ErrorMessage = @ErrorMessage + '|Msg=0' 	
/**/
IF (@@ERROR > 0)
BEGIN SET @ErrorFrom='Error In Insert PreOrder Payment' SET @ErrorNumber=@@ERROR ROLLBACK TRAN END		
ELSE 
COMMIT TRAN
/**/
/**/
/********************************************************************************/
/********************************************************************************/
/****************** rollback when have error Start ******************************/
/*AI14000323Added because of Linked Products*/
EXEC [dbo].[MobspCalcAndUpdatePreOrderAmounts] @InsertPordId, @Month1, @LanguageID, @DefaultLanguageID

IF(@ErrorMessage IS NOT NULL AND @ErrorMessage <> '')
BEGIN
	ROLLBACK TRANSACTION Main
 	SELECT 1
	SET @InsertedPreOrderId = @PreOrderIdInError
	SET @InsertedPordItemIds = @PordItemIdsInError
	SET @InsertedPordItemSpcfIds = @PordItemSpcfIdsInError
	SET @InsertedPordPaymentIds = @PordPaymentIdsInError			
END
/****************** rollback when have error End ******************************/
/**/
IF (@ErrorNumber > 0)
BEGIN 
	RAISERROR('Error From TransAction %S', 16, 1, @ErrorFrom)
	ROLLBACK TRANSACTION Main
 	SELECT 0
END 
ELSE
BEGIN
	IF(@ErrorMessage IS NULL OR @ErrorMessage = '')
	BEGIN
	COMMIT TRANSACTION Main 
	SELECT 1
	END
	ELSE
	SELECT 0
END	
/**/
END








-- ==================================================
-- [dbo].[MobspInsertQuickCompany]
-- ==================================================





/*RZ961002*/
/*RZ961004-change |#| to |#|*/
/*RZ961017- convert image*/
/*RZ961027- change search cmpname*/
/*RZ961028- check deactive lang*/
/*RZ961129- Multi language*/
/*RZ961206- Add time to date*/
/*RZ971219- Remove check dublicate in tel and other fields*/
/*RZ971222- Add check access to data permission*/
/*RZ990509- Add cmpKind*/
/*AI14011027 - Convert Arabic characters to Persian*/
CREATE PROCEDURE [dbo].[MobspInsertQuickCompany]
/*
	EXEC MobspInsertQuickCompany  	
*/
(
@Company NVARCHAR(MAX) ,
@UserId INT,
@Year1 SMALLINT,
@Month1 SMALLINT,
@RegisterDate DATETIME,
@RegisterDatePersian NVARCHAR(8),
@AppType VARCHAR(20), 
@LanguageID INT = NULL,
@DefaultLanguageID INT = NULL,
@PersonLabel NVARCHAR(50),
/**/
@InsertedCompanyId INT OUTPUT,
@ErrorMessage NVARCHAR(MAX) OUTPUT
)
AS
BEGIN
/**/
/********************************************************************************/
/****************************** TRANSACTION *************************************/
/********************************************************************************/
BEGIN TRANSACTION Main
/**/
/********************************************************************************/
/***************************** Define fields ************************************/
/********************************************************************************/
DECLARE @IsDuplicate BIT,
/*********** Company ***********/
@CompanyId INT,
@cmpImportDate NVARCHAR(8),
@cmpImportDateTime DATETIME,
/*@cmpNumber INT,*/
@cmpName NVARCHAR(300),
@cmpNameEng NVARCHAR(300),
@cmpTitleId INT,
@cmpFame NVARCHAR(200),
@cmpActivityType NVARCHAR(100),
@cmpGroupId INT,
@cmpSubGroupId INT,
@cmpGroupIdII INT,
@cmpGroupIdIII INT,
@cmpGroupIdIV INT,
@cmpTypeId INT,
@cmpPresentationId INT,
@cmpPreTel NVARCHAR(10),
@cmpTel NVARCHAR(15),
@cmpTelUpTo NVARCHAR(10),
@cmpTelDesc NVARCHAR(50),
@cmpPreTelII NVARCHAR(10),
@cmpTelII NVARCHAR(15),
@cmpTelUpToII NVARCHAR(10),
@cmpTelIIDesc NVARCHAR(50),
@cmpMobile NVARCHAR(15),
@cmpFax NVARCHAR(15),
@cmpFaxDesc NVARCHAR(50),
@cmpCountryId INT,
@cmpProvinceId INT,
@cmpCityId INT,
@cmpAddress NVARCHAR(250),
@cmpAddressDesc NVARCHAR(100),
@cmpZipCode NVARCHAR(50),
@cmpPostBox NVARCHAR(50),
@cmpWebSite NVARCHAR(50),
@cmpEmail NVARCHAR(50),
@cmpInteresting NVARCHAR(500),
@cmpSpecialPoint NVARCHAR(500),
@cmpDescription NVARCHAR(500),
@cmpUserName NVARCHAR(50),
@cmpPassword NVARCHAR(50),
@cmpRegistrationNumber NVARCHAR(50),
@cmpEconomicCode NVARCHAR(50),
@cmpNationalCode NVARCHAR(50),
@cmpBuyLimit NVARCHAR(100),
@cmpAccountNumber NVARCHAR(500),
@cmpAccInquiry NVARCHAR(500),
@cmpFreight NVARCHAR(200),
@cmpInquiry NVARCHAR(500),
@cmpReferUserId INT,
@cmpSpecialWordForCI VARCHAR(300),
@cmpCode NVARCHAR(500),
@cmpEffectiveDatePersian VARCHAR(8),
@cmpEffectiveDateTime DATETIME,
@cmpFirstRegisterUserId INT,
@cmpFirstRegisterDatePersian VARCHAR(8),
@cmpFirstRegisterDate DATETIME,
@cmpCoordinateLatitude NVARCHAR(100),
@cmpCoordinateLongitude NVARCHAR(100),
@cmpCoordinateLastDate DATETIME,
@cmpCoordinateLastDatePersian VARCHAR(8),
@cmpCoordinateZoomLevel INT,
@cmpCoordinateLastUserId INT,
@cmpMultiGroups NVARCHAR(500),
@CompanyLogo VARBINARY(MAX)/*IMAGE*/
/************************************ Current Year ******************************/
DECLARE @CurrYear SMALLINT
SET @CurrYear = CAST(SUBSTRING(@RegisterDatePersian, 1, 4) AS SMALLINT)	
/*RZ961206- Add time to date*/
DECLARE @RegisterTime NVARCHAR(8)
SET @RegisterTime = (CONVERT(CHAR(8), @RegisterDate, 108))					
/************************************ Error *************************************/
DECLARE @ErrorFrom VARCHAR(50), @ErrorNumber INT
/********************************************************************************/
/***************************** Split fields *************************************/
/********************************************************************************/
SET @IsDuplicate  = 0
/********************************************************************************/
/****************************** Company *****************************************/
/********************************************************************************/
BEGIN TRAN
/**/
IF(@Company IS NOT NULL AND @Company <> '')
BEGIN
DECLARE @CmpField NVARCHAR(MAX) = NULL
WHILE LEN(@Company) > 0
BEGIN
    IF PATINDEX('%|#|%', @Company) > 0
    BEGIN
        SET @CmpField = SUBSTRING(@Company,
                                    0,
                                    PATINDEX('%|#|%', @Company))
        /*SELECT @CmpField*/
        /*--------------------------------------------------------------------------------*/
        /*Get parameter name and split value form parameter*/
        /*For example => cmpTypeId=102 => cmpTypeId= => cmpTypeId => Compare => value*/
        DECLARE @CmpFieldValue NVARCHAR(MAX)
        DECLARE @CmpFieldName NVARCHAR(200)
        SET @CmpFieldName = SUBSTRING(@CmpField, 1, CHARINDEX('=',@CmpField)-1)                                    
		SET @CmpFieldValue = SUBSTRING(@CmpField, CHARINDEX('=', @CmpField)+1, Len(@CmpField))
        /*--------------------------------------------------------------------------------*/
        /*---------------------------- BEGIN Fill variabele ------------------------------*/
        /*BEGIN COMPANY*/
        IF @CmpFieldName = 'CompanyID'
        BEGIN
			SELECT @CompanyId = CASE WHEN CAST(@CmpFieldValue AS INT) = -1 THEN NULL ELSE CAST(@CmpFieldValue AS INT) END
        END
        /*--------------------------*/
		IF @CmpFieldName = 'cmpImportDate'
        BEGIN
			SET @cmpImportDate = CASE WHEN @CmpFieldValue = '' THEN NULL ELSE REPLACE(@CmpFieldValue, '/', '') END
			/*RZ961206- Add time to date*/							
			SET @cmpImportDateTime = CASE WHEN @cmpImportDate IS NULL THEN NULL ELSE dbo.fnPersianToGregorianDate(@cmpImportDate, @RegisterTime) END			 	
        END
        /*--------------------------*/
		IF @CmpFieldName = 'cmpName'
        BEGIN
			SET @cmpName = CASE WHEN @CmpFieldValue = '' THEN NULL ELSE @CmpFieldValue END
			SET @cmpName = (SELECT dbo.[fnConvertArabicCharacterToPersian](@cmpName)) /*AI14011027*/
			/*IF(@cmpName IS NOT NULL AND @cmpName <> @PersonLabel)/*check duplicate*/
			BEGIN					
				DECLARE	@ResultCheckDuplicate INT = 0	
				EXEC [dbo].[MobspCheckDuplicateCompany]
						@Type  = 0, 
						@Id = 0, 
						@Val = @cmpName, 
						@TitleID = 0, 
						@CityId = 0, 
						@UserID  = @UserId ,							
						@Result = @ResultCheckDuplicate OUTPUT,
						/*RZ961129- Multi language*/
						@LanguageID = @LanguageID,
						@DefaultLanguageID = @DefaultLanguageID
				IF(@ResultCheckDuplicate > 0)
					SET @IsDuplicate = 1						
			END	*/		
        END
        /*--------------------------*/
		IF @CmpFieldName = 'cmpNameEng'
        BEGIN
			SET @cmpNameEng = CASE WHEN @CmpFieldValue = '' THEN NULL ELSE @CmpFieldValue END
        END
        /*--------------------------*/
		IF @CmpFieldName = 'cmpTitleId'
        BEGIN
			SELECT @cmpTitleId = CASE WHEN CAST(@CmpFieldValue AS INT) = -1 THEN NULL ELSE CAST(@CmpFieldValue AS INT) END
        END
        /*--------------------------*/
		IF @CmpFieldName = 'cmpFame'
        BEGIN
			SET @cmpFame = CASE WHEN @CmpFieldValue = '' THEN NULL ELSE @CmpFieldValue END
        END
        /*--------------------------*/
		IF @CmpFieldName = 'cmpActivityType'
        BEGIN
			SET @cmpActivityType = CASE WHEN @CmpFieldValue = '' THEN NULL ELSE @CmpFieldValue END
        END
        /*--------------------------*/
		IF @CmpFieldName = 'cmpGroupId'
        BEGIN
			SELECT @cmpGroupId = CASE WHEN CAST(@CmpFieldValue AS INT) = -1 THEN NULL ELSE CAST(@CmpFieldValue AS INT) END

			/*RZ971222- Add check access to data permission*/
			IF(@cmpGroupId IS NOT NULL)
			BEGIN
				DECLARE @IsAccessGroupId BIT = 0

				EXEC dbo.MobspCheckCmpDataPermissionForUser 	
				@Mode = 3,
				@Id = @cmpGroupId,
				@CrmUserId = @UserId,
				@IsAccess = @IsAccessGroupId OUTPUT
				
				IF(@IsAccessGroupId = 0)
				BEGIN
					DECLARE @GroupName NVARCHAR(1000)
					SET @GroupName = (SELECT
										CASE  WHEN (@LanguageID IS NULL OR @LanguageID = @DefaultLanguageID)
											THEN (SELECT GroupName FROM dbo.CrmCompanyGroups CG WHERE CG.CompanyGroupID = @cmpGroupId)
											ELSE (SELECT GroupName FROM dbo.CrmCompanyGroups_Lang CG WHERE CG.CompanyGroupID = @cmpGroupId AND CG.LanguageID = @LanguageID)
										END
									 )

					SET @ErrorMessage = @ErrorMessage + '|Msg=CmpGroupIdAccess|' + ISNULL(@GroupName,'') + '|Msg=CmpGroupIdAccess|'
				END	 
			END
			/*RZ971222- END*/
        END
        /*--------------------------*/
		IF @CmpFieldName = 'cmpSubGroupId'
        BEGIN
			SELECT @cmpSubGroupId = CASE WHEN CAST(@CmpFieldValue AS INT) = -1 THEN NULL ELSE CAST(@CmpFieldValue AS INT) END

			/*RZ971222- Add check access to data permission*/
			IF(@cmpSubGroupId IS NOT NULL)
			BEGIN
				DECLARE @IsAccessSubGroupId BIT = 0

				EXEC dbo.MobspCheckCmpDataPermissionForUser 	
				@Mode = 4,

										END
									 )

				END																				
			END
			/*RZ971222- END*/

/*RZ961027- change search cmpname*/
IF(@cmpName IS NOT NULL AND @cmpName <> @PersonLabel)/*check duplicate*/
BEGIN
DECLARE @TitleId INT , @CityId INT
SELECT @TitleId = CASE WHEN @cmpTitleId IS NULL THEN 0 ELSE @cmpTitleId END
SELECT @CityId = CASE WHEN @cmpCityId IS NULL THEN 0 ELSE @cmpCityId END
					
	DECLARE	@ResultCheckDuplicateForCmpName INT = 0	
	EXEC [dbo].[MobspCheckDuplicateCompany]
			@Type  = 1, 
			@Id = 0, 
			@Val = @cmpName, 
			@TitleID = @TitleId, 
			@CityId = @CityId, 
			@UserID  = @UserId ,							
			/*@Result = @ResultCheckDuplicateForCmpName OUTPUT, AI14010529 - Because sp changed*/
			/*RZ961129- Multi language*/
			@LanguageID = @LanguageID,
			@DefaultLanguageID = @DefaultLanguageID

	SET @ResultCheckDuplicateForCmpName = @@ROWCOUNT
	IF(@ResultCheckDuplicateForCmpName > 0)
		SET @IsDuplicate = 1						
END	

END
ELSE
	SET @ErrorMessage = @ErrorMessage + '|Msg=0'

DECLARE @InsertCmpId INT
BEGIN TRAN
/**/
/** Get company number **/
DECLARE @cmpNumber INT 
SET @cmpNumber = (SELECT [dbo].[CrmfnReturnCompanyNumber]())

IF(@IsDuplicate = 1) /* @IsDuplicate = 1 ====> insert to tmp table*/
BEGIN
	SET @ErrorMessage = @ErrorMessage + 'Dup=1Msg=0'				                           
END
ELSE IF(@ErrorMessage IS NULL OR  @ErrorMessage = '')/* @IsDuplicate = 0 ====> insert to main table*/
BEGIN
	/*==================================*/
	/*RZ990509- Add cmpKind*/
	DECLARE @cmpKind INT = 0
	IF((REPLACE(REPLACE(REPLACE(CAST(@cmpName AS NVARCHAR(2000)) , NCHAR(1740), NCHAR(1610)),NCHAR(1603),NCHAR(1705)),NCHAR(1609),NCHAR(1740)) 
	 = REPLACE(REPLACE(REPLACE(CAST(N'شخص' AS NVARCHAR(2000)), NCHAR(1740), NCHAR(1610)),NCHAR(1603),NCHAR(1705)),NCHAR(1609),NCHAR(1740)) ))
	 BEGIN
		SET @cmpKind = 1
	 END
	/*RZ990509- End*/
	/*==================================*/

	INSERT INTO [dbo].[CrmCompany]
           (
            [ImportDate]

           ,[cmpRegistrationNumber]
           ,[cmpEconomicCode]

     VALUES

           ,@cmpAddressDesc

           ,@cmpPostBox

           ,@cmpEmail

           ,@Year1

           ,@RegisterDate

           ,@cmpRegistrationNumber

           ,@cmpNationalCode

		   ,@cmpKind/*RZ990509- Add cmpKind*/

    SET @InsertCmpId = SCOPE_IDENTITY()
	/*********** Insert to lang table ***********/
	INSERT INTO [dbo].[CrmCompany_Lang]
           (
			[CompanyID]
           ,[LanguageID]
           ,[cmpName]
           ,[cmpFame]
           ,[cmpActivityType]
           ,[cmpTelDesc]
           ,[cmpTelIIDesc]
           ,[cmpFaxDesc]

           ,[cmpInteresting]
           ,[cmpSpecialPoint]
           ,[cmpDescription]
           ,[cmpSpecialWordForCI]
           ,[cmpInquiry]
           )
     SELECT
			@InsertCmpId
		   ,LN.LanguageID
		   ,@cmpName
           ,@cmpFame
           ,@cmpActivityType
           ,@cmpTelDesc
           ,@cmpTelIIDesc
           ,@cmpFaxDesc
           ,@cmpAddress
           ,@cmpAddressDesc
           ,@cmpInteresting
           ,@cmpSpecialPoint
           ,@cmpDescription
           ,@cmpSpecialWordForCI
           ,@cmpInquiry
	 FROM dbo.Languages LN 
	 WHERE LN.LanguageID <> @DefaultLanguageID	
	 /*RZ961028- check deactive lang*/
	 AND LN.IsActive =1
	/******* Insert to multi group table ********/
	IF(@cmpMultiGroups IS NOT NULL)
	BEGIN
		DECLARE @GroupId INT
		DECLARE CrsrMultiGroup Cursor For
			SELECT splitdata 
			FROM dbo.fnSplitString(@cmpMultiGroups, '|')		
		/**/
		OPEN CrsrMultiGroup
		FETCH NEXT FROM CrsrMultiGroup INTO @GroupId
		WHILE @@Fetch_Status = 0
		BEGIN
	 		INSERT INTO [dbo].[CrmCompanyMultiGroups]
			   (
				[CompanyID]
			   ,[CompanyGroupsMultiID]
			   ,[UserId]
			   ,[Year1]
			   ,[Month1]
			   ,[RegisterDate]
			   ,[RegisterDatePersian]
			   ,[AppType]
			   ,[LanguageID]
			   ,[FirstRegisterUserId]
			   ,[FirstRegisterDatePersian]
			   ,[FirstRegisterDate]
				)
			VALUES
			   (
				@InsertCmpId
			   ,CAST(@GroupId AS INT)
			   ,@UserId
			   ,@Year1
			   ,@Month1
			   ,@RegisterDate
			   ,@RegisterDatePersian
			   ,@AppType
			   ,@DefaultLanguageID
			   ,@cmpFirstRegisterUserId
			   ,@cmpFirstRegisterDatePersian
			   ,@cmpFirstRegisterDate
			   )			
		FETCH NEXT FROM CrsrMultiGroup INTO @GroupId
		END
		CLOSE CrsrMultiGroup
		DEALLOCATE CrsrMultiGroup
	END		
	/******* Insert to Logo table ***************/	
	IF(@CompanyLogo IS NOT NULL)	
	BEGIN
		INSERT INTO [dbo].[CrmCompanyLoGo]
           ([CompanyId]
           ,[CompanyLogo]
           ,[UserId]
           ,[Year1]
           ,[Month1]
           ,[RegisterDate]
           ,[RegisterDatePersian]
           ,[AppType]
           ,[LanguageID]
           ,[FirstRegisterUserId]
           ,[FirstRegisterDatePersian]
           ,[FirstRegisterDate])
		VALUES
           (@InsertCmpId
           ,@CompanyLogo
           ,@UserId
           ,@Year1
           ,@Month1
           ,@RegisterDate
           ,@RegisterDatePersian
           ,@AppType
           ,@DefaultLanguageID
           ,@cmpFirstRegisterUserId
           ,@cmpFirstRegisterDatePersian
           ,@cmpFirstRegisterDate)	           
	END		 	 	 
END
/**/
IF (@@ERROR > 0)
BEGIN SET @ErrorFrom='Error In Insert Company' SET @ErrorNumber=@@ERROR ROLLBACK TRAN END		
ELSE 
COMMIT TRAN 
/**/
SET @InsertedCompanyId = @InsertCmpId
/**/
/**/
/**/
IF (@ErrorNumber > 0)
BEGIN 
	RAISERROR('Error From TransAction %S', 16, 1, @ErrorFrom)
	ROLLBACK TRANSACTION Main
 	SELECT 0
END 
ELSE
BEGIN
	COMMIT TRANSACTION Main 
	SELECT 1
END	
/**/
END

			SET @cmpIsDraft = CASE WHEN @CmpFieldValue = 'null' OR @CmpFieldValue = 'null' THEN NULL 
								WHEN @CmpFieldValue = 'false' THEN 0 
								ELSE 1 END	
		END
		/*--------------------------*/
		IF @CmpFieldName = 'cmpKind'
		BEGIN
			SELECT @cmpKind = CASE WHEN @CmpFieldValue = 'null' THEN NULL ELSE CAST(@CmpFieldValue AS INT) END
		END
		/*--------------------------*/
		IF @CmpFieldName = 'cmpMainPersonId'
		BEGIN
			SELECT @cmpMainPersonId = CASE WHEN @CmpFieldValue = 'null' THEN NULL ELSE CAST(@CmpFieldValue AS INT) END
		END
		/*--------------------------*/ /*AI14010529*/
		IF @CmpFieldName = 'cmpCampaignIds'
		BEGIN
			SELECT @cmpCampaignIds = CASE WHEN @CmpFieldValue = '' OR @CmpFieldValue = 'null' THEN NULL ELSE @CmpFieldValue END
		END
		/*--------------------------*/ /*AI140240428*/
		IF @CmpFieldName = 'cmpNameOfficial'
		BEGIN
			SELECT @cmpNameOfficial = CASE WHEN @CmpFieldValue = '' OR @CmpFieldValue = 'null' THEN NULL ELSE @CmpFieldValue END
		END
		/*--------------------------*/
		IF @CmpFieldName = 'cmpSuppUserId'
		BEGIN
			SELECT @cmpSuppUserId = CASE WHEN @CmpFieldValue = 'null' THEN NULL ELSE CAST(@CmpFieldValue AS INT) END
		END
		/*--------------------------*/
		IF @CmpFieldName = 'cmpOfficialCityId'
		BEGIN
			SELECT @cmpOfficialCityId = CASE WHEN @CmpFieldValue = 'null' THEN NULL ELSE CAST(@CmpFieldValue AS INT) END
		END
		/*--------------------------*/
		IF @CmpFieldName = 'cmpOfficialAddress'
		BEGIN
			SELECT @cmpOfficialAddress = CASE WHEN @CmpFieldValue = '' OR @CmpFieldValue = 'null' THEN NULL ELSE @CmpFieldValue END
		END
		/*--------------------------*/
		IF @CmpFieldName = 'cmpOfficialZipCode'
		BEGIN
			SELECT @cmpOfficialZipCode = CASE WHEN @CmpFieldValue = '' OR @CmpFieldValue = 'null' THEN NULL ELSE @CmpFieldValue END
		END
		/*--------------------------*/
		IF @CmpFieldName = 'cmpOfficialDesc'
		BEGIN
			SELECT @cmpOfficialDesc = CASE WHEN @CmpFieldValue = '' OR @CmpFieldValue = 'null' THEN NULL ELSE @CmpFieldValue END
		END
		/*--------------------------*/
		IF @CmpFieldName = 'cmpOfficialTel'
		BEGIN
			SELECT @cmpOfficialTel = CASE WHEN @CmpFieldValue = '' OR @CmpFieldValue = 'null' THEN NULL ELSE @CmpFieldValue END
		END
		/*--------------------------*/
		IF @CmpFieldName = 'cmpTspCmpTob'
		BEGIN
			SELECT @cmpTspCmpTob = CASE WHEN @CmpFieldValue = 'null' THEN NULL ELSE CAST(@CmpFieldValue AS INT) END
		END
		/*--------------------------*/
		IF @CmpFieldName = 'cmpTspCmpBbc'
		BEGIN
			SELECT @cmpTspCmpBbc = CASE WHEN @CmpFieldValue = '' OR @CmpFieldValue = 'null' THEN NULL ELSE @CmpFieldValue END
		END
		/*--------------------------*/
		IF @CmpFieldName = 'cmpTspCmpBpn'
		BEGIN
			SELECT @cmpTspCmpBpn = CASE WHEN @CmpFieldValue = '' OR @CmpFieldValue = 'null' THEN NULL ELSE @CmpFieldValue END
		END
		/*--------------------------*/ /*AI14020428*/
        /*END COMPANY*/		
        /*END GENERAL*/
        /*---------------------------- END Fill variabele --------------------------------*/
        /*---------------------------- Remove current parameter from listparam ------------*/
        SET @Company = SUBSTRING(@Company,
                                  LEN(@CmpField + '|#|') + 1,
                                  LEN(@Company))
    END
    ELSE
    BEGIN
        SET @CmpField = @Company
        SET @Company = NULL        
    END
END
/************************** cmpCoordinateLastDate *************************/ /*AI14010621*/
IF((ISNULL(@cmpCoordinateLongitude, '') <> '') AND (ISNULL(@cmpCoordinateLatitude, '') <> ''))
BEGIN
	SET @cmpCoordinateLastDate = @RegisterDate
	SET @cmpCoordinateLastDatePersian = @RegisterDatePersian
END

/**/
IF(@CompanyId IS NOT NULL)
BEGIN
	DECLARE @IsAccessCompanyId BIT = 0

	EXEC dbo.MobspCheckCmpDataPermissionForUser 	
	@Mode = 8,
	@Id = @CompanyId,
	@CrmUserId = @UserId,
	@IsAccess = @IsAccessCompanyId OUTPUT
				
	IF(@IsAccessCompanyId = 0)
	BEGIN
		DECLARE @FoundCmpName NVARCHAR(2000)
		SET @FoundCmpName = (SELECT
							CASE  WHEN (@LanguageID IS NULL OR @LanguageID = @DefaultLanguageID)
								THEN (SELECT  
										ISNULL((SELECT CompanyTitle FROM dbo.CrmCompanyTitle CT WHERE CT.CompanyTitleID = C.cmpTitleId),'') + ' ' +
										ISNULL(cmpName,'')
								FROM dbo.CrmCompany C WHERE C.CompanyID = @CompanyId)
								ELSE (SELECT  
										ISNULL((SELECT CompanyTitle FROM dbo.CrmCompanyTitle CT WHERE CT.CompanyTitleID = C.cmpTitleId AND CT.LanguageID = @LanguageID),'') + ' ' +
										ISNULL((SELECT cmpName FROM dbo.CrmCompany_Lang CL WHERE CL.CompanyID = C.CompanyID AND CL.LanguageID = @LanguageID),'')
										FROM dbo.CrmCompany C WHERE C.CompanyID = @CompanyId)
							END
							)
							
		SET @ErrorMessage = @ErrorMessage + '|Msg=CompanyAccess|' + ISNULL(@FoundCmpName,'') + '|Msg=CompanyAccess|'
	END	 
END
/**/
IF(@cmpGroupId IS NOT NULL)
BEGIN
	DECLARE @IsAccessGroupId BIT = 0

	EXEC dbo.MobspCheckCmpDataPermissionForUser 	
	@Mode = 3,
	@Id = @cmpGroupId,
	@CrmUserId = @UserId,
	@IsAccess = @IsAccessGroupId OUTPUT
				
	IF(@IsAccessGroupId = 0)
	BEGIN
		DECLARE @GroupName NVARCHAR(1000)
		SET @GroupName = (SELECT
							CASE  WHEN (@LanguageID IS NULL OR @LanguageID = @DefaultLanguageID)
								THEN (SELECT GroupName FROM dbo.CrmCompanyGroups CG WHERE CG.CompanyGroupID = @cmpGroupId)
								ELSE (SELECT GroupName FROM dbo.CrmCompanyGroups_Lang CG WHERE CG.CompanyGroupID = @cmpGroupId AND CG.LanguageID = @LanguageID)
							END
							)

		SET @ErrorMessage = @ErrorMessage + '|Msg=CmpGroupIdAccess|' + ISNULL(@GroupName,'') + '|Msg=CmpGroupIdAccess|'
	END	 
END

/************************ Make A Table For check duplicate ***************************/
DECLARE @TmpTableForCheckDuplicate TABLE (
	[CompanyID] INT,	
	[cmpId] INT,	
	[cmpName] NVARCHAR(MAX),
	[Title] NVARCHAR(MAX),	
	[CompanyWithTitle] NVARCHAR(MAX),	
	[cmpTel] NVARCHAR(50),
	[cmpTelII] NVARCHAR(50),	
	[cmpFax] NVARCHAR(50),
	[Country] NVARCHAR(MAX),
	[Provice] NVARCHAR(MAX),
	[City] NVARCHAR(MAX),	
	[cmpAddress] NVARCHAR(MAX),
	[prsName] NVARCHAR(MAX),
	[prsTel] NVARCHAR(50),
	[prsTelII] NVARCHAR(50),
	[prsFax] NVARCHAR(50),
	[prsMobile] NVARCHAR(50),
	[prsEmail] NVARCHAR(300),
	[prsEmailII] NVARCHAR(300),
	[cmpNumber] INT,	
	[ReferUser] NVARCHAR(MAX)			
)
DECLARE @DupCompanyId INT , @DupCmpTitle NVARCHAR(MAX), @DupCmpName NVARCHAR(MAX), @DupCmpCity NVARCHAR(MAX), @DupCmpNumber INT
/******************************* cmpName with city and title *************************/
IF(@ErrorMessage = '' OR @ErrorMessage IS NULL)
BEGIN
	IF(@cmpName IS NOT NULL AND @cmpName <> '' AND @cmpName <> @PersonLabel)
	BEGIN
		INSERT INTO @TmpTableForCheckDuplicate 
		EXEC [dbo].[MobspCheckDuplicateCompanyForSetReason]
				@Type  = 1, 
				@Id = @CompanyId, 
				@Val = @cmpName, 
				@TitleID = @cmpTitleId, 
				@CityId = @cmpCityId, 
				@UserID  = @UserId,
				@LackOfShowText = @LackOfShowText,
				@LanguageID  = @LanguageID,
				@DefaultLanguageID = @DefaultLanguageID

		SELECT TOP 1
			@DupCompanyId = [CompanyID],
			@DupCmpTitle = [Title], 
			@DupCmpName = [cmpName], 
			@DupCmpCity = [City], 
			@DupCmpNumber = [cmpNumber]
		FROM @TmpTableForCheckDuplicate	 
		
		IF(@DupCompanyId IS NOT NULL)
		BEGIN
			SET @ErrorMessage = @ErrorMessage + '@cmpName=Dup,' + '@DupCompanyId='+ ISNULL(CAST(@DupCompanyId AS NVARCHAR(10)),'') + 
			',@DupCmpTitle='+ ISNULL(@DupCmpTitle,'') + ',@DupCmpName=' + ISNULL(@DupCmpName,'') + ',@DupCmpCity=' + ISNULL(@DupCmpCity,'') +
			',@DupCmpNumber=' + ISNULL(CAST(@DupCmpNumber AS NVARCHAR(10)),'') + '|^|'	
		END			
	END
	ELSE IF(@cmpName IS NULL)
		SET @ErrorMessage = @ErrorMessage + '@cmpName=IsNull'+ '|^|'
END			
/**/

/******************************* cmpCode *********************************************/
IF(@cmpCode IS NOT NULL AND @cmpCode <> '')
BEGIN
/**/
	IF EXISTS (
	SELECT * FROM dbo.CrmsysRollPermission
	WHERE FormId = (SELECT ID FROM dbo.CrmsysForms WHERE FormName = N'frmCompanyI' )
	AND FormActionId = (SELECT Id FROM dbo.CrmsysFormActions WHERE ActionName = N'actCmpCodeNotDup'
						AND FormId = (SELECT ID FROM dbo.CrmsysForms WHERE FormName = N'frmCompanyI' ))
	AND RollId = (SELECT TOP 1 RollId FROM dbo.UsersPermission WHERE UserId = @UserId AND PrjId = 11/*CRM*/))
	BEGIN
	/**/
		DECLARE @result NVARCHAR(700) = ''
		EXEC MobspCheckDuplicateCompanyCode
		@cmpCode = @cmpCode, 
		@CurrCmpID = @CompanyId,
		@LanguageID  = @LanguageID,
		@DefaultLanguageID = @DefaultLanguageID ,
		@RecordCount = @result OUTPUT 
					
		IF(@result IS NOT NULL AND @result <> '')
		BEGIN
			SET @ErrorMessage = @ErrorMessage + '@cmpCode=Dup,@cmpName='+ @result + '|^|'	
		END		
	/**/
	END
/**/
END
/******************************* cmpTel *********************************************/
IF(@cmpTel IS NOT NULL AND @cmpTel <> '')
BEGIN
/**/
	DECLARE @HavePermissionDuplicatecmpTel INT
	IF EXISTS (
	SELECT * FROM dbo.CrmsysRollPermission
	WHERE FormId = (SELECT ID FROM dbo.CrmsysForms WHERE FormName = N'frmCompanyI' )
	AND FormActionId = (SELECT Id FROM dbo.CrmsysFormActions WHERE ActionName = N'actCanSaveTelDupAtTimeLimit'
						AND FormId = (SELECT ID FROM dbo.CrmsysForms WHERE FormName = N'frmCompanyI' ))
	AND RollId = (SELECT TOP 1 RollId FROM dbo.UsersPermission WHERE UserId = @UserId AND PrjId = 11/*CRM*/))
	SET @HavePermissionDuplicatecmpTel = 1

	ELSE
	SET @HavePermissionDuplicatecmpTel = 0

	IF(@HavePermissionDuplicatecmpTel = 0 AND @CantSaveDupTel = 1)
	BEGIN
	/**/
		DECLARE @TmpTableForCheckForDuplicate TABLE (
			[CompanyID] INT,	
			[cmpNumber] INT,	
			[CompanyName] NVARCHAR(MAX),
			[CompanyPersonId] INT,	
			[PersonName] NVARCHAR(MAX))

		/*AI14000304 - preTel + Tel for checking duplicate instead of Tel*/
		DECLARE @chckCmpTel NVARCHAR(100)
		SET @chckCmpTel = ISNULL(@cmpPreTel, '') + @cmpTel

		INSERT INTO @TmpTableForCheckForDuplicate 
		EXEC [dbo].[MobspCheckDuplicateTel] /*AI14010329*/
				@Tel  = @chckCmpTel ,
				@Mode = 1, 
				@CurrId = @CompanyId, 
				@LanguageID = @LanguageID,
				@DefaultLanguageID = @DefaultLanguageID

		SELECT TOP 1
			@DupCompanyId = [CompanyID],
			@DupCmpName = [CompanyName], 
			@DupCmpNumber = [cmpNumber]
		FROM @TmpTableForCheckForDuplicate	 
		
		IF(@DupCompanyId IS NOT NULL)
		BEGIN
			SET @ErrorMessage = @ErrorMessage + '@cmpTel=Dup,' + '@DupCmpName=' + ISNULL(@DupCmpName,'') +
			',@DupCmpNumber=' + ISNULL(CAST(@DupCmpNumber AS NVARCHAR(10)),'') + '|^|'	
		END
	/**/				
	END
/**/
END

SET @DupCompanyId = NULL SET @DupCmpTitle = NULL SET @DupCmpName = NULL SET @DupCmpCity = NULL SET @DupCmpNumber = NULL
DELETE FROM @TmpTableForCheckDuplicate
