		BEGIN
			SET @ErrorMessage = @ErrorMessage + '@cmpNationalCode=Dup,' + '@DupCmpName=' + ISNULL(@DupCmpName,'') +
			',@DupCmpNumber=' + ISNULL(CAST(@DupCmpNumber AS NVARCHAR(10)),'') + '|^|'	
		END
	/**/				
	END
/**/
END
/*---------------------- SET NULL ---------------*/
SET @DupCompanyId = NULL SET @DupCmpTitle = NULL SET @DupCmpName = NULL SET @DupCmpCity = NULL SET @DupCmpNumber = NULL
DELETE FROM @TmpTableForCheckDuplicate
DELETE FROM @TmpTableForCheckForDuplicate
DELETE FROM @TmpTableForCheckForDuplicate2
/******************************* cmpEconomicCode *********************************************/
IF(@cmpEconomicCode IS NOT NULL AND @cmpEconomicCode <> '')
BEGIN
/**/
	IF(@CantSaveDupCmpEconomicCode= 1)
	BEGIN
	/**/
		INSERT INTO @TmpTableForCheckForDuplicate2 
		EXEC [dbo].[MobspCheckDuplicateCmpEconomicCode] /*AI14010329*/
				@CmpEconomicCode  = @cmpEconomicCode ,
				@CurrId = @CompanyId, 
				@LanguageID = @LanguageID,
				@DefaultLanguageID = @DefaultLanguageID

		SELECT TOP 1
			@DupCompanyId = [CompanyID],
			@DupCmpName = [CompanyName], 
			@DupCmpNumber = [cmpNumber]
		FROM @TmpTableForCheckForDuplicate2	 
		
		IF(@DupCompanyId IS NOT NULL)
		BEGIN
			SET @ErrorMessage = @ErrorMessage + '@cmpEconomicCode=Dup,' + '@DupCmpName=' + ISNULL(@DupCmpName,'') +
			',@DupCmpNumber=' + ISNULL(CAST(@DupCmpNumber AS NVARCHAR(10)),'') + '|^|'	
		END
	/**/				
	END
/**/
END
/*---------------------- SET NULL ---------------*/
SET @DupCompanyId = NULL SET @DupCmpTitle = NULL SET @DupCmpName = NULL SET @DupCmpCity = NULL SET @DupCmpNumber = NULL
DELETE FROM @TmpTableForCheckDuplicate
DELETE FROM @TmpTableForCheckForDuplicate
DELETE FROM @TmpTableForCheckForDuplicate2
/******************************* cmpEmail *********************************************/
IF(@cmpEmail IS NOT NULL AND @cmpEmail <> '')
BEGIN
/**/
	INSERT INTO @TmpTableForCheckForDuplicate2 
	EXEC [dbo].[MobspCheckDuplicateCmpEmail] /*AI14010329*/
			@CmpEmail  = @cmpEmail ,
			@CurrId = @CompanyId, 
			@LanguageID = @LanguageID,
			@DefaultLanguageID = @DefaultLanguageID

	SELECT TOP 1
		@DupCompanyId = [CompanyID],
		@DupCmpName = [CompanyName], 
		@DupCmpNumber = [cmpNumber]
	FROM @TmpTableForCheckForDuplicate2	 
		
	IF(@DupCompanyId IS NOT NULL)
	BEGIN
		SET @ErrorMessage = @ErrorMessage + '@cmpEmail=Dup,' + '@DupCmpName=' + ISNULL(@DupCmpName,'') +
		',@DupCmpNumber=' + ISNULL(CAST(@DupCmpNumber AS NVARCHAR(10)),'') + '|^|'	
	END
/**/
END
/*---------------------- SET NULL ---------------*/
SET @DupCompanyId = NULL SET @DupCmpTitle = NULL SET @DupCmpName = NULL SET @DupCmpCity = NULL SET @DupCmpNumber = NULL
DELETE FROM @TmpTableForCheckDuplicate
DELETE FROM @TmpTableForCheckForDuplicate
DELETE FROM @TmpTableForCheckForDuplicate2
/******************************* cmpMobile *********************************************/
IF(@cmpMobile IS NOT NULL AND @cmpMobile <> '')
BEGIN
/**/
	DECLARE @TmpTableForCheckCmpMobile TABLE (
	[CompanyID] INT,	
	[cmpNumber] INT,	
	[CompanyName] NVARCHAR(MAX),
	[cmpMainPersonId] INT
	)

	INSERT INTO @TmpTableForCheckCmpMobile 
	EXEC [dbo].[MobspCheckDuplicateCmpMobile] /*AI14010329*/
			@CmpMobile  = @cmpMobile ,
			@CurrId = @CompanyId, 
			@LanguageID = @LanguageID,
			@DefaultLanguageID = @DefaultLanguageID

	SELECT TOP 1
		@DupCompanyId = [CompanyID],
		@DupCmpName = [CompanyName], 
		@DupCmpNumber = [cmpNumber]
	FROM @TmpTableForCheckCmpMobile	 
		
	IF(@DupCompanyId IS NOT NULL)
	BEGIN
		SET @ErrorMessage = @ErrorMessage + '@cmpMobile=Dup,' + '@DupCmpName=' + ISNULL(@DupCmpName,'') +
		',@DupCmpNumber=' + ISNULL(CAST(@DupCmpNumber AS NVARCHAR(10)),'') + '|^|'	
	END
/**/
END
/*AI990719 - until here*/
/*---------------------- SET NULL ---------------*/
SET @DupCompanyId = NULL SET @DupCmpTitle = NULL SET @DupCmpName = NULL SET @DupCmpCity = NULL SET @DupCmpNumber = NULL
DELETE FROM @TmpTableForCheckDuplicate
DELETE FROM @TmpTableForCheckForDuplicate
DELETE FROM @TmpTableForCheckForDuplicate2
DELETE FROM @TmpTableForCheckCmpMobile
/*==============================================================================*/
IF(@ErrorMessage = '' OR @ErrorMessage IS NULL) /*Is not Duplicate =====> update*/
BEGIN
	UPDATE [dbo].[CrmCompany] 
		SET 
		 [cmpName] = @cmpName
		,[cmpNameEng] = @cmpNameEng
		,[cmpTitleId] = @cmpTitleId
		,[cmpFame] = @cmpFame
		,[cmpActivityType] = @cmpActivityType
		,[cmpGroupId] = @cmpGroupId
		,[cmpSubGroupId] = @cmpSubGroupId
		,[cmpGroupIdII] = @cmpGroupIdII
		,[cmpGroupIdIII] = @cmpGroupIdIII
		,[cmpGroupIdIV] = @cmpGroupIdIV
		,[cmpTypeId] = @cmpTypeId
		,[cmpPresentationId] = @cmpPresentationId
		,[cmpPreTel] = @cmpPreTel
		,[cmpTel] = @cmpTel
		,[cmpTelUpTo] = @cmpTelUpTo
		,[cmpTelDesc] = @cmpTelDesc
		,[cmpPreTelII] = @cmpPreTelII
		,[cmpTelII] = @cmpTelII
		,[cmpTelUpToII] = @cmpTelUpToII
		,[cmpTelIIDesc] = @cmpTelIIDesc
		,[cmpMobile] = @cmpMobile
		,[cmpFax] = @cmpFax
		,[cmpFaxDesc] = @cmpFaxDesc
		,[cmpCountryId] = @cmpCountryId
		,[cmpProvinceId] = @cmpProvinceId
		,[cmpCityId] = @cmpCityId
		,[cmpAddress] = @cmpAddress
		,[cmpAddressDesc] = @cmpAddressDesc
		,[cmpZipCode] = @cmpZipCode
		,[cmpPostBox] = @cmpPostBox
		,[cmpWebSite] = @cmpWebSite
		,[cmpEmail] = @cmpEmail
		,[cmpInteresting] = @cmpInteresting
		,[cmpSpecialPoint] = @cmpSpecialPoint
		,[cmpDescription] = @cmpDescription
		/*,[cmpUserName] = @cmpUserName
		,[cmpPassword] = @cmpPassword AI14020220*/
		,[UserId] = @UserId
		,[Year1] = @Year1
		,[Month1] = @Month1
		,[RegisterDate] = @RegisterDate
		,[RegisterDatePersian] = @RegisterDatePersian		
		,[cmpRegistrationNumber] = @cmpRegistrationNumber
		,[cmpEconomicCode] = @cmpEconomicCode
		,[cmpNationalCode] = @cmpNationalCode      
		,[cmpBuyLimit] = @cmpBuyLimit
		,[cmpAccountNumber] = @cmpAccountNumber
		,[cmpAccInquiry] = @cmpAccInquiry
		,[cmpFreight] = @cmpFreight
		,[cmpInquiry] = @cmpInquiry
		,[cmpReferUserId] = @cmpReferUserId
		,[AppType] = @AppType
		,[cmpSpecialWordForCI] = @cmpSpecialWordForCI
		,[cmpCode] = @cmpCode
		,[cmpEffectiveDatePersian] = @cmpEffectiveDatePersian
		,[cmpEffectiveDateTime] = @cmpEffectiveDateTime	      
		,[cmpCoordinateLatitude] = @cmpCoordinateLatitude
		,[cmpCoordinateLongitude] = @cmpCoordinateLongitude
		,[cmpCoordinateLastDate] = @cmpCoordinateLastDate /*AI14010621 - @cmpCoordinateLastDate*/
		,[cmpCoordinateLastDatePersian] = @cmpCoordinateLastDatePersian /*AI14010621 - @cmpCoordinateLastDatePersian*/
		,[cmpCoordinateZoomLevel] = @cmpCoordinateZoomLevel
		,[cmpCoordinateLastUserId] = @cmpCoordinateLastUserId
		,[cmpIsDraft] = @cmpIsDraft /*AI990827*/
		,[cmpKind] = @cmpKind /*AI990827*/
		,[cmpMainPersonId] = @cmpMainPersonId /*AI990827*/
		,[cmpCampaignIds] = @cmpCampaignIds /*AI14010529*/
		/*AI14020428*/
		,[cmpNameOfficial] = @cmpNameOfficial
		,[cmpSuppUserId] = @cmpSuppUserId
		,[cmpOfficialCityId] = @cmpOfficialCityId
		,[cmpOfficialAddress] = @cmpOfficialAddress
		,[cmpOfficialZipCode] = @cmpOfficialZipCode
		,[cmpOfficialDesc] = @cmpOfficialDesc
		,[cmpOfficialTel] = @cmpOfficialTel
		,[TspCmpTob] = @cmpTspCmpTob
		,[TspCmpBbc] = @cmpTspCmpBbc
		,[TspCmpBpn] = @cmpTspCmpBpn
		/*AI14020428-End*/
	WHERE CompanyID = @CompanyId
	/*********** Update to lang table ***********/
	UPDATE [dbo].[CrmCompany_Lang]
	   SET 
		   [cmpName] = @cmpName
		  ,[cmpFame] = @cmpFame
		  ,[cmpActivityType] = @cmpActivityType
		  ,[cmpTelDesc] = @cmpTelDesc
		  ,[cmpTelIIDesc] = @cmpTelIIDesc
		  ,[cmpFaxDesc] = @cmpFaxDesc
		  ,[cmpAddress] = @cmpAddress
		  ,[cmpAddressDesc] = @cmpAddressDesc
		  ,[cmpInteresting] = @cmpInteresting
		  ,[cmpSpecialPoint] = @cmpSpecialPoint
		  ,[cmpDescription] = @cmpDescription
		  ,[cmpSpecialWordForCI] = @cmpSpecialWordForCI
		  ,[cmpInquiry] = @cmpInquiry
	 WHERE CompanyID = @CompanyId
	 AND [LanguageID] IN (SELECT LN.LanguageID FROM dbo.Languages LN 
							WHERE LN.LanguageID <> @DefaultLanguageID
							AND LN.IsActive = 1)		
	/******* Insert to multi group table ********/
	IF(@cmpMultiGroups IS NOT NULL)
	BEGIN
		/************ For add group *************/
		DECLARE @GroupId INT
		DECLARE CrsrMultiGroup CURSOR FOR
			SELECT splitdata 
			FROM dbo.fnSplitString(@cmpMultiGroups, '|')		
		/**/
		OPEN CrsrMultiGroup
		FETCH NEXT FROM CrsrMultiGroup INTO @GroupId
		WHILE @@Fetch_Status = 0
		BEGIN
			/***** Find group multi for the company  *****/
			DECLARE @CompanyGroupsMultiId INT
			SET @CompanyGroupsMultiId = (SELECT TOP 1 CompanyGroupsMultiID FROM dbo.CrmCompanyMultiGroups
											WHERE CompanyID = @CompanyId
											AND CompanyGroupsMultiID = CAST(@GroupId AS INT))
			/*IF multi group is not then add*/
			IF(@CompanyGroupsMultiId IS NULL)
			BEGIN
				INSERT INTO [dbo].[CrmCompanyMultiGroups]
				   (
					[CompanyID]
				   ,[CompanyGroupsMultiID]
				   ,[UserId]
				   ,[Year1]
				   ,[Month1]
				   ,[RegisterDate]
				   ,[RegisterDatePersian]
				   ,[AppType]
				   ,[LanguageID]
				   ,[FirstRegisterUserId]
				   ,[FirstRegisterDatePersian]
				   ,[FirstRegisterDate]
					)
				VALUES
				   (
					@CompanyId
				   ,CAST(@GroupId AS INT)
				   ,@UserId
				   ,@Year1
				   ,@Month1
				   ,@RegisterDate
				   ,@RegisterDatePersian
				   ,@AppType
				   ,@DefaultLanguageID
				   ,@UserId
				   ,@RegisterDatePersian
				   ,@RegisterDate
				   )
			END																							 					
		FETCH NEXT FROM CrsrMultiGroup INTO @GroupId
		END
		CLOSE CrsrMultiGroup
		DEALLOCATE CrsrMultiGroup
		/************ For delete group *************/
		DELETE FROM [dbo].[CrmCompanyMultiGroups]
					WHERE CompanyID = @CompanyId
					AND CompanyGroupsMultiID NOT IN(SELECT CAST(splitdata AS INT) 
														FROM dbo.fnSplitString(@cmpMultiGroups, '|'))				
	END				 	 	 
END
/**/
IF (@@ERROR > 0)
BEGIN SET @ErrorFrom='Error In Update Company' SET @ErrorNumber=@@ERROR ROLLBACK TRAN END		
ELSE 
COMMIT TRAN 
/**/
SET @UpdatedCompanyId = @CompanyId
/**/
IF (@ErrorNumber > 0)
BEGIN 
	RAISERROR('Error From TransAction %S', 16, 1, @ErrorFrom)
	ROLLBACK TRANSACTION Main
 	SELECT 0
END 
ELSE
BEGIN
	COMMIT TRANSACTION Main 
	SELECT 1
END	

IF (@ErrorNumber > 0)
BEGIN 
	RAISERROR('Error From TransAction %S', 16, 1, @ErrorFrom)
	ROLLBACK TRANSACTION Main
 	SELECT 0
END 
ELSE
BEGIN
	COMMIT TRANSACTION Main 
	SELECT 1
END	
/**/
END








-- ==================================================
-- [dbo].[WSfnProductsInGroups]
-- ==================================================




/*--ADBF9193049404*/
/*RZ980308- convert star to fields*/
Create Function [dbo].[WSfnProductsInGroups]()
RETURNS TABLE	-- Select * from WSfnProductsInGroups()
As
Return 		
(

SELECT DISTINCT F.PrdFirstGroupID FGId, S.PrdSecondGroupID SGId, Isnull(F.FGName,'') + ' - ' + Isnull(S.SGName, '') GName,
F.FGName + ' - ' + S.SGName + ' - ' + P.PrdName PGName, 
F.FGCode , F.FGNAME , S.SGCode , S.SGName, P.PrdName PName, 
/*P.*,*/
/*RZ980308- convert star to fields*/
P.[ProductID]
      ,P.[PrdGroupID]
      ,P.[PrdCode]
      ,P.[PrdName]
      ,P.[PrdLatinName]
      ,P.[PrdType]
      ,P.[PrdDesign]
      ,P.[PrdSize]
      ,P.[PrdColorId]
      ,P.[PrdUnitId]
      ,P.[PrdMinQty]
      ,P.[PrdMaxQty]
      ,P.[PrdSleepDay]
      ,P.[PrdFirstSerial]
      ,P.[PrdSecondSerial]
      ,P.[PrdThirdSerial]
      ,P.[PrdFirstBarcode]
      ,P.[PrdSecondBarcode]
      ,P.[PrdThirdBarcode]
      ,P.[PrdDescription]
      ,P.[UserId]
      ,P.[Month1]
      ,P.[FPId]
      ,P.[RegisterDate]
      ,P.[RegisterDatePersian]
      ,P.[PrdBarCode]
      ,P.[PrdBarCodeTypeId]
      ,P.[PrdAutoNumBarcode]
      ,P.[PrdAutoNumBarCodeTypeId]
      ,P.[PrdAutoCharBarcode]
      ,P.[prdAutoCharBarCodeTypeId]
      ,P.[PrdModel]
      ,P.[GUId]
      ,P.[PrdInactiveInSale]
      ,P.[PrdInactiveInBuy]
      ,P.[AppType]
      ,P.[LanguageID]
      ,P.[FirstRegisterUserId]
      ,P.[FirstRegisterDatePersian]
      ,P.[FirstRegisterDate],
/*RZ980308- End*/
(Select Color From WSProductColor c Where c.ProductColorId = P.PrdColorID) Color,
(Select Unit From WSProductUnits u Where u.ProductUnitId = P.PrdUnitId) [Unit]
FROM dbo.WSProductFirstGroup F INNER JOIN dbo.WSProductSecondGroup S
					ON(F.PrdFirstGroupID = S.FirstGroupId)
				INNER JOIN dbo.WSProducts P
					ON(S.PrdSecondGroupId = P.PrdGroupId)
)




-- ==================================================
-- [dbo].[WSfnProductsInGroups_Lang]
-- ==================================================


/*RZ960231 For multi language*/
Create Function [dbo].[WSfnProductsInGroups_Lang]
(
/*
	Select * from dbo.WSfnProductsInGroups_Lang(1033, 1065)
*/
@LanguageID INT = NULL,
@DefaultLanguageID INT = NULL
)
RETURNS TABLE	
As
Return 		
(
		SELECT DISTINCT 
			F.PrdFirstGroupID FGId, 
			S.PrdSecondGroupID SGId, 
				ISNULL((SELECT FGName FROM dbo.WSProductFirstGroup_Lang FGLN WHERE FGLN.PrdFirstGroupID = F.PrdFirstGroupID AND FGLN.LanguageID = @LanguageID), '')
				+ ' - ' + 
				ISNULL((SELECT SGName FROM dbo.WSProductSecondGroup_Lang SGLN WHERE SGLN.PrdSecondGroupID = S.PrdSecondGroupID AND SGLN.LanguageID = @LanguageID), '') 
			GName,
				(SELECT FGName FROM dbo.WSProductFirstGroup_Lang FGLN WHERE FGLN.PrdFirstGroupID = F.PrdFirstGroupID AND FGLN.LanguageID = @LanguageID) 
				+ ' - ' + 
				(SELECT SGName FROM dbo.WSProductSecondGroup_Lang SGLN WHERE SGLN.PrdSecondGroupID = S.PrdSecondGroupID AND SGLN.LanguageID = @LanguageID) 
				+ ' - ' + 
				(SELECT PrdName FROM dbo.WSProducts_Lang PLN WHERE PLN.ProductID = P.ProductID AND PLN.LanguageID = @LanguageID)
			PGName, 
			F.FGCode , 
			(SELECT FGName FROM dbo.WSProductFirstGroup_Lang FGLN WHERE FGLN.PrdFirstGroupID = F.PrdFirstGroupID AND FGLN.LanguageID = @LanguageID) FGNAME,
			S.SGCode , 
			(SELECT SGName FROM dbo.WSProductSecondGroup_Lang SGLN WHERE SGLN.PrdSecondGroupID = S.PrdSecondGroupID AND SGLN.LanguageID = @LanguageID) SGName, 
			(SELECT PrdName FROM dbo.WSProducts_Lang PLN WHERE PLN.ProductID = P.ProductID AND PLN.LanguageID = @LanguageID) PName, 
			P.[ProductID],
			P.[PrdGroupID],
			P.[PrdCode],
			(SELECT [PrdName] FROM dbo.WSProducts_Lang PLN WHERE PLN.ProductID = P.ProductID AND PLN.LanguageID = @LanguageID) [PrdName],
			(SELECT [PrdLatinName] FROM dbo.WSProducts_Lang PLN WHERE PLN.ProductID = P.ProductID AND PLN.LanguageID = @LanguageID) [PrdLatinName],
			(SELECT [PrdType] FROM dbo.WSProducts_Lang PLN WHERE PLN.ProductID = P.ProductID AND PLN.LanguageID = @LanguageID) [PrdType],
			(SELECT [PrdDesign] FROM dbo.WSProducts_Lang PLN WHERE PLN.ProductID = P.ProductID AND PLN.LanguageID = @LanguageID) [PrdDesign],
			(SELECT [PrdSize] FROM dbo.WSProducts_Lang PLN WHERE PLN.ProductID = P.ProductID AND PLN.LanguageID = @LanguageID) [PrdSize],
			P.[PrdColorId],
			P.[PrdUnitId],
			P.[PrdMinQty],
			P.[PrdMaxQty],
			P.[PrdSleepDay],
			P.[PrdFirstSerial],
			P.[PrdSecondSerial],
			P.[PrdThirdSerial],
			P.[PrdFirstBarcode],
			P.[PrdSecondBarcode],
			P.[PrdThirdBarcode],
			(SELECT [PrdDescription] FROM dbo.WSProducts_Lang PLN WHERE PLN.ProductID = P.ProductID AND PLN.LanguageID = @LanguageID) [PrdDescription],
			P.[UserId],
			P.[Month1],
			P.[FPId],
			P.[RegisterDate],
			P.[RegisterDatePersian],
			P.[PrdBarCode],
			P.[PrdBarCodeTypeId],
			P.[PrdAutoNumBarcode],
			P.[PrdAutoNumBarCodeTypeId],
			P.[PrdAutoCharBarcode],
			P.[prdAutoCharBarCodeTypeId],
			(SELECT [PrdModel] FROM dbo.WSProducts_Lang PLN WHERE PLN.ProductID = P.ProductID AND PLN.LanguageID = @LanguageID) [PrdModel],
			P.[GUId],
			P.[PrdInactiveInSale],
			P.[PrdInactiveInBuy],
			P.[AppType],
			P.[LanguageID],
			P.[FirstRegisterUserId],
			P.[FirstRegisterDatePersian],
			P.[FirstRegisterDate],
			(SELECT Color FROM WSProductColor_Lang c WHERE c.ProductColorId = P.PrdColorID AND c.LanguageID = @LanguageID) Color,
			(SELECT Unit FROM WSProductUnits_Lang u WHERE u.ProductUnitId = P.PrdUnitId AND u.LanguageID = @LanguageID) [Unit]
		FROM dbo.WSProductFirstGroup F 
		INNER JOIN dbo.WSProductSecondGroup S ON(F.PrdFirstGroupID = S.FirstGroupId)
		INNER JOIN dbo.WSProducts P ON(S.PrdSecondGroupId = P.PrdGroupId)
)
